yādavaprakāśaviracitaḥ
yatidharmasamuccayaḥ
prathamaṃ parva
pārivrājyavidhiḥ
oṃkāro vācako yasya yogakāle śruto hareḥ |
taṃ śrutiśreṇiniḥśreṇiśrayaṇīyaṃ śrayāmahe || 1.1 ||
śrīmāñ jayati lokānāṃ nāyako garuḍadhvajaḥ |
pāṣaṇḍebhyas trayī trātā yena prāg yatiliṅginā ||1.2 ||
saṃnyāsaviṣayāḥ sarvāḥ samāhṛtya śrutismṛtīḥ |
kriyate yādavenāyaṃ yatidharmasamuccayaḥ || 1.3 ||
vidhiḥ kartṛdaśā liṅgaṃ prayogo mukhyakarma ca |
ahorātrakriyācāro liṅgadharmā gatisthitiḥ || 1.4 ||
prāyaścittāni saṃskāra ity ekādaśaparvakaḥ |
yatidharmaḥ kramād atra dharmaśāstrebhya uddhṛtaḥ ||1.5 ||
vaktāro dharmaśāstrāṇāṃ manur viṣṇur yamo ʼṅgirāḥ |
vasiṣṭhadakṣasaṃvartaśātātapaparāśarāḥ || 1.6 ||
kātyāyanośanovyāsā āpastambo bṛhaspatiḥ |
hārītaśaṅkhalikhitayājñavalkyātrigautamāḥ || 1.7 ||
bodhāyano vṛddhadakṣaḥ kāśyapaḥ kapilaḥ kratuḥ |
śāṇḍilyo vṛddhajābālo dattātreyaś ca devalaḥ || 1.8 ||
gārgyo vṛddhavasiṣṭhaś ca viśvāmitraś ca gālavaḥ |
medhātithir bharadvājo jamadagniś ca śaunakaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ satyakāmo vāyuś cetyevamādayaḥ || 1.9 ||
eṣāṃ śāstreṣu saṃnyāsapradeśeṣv eva saṃcitaḥ |
dharmo ʼyaṃ nānyadeśeṣu netihāsapurāṇayoḥ || 1.10 ||
tatra saṃnyāsavidhir asti vā na veti nirūpyate ||1.11 || tatra kecid āhuḥ | śrutāv adṛṣṭatvāt śrutivirodhe smṛtīnām aprāmāṇyāt nasty eveti || 1.12 || tathā ca gautamaḥ:
aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ pratyakṣavidhānād gārhasthyasyeti [GDh 3.36] || 1.13 ||
āha bodhāyanaḥ |
tasya ha vā etasya caturdhā vibhedam eka āhuḥ | adṛṣṭārtham eva catvāra iti karmavāda aiṣṭikasaumikapāśukadārvikahaumikānām | tad eṣā bhavanti | brahmacārī gṛhastho vānaprasthaḥ parivrājaka iti | brahmacārī guruśuśrūṣurāmaraṇāt | vānaprastho vaikhānasaśāstrasamudācāreṇa vane mūlaphalāśī tapaḥśīlaḥ śrāmaṇakenāgniṃ samādhāya savaneṣūdakam upaspṛśet | agrāmyabhojī bhaikṣam apy upayuñjīta | grāmaṃ ca na praviśet | jaṭilaś cīrājinavāsā nātisaṃvatsaraṃ bhuñjīta | parivrājakaḥ parityajya bandhūn aparigrahaḥ parivrajet | araṇyaṃ gatvā muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā kaupīnācchādano varṣāsv ekatrasthaḥ | kāṣāyavāsāḥ sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktajane nivṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣeta | vāṅmanaḥkāyadaṇḍair bhūtānām adrohī | pavitrabhṛc chaucārtham uddhṛtābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kuryāt | apavidhya daivikāgnikarmāṇy ubhayataḥ paricchinnā madhyamaṃ saṃśliṣyāmaha iti bruvantaḥ | aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryā aprajananatvād itareṣām | tatrodāharanti |
prāhlādir vai kāpilo nāmāsurir āsa | sa etān bhedāṃś cakāra devaiḥ spardhamānaḥ | tasmān manīṣī nādriyeteti |
dṛṣṭārthatvāt | tad eṣāpy anūdyate |
eṣa nityo mahimeti | [TB 3.12.9.7–8]
sa brūyāt |
yena sūryas tapati . . . sāṃparāya iti [TB 3.12.9.7] |
ime ye nārvāṅ na paraś caranti na brāhmaṇāso na sutekarāsaḥ |
ta ete vācam abhipadya pāpayā sirīstantraṃ tanvate aprajajñaya iti [RV 10.71.9] || prajābhir agne amṛtatvam aśyām [RV 5.4.10] |
jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇas tribhir ṛṇavā jāyate | brahmacaryeṇarṣibhyo yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhya iti [TS 6.3.10.5] |
evam ṛṇasaṃyogavādīny asaṃkhyātāni bhavanti niṣedhāś ca [BDh 2.11.9–33] || 1.14 ||
āpastambaḥ |
trayīṃ vidyāṃ brahmacaryaṃ prajātiṃ śraddhāṃ tapo yajñam anupradānam | ya etāni kurvate tair it saha smo rajo bhūtvā dhvaṃsate ʼnyat praśaṃsann iti [ApDh 2.24.8]
|| 1.15 ||
tasmāt śrutāv adṛṣṭatvāt pratyakṣaśrutivirodhe smṛtīnām aprāmāṇyān na santy ūrdhvaretasa āśramā iti || 1.16 ||
atha syāt | asti śrutir vājasaneyināṃ pratyakṣā |
etam eva pravrājino lokam icchantaḥ pravrajantīti [BaU 4.4.22] |
tasmād brāhmaṇaḥ pāṇḍityaṃ nirvidya bālyena tiṣṭhāset | bālyaṃ ca pāṇḍityaṃ ca nirvidyātha munir iti ca [BaU 3.5.1] || 1.17 ||
ācāraṃ ca darśayati |
etad dha sma vai pūrve vidvāṃsaḥ prajāṃ na kāmayante | kiṃ prajayā kariṣyāmo yeṣāṃ no ʼyam ātmāyaṃ loka iti [BaU 4.4.22] |
ahiotraṃ na juhuvāñ cakrira iti ca || 1.18 ||
atrocyate | kiṃ pārivrājyaṃ puruṣārthaṃ vidhīyate uta pāṇigrahaṇādivad anuṣṭhānāntarārtham | puruṣārthatve tu sa svargaḥ syād iti [PMS 4.3.7.15] nyāyena svargārthaṃ vā syād anyārthaṃ vā | na tāvat svargārtham apavargaprakaraṇavirodhāt | tathā hi sarvatrāpavargaprakaraṇa eva pārivrājyaṃ vidhīyate | tenāpavargaprakaraṇa eva kathaṃcit saṃbandho vācyaḥ | so ʼpi vā na śakyate vaktuṃ jñānād evāpavargaśruteḥ |
tam evaṃ viditvāti mṛtyum eti | nānyaḥ panthā vidyate ʼyanāyeti [VS 31.18] |
vidyayaiva tadārohati yatra kāmāḥ parāhatāḥ |
na tatra dakṣiṇā yanti nāvidvāṃsa stapasvina iti [SB 10.5.4.16] || 1.19||
atha jñānānuṣṭhānāṅgatvena vidhīyata iti matam |
naitat | jñānānuṣṭhānaṃ gṛhasthānām api saṃbhavatīti na tadartham āśramāntaravidhiḥ kalpyaḥ | kalpyamāno ʼpi nāśramāntaraṃ prāpayet | kiṃ tv āhitāvritavad gṛhasthānām eva kiṃcit pārivrājyaṃ nāma vrataṃ vidadhyāt | tac ca pravṛttagārhasthyāvirodhī kaścit saṅgatyāgātmako dharma iti gamyate | smṛtiṣu dṛṣṭānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ śrutivirodhān na saṃgrahaḥ | na ca pārivrājyaśabda evāśramāntaravacano ʼnyatra prasiddhaḥ aśrautatvaprasaṅgāt | tathā ca smṛtiprasiddhaṃ pārivrājyam iyaṃ śrutir vidyāstutaye ʼnuvadatīti syād itthaṃ mahīyasī vidyā yadbalād vihitāny api karmāṇi parityajanto na pratyavayantīti || 1.20 ||
tasmād agnihotrādivad asādhāraṇasya gṛhastheṣv asaṃbhavataḥ śrautasyāśramadharmasyābhāvāt bhikṣāṭanādīnāṃ ca saṃdhyāvandanādivad ācāratvenāśramadharmārthatvāt svayam āśramadharmatvānupapatteḥ na pārivrājyaṃ nāmāśramo ʼstīti kecid ācāryā manyante ||1.21 ||
atrocyate | asty asādhāraṇo dharmo jñānayogo nāma | tathā hi satyādīni mānasāntāni karmāṇy upanyasya
tasmān nyāsam eṣāṃ tapasām atiriktam āhuḥ [MNU 538] |
iti śrutyā saṃnyāsaṃ vidhāya
ātmānaṃ yuñjīta [MNU 540] |
iti nyāsaśabdavācyaṃ jñānayogaṃ vidhatte | jñānayogo nāma kevalasya jñānamātrasyānuṣṭhānam | na hy asau gṛhasthānāṃ saṃbhavati ahiotrāder apy anuṣṭheyatvāt | atas teṣāṃ karmayoga eva | karmayogo nāma jñānakarmasamuccayānuṣṭhānam tato mokṣaḥ | parivrājakānāṃ tu jñānayogād eva mokṣaḥ | ata eṣāṃ karmābhāvāt karmasaṃpādanaṃ pareṇa darśayati,
tasyaivaṃ viduṣo yajñasyātmā [MNU 543] |
ity anuvākena iyam apy ahany ahany anuṣṭheyaiva parivrājakasya vidhīyate | na stutimātram apūrvatvāt || 1.22 ||
nanu parivrājakānām api saṃdhyāvandanādikarmāṇi santi |
satyam | teṣāṃ samācāratvāt āśramadharmārthatvam eva na mokṣārthatvam | mokṣārthaṃ tu jñānayoga eva | tadarthāni saṃdhyāvandanādīni | na hi saṃdhyāhīnasya tatrādhikāraḥ,
saṃdhyāhīno ʼśucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
iti smaraṇāt |
ācārahīnaṃ na punanti vedāḥ |
iti ca | japatapobhikṣāṭanādīnām api yamaniyameṣv antarbhāvo varṇyate | yamaniyamādīnāṃ ca yogāṅgatvaṃ sarvaśāstrasiddham | atas teṣām api jñānayoga evāntarbhāvaḥ || 1.23 ||
tasmāt parivrājakānāṃ jñānayogo gṛhasthānāṃ karmayoga iti sthitam | tathā ca,
na karmaṇā na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike amṛtatvam ānaśuḥ [MNU 227] |
iti karmatyāgenaivāmṛtatvaṃ śrūyate |
vedāntavijñānasuniścitārthāḥ
saṃnyāsayogād yatayaḥ śuddhasattvāḥ |
te brahmaloke tu parāntakāle
parāmṛtāt parimucyanti sarve || [MNU 229–30]
iti ca | tathā ca bhagavān vāsudevaḥ |
jñānayogena sāṃkhyānāṃ karmayogena yoginām | iti [BhG 3.3] || 1.24 ||
tad evam asādhāraṇasya dharmasya bhāvāt asti pārivrājyaṃ nāma śrauta āśramaḥ | tanmūlatvāc ca smṛtīnāṃ prāmāṇyam | na caiṣa dharmo gṛhasthānāṃ saṃbhavati bhikṣācaryaṃ carantīti sarvatra dravyatyāgaśravaṇāt | tasmād āśramāntaram eva pārivrājyam iti siddham || 1.25 ||
iti yādavaprakāśena viracite yatidharmasamuccaye
pārivrājyavidhir nāma prathamaṃ parva || 1.26 ||
dvitīyaṃ parva
pārivrājyādhikāridaśānirūpaṇam
athādhikāridaśā cintyate || 2.1 || vanavāsād ūrdhvam eva pārivrājyam na brahmacaryād ity ekaḥ pakṣaḥ || 2.2 || tathā ca śrutiḥ |
ācāryāya priyaṃ dhanam āhṛtya prajātantuṃ mā vyavacchetsīr iti [TU 1.11.1]
|| 2.3 ||
tathā |
jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇa iti ca | [TS 6.3.10.5]
prajananaṃ vai pratiṣṭhā sādhu prajāyās tantuṃ tanvānaḥ pitṝṇām anṛṇo bhavatīti ca | [MNU 525] || 2.4 ||
tathā ca manuḥ |
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamāno vrajaty adhaḥ || iti [MDh 6.35] || 2.5 ||
āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
adhītavedo japakṛt putravān annado ʼgnimān |
śaktyā ca yajñakṛn mokṣe manaḥ kuryāt tu nānyathā || iti [YDh 3.57] || 2.6 ||
āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
vanavāsād ūrdhvaṃ pariṇatavayasaḥ kāmataḥ pārivrājyam iti || 2.7 ||
āha medhātithiḥ |
putradāragṛhakṣetragohiraṇyādibandhanaiḥ |
virakto mānuṣair bhogair divyaiś ca śrutidarśitaiḥ || 2.8 ||
vedābhyāsena yajñena tapasā vītakalmaṣaḥ |
janmajanmāntarābhyāsād viśuddhātmā jitendriyaḥ || 2.9 ||
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ śrutyā ca pratibodhitaḥ |
yatate paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatanāt sa yatir bhavet |
tasyeyaṃ paramā siddhir yater nānyasya kasyacit || iti || 2.10 ||
kecit tu brahmacaryād gṛhād vanād vā yathākāmaṃ pārivrājyam ity āhuḥ || 2.11 ||
tathā ca jābāliśrutiḥ |
brahmacaryaṃ samāpya gṛhī bhavet | gṛhī bhūtvā vanī bhavet | vanī bhūtvā pravrajet | yadi vetarathā brahmacaryād eva pravrajed gṛhād vanād veti | [JU 64] || 2.12 ||
āha yamaḥ |
cīrṇavedavrato vidvān brāhmaṇo mokṣam āśrayet |
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu sthāvareṣu careṣu ca || 2.13 ||
jātaputro gṛhastho vā vijitātmā jitendriyaḥ |
niḥspṛhaḥ sarvakāmebhyaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥ parivrajet |
vanastho vā taptatapāḥ paraṃ panthānam āśrayet || iti || 2.14 ||
atra yadi brahmacārī pravrajet cīrṇavrata eva pravrajet | yadi gṛhastho jātaputra eva iṣṭayajñaś ca tān agnīn hṛdi saṃhṛtyeti pūrvam uktatvāt | vānaprasthaś cet taptatapā eva pravrajet | iti gamyate | etad uttaratrāpi yojyam | etadviṣayam eva mānavam api vacanam ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtyetyādi || 2.15 || kecid āhuḥ |
brahmacārī gṛhastho vā mṛtadāro vanecaraḥ |
jñātvā Samyak paraṃ brahma tyaktvā saṅgān parivrajet ||iti || 2.16 ||
atra gṛhasthasya mṛtadārasya putrotpattyādeḥ prāg api sati vairāgye pārivrājyaṃ gamyate || 2.17 || āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
asāram eva saṃsāraṃ dṛṣṭvā sāradidṛkṣayā |
pravrajed akṛtodvāhaḥ paraṃ vairāgyam āsthitaḥ || 2.18 ||
anyo ʼpy utpannasantāna iṣṭvā ca vividhair makhaiḥ |
patitārdhaśarīro vā vanād anyaḥ parivrajet || iti || 2.19 ||
ardhaśarīraṃ bhāryā,
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya bhāryā yasya surāṃ pibet | [VaDh 21.15]
iti darśanāt,
ardho vā eṣa ātmano yatpatnī | [TS 6.1.8.5]
iti śruteś ca || 2.20 || āha śaṅkhaḥ |
evaṃ nītvā vane kālaṃ dvijo brahmāśramī bhavet |
nānopaniṣadāṃ jñānāt prāptavairāgyapāṭavaḥ || 2.21 ||
samāhṛtendriyagrāmo viṣayebhyaḥ sudūrataḥ |
ātmaikarāmo manasā nirasya paripanthinaḥ || 2.22 ||
yogābhyāsena bahuśo labdhātmā dṛḍhabandhanaḥ |
sarvasaṅganivṛttātmā brahmacāry api suvrataḥ || 2.23 ||
etair guṇair upetaś ced gṛhastho vā prajāpateḥ |
kṛtveṣṭiṃ vidhivad dattvā sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya dvijo brahmāśramī bhavet || iti || 2.24 ||
evaṃ kāścit śrutayaḥ smṛtayaś ca brahmacaryād eva pārivrājyaṃ vidadhati kāścit tu ṛṇāpakaraṇāder ūrdhvam eveti | evaṃ virodhe sati kecid āhuḥ: andhapaṃgvādīn karmasv anadhikṛtān adhikṛtya brahmacaryād utthānaśrutayaḥ smṛtayaś ca || 2.25 || tathā cāha likhitaḥ |
janmakoṭiśatārabdhapuṇyāptaṃ devakāritam |
vairāgyaṃ jāyate yasya tṛṣṇāpi brahmaṇi sthirā || 2.26 ||
so ʼdhītavedatattvārtho gṛhavāsam aśaknuvan |
pravrajed brahmacaryāc ca gṛhāj jīrṇo vanād api || 2.27 ||
nīrujaś ca yuvā caiva vased gārhasthyam ādṛtaḥ |
andhaḥ paṅgus tathā kuṣṭhī vṛddho maunaṃ samāśrayet || iti || 2.28 ||
anye tv āhuḥ | ubhayīṣu śrutismṛtiṣu viparītaḥ kiṃ na bhavet | aviraktaḥ pārivrājyaṃ kartum aśaknuvan kiṃ na gṛhī bhavet | na hy aviraktaḥ pārivrājyaṃ kartuṃ śaknotīti || 2.29 || tathā ca dakṣaḥ |
sattvotkaṭāḥ sarve te ʼpi viṣayais tu vaśīkṛtāḥ |
kiṃ punaḥ kṣudrasattvais tu mānavair atra kā katha || 2.30 ||
tasmāt pakvakaṣāyeṇa kartavyaṃ daṇḍadhāraṇam |
itaras tu na śaknoti viṣayair hriyate yataḥ || 2.31 ||
tridaṇḍaṃ liṅgam āśritya jīvanti bahavo dvijāḥ |
yo hi brahma na jānāti tridaṇḍārho na sa smṛtaḥ || iti || 2.32 ||
āha likhitaḥ |
yaḥ kṛtvā sarvasaṃnyāsaṃ pratyāpattiṃ bhajed dvijaḥ |
taṃ dāsye yojayed rājā śvapadenāṅkayet tathā || 2.33 ||
tasmād āśayaśuddhiṃ prāg bahukālaṃ parīkṣya ca |
mano na calate dharmād iti jñātvātha saṃnyaset || iti || 2.34 ||
āha yamaḥ |
nivṛttaḥ kāmarāgādeḥ praśānto brahmatatparaḥ |
sa saṃnyāse ʼdhikārī syād itaro jīvikāparaḥ || iti || 2.35 ||
na cānadhikṛtaviṣayā hy utthānaśrutir iti yuktaṃ teṣām uttaratra pṛthag eva vidhānāt |
atha punar vratī vāvratī vā snātako vāsnātako vā sārinakio vetyādinā |
[JU 64] || 2.36 ||
āha vṛddhajābāliḥ |
vratiko vāvratiko vā snātako vāsnātako vā āvasathy anāvasathī vā agnihotry anagnihotrī vā grāmād agnim āhṛtyetyādi || 2.37 ||
yat punar uktam,
andhaḥ paṅgus tathā kuṣṭhī vṛddho maunaṃ samāśrayet |
iti, tad apy aviruddham andhādiśabdānām anyathā vyākhyātatvāt || 2.38 || tathā hi medhātithiḥ |
adyajātāṃ yathā nārīṃ tathā ṣoḍaśavārṣikīm |
śatavarṣāṃ ca yo dṛṣṭvā nirvikāraḥ sa ṣaṇḍhakaḥ || 2.39 ||
bhikṣārthaṃ gamanaṃ yasya viṇmūtrakaraṇāya ca |
yojanān na paraṃ yāti yaḥ sadā paṅgur eva saḥ || 2.40 ||
tiṣṭhato vrajato vāpi yasya cakṣur na dūragam |
caturyugād bhayaṃ muktvā parivrāṭ so ʼndha ucyate || 2.41 ||
hitāhitaṃ manorāmaṃ vacaḥ śokāvahaṃ ca yat |
śrutvā yo na śṛṇotīva badhiraḥ parikīrtitaḥ || iti || 2.42 ||
na cāndhasya pārivrājye ʼdhikāraḥ,
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyasetpādam | [MDh 6.46]
iti dharmavidhānāt || 2.43 || nāpi paṅgoḥ kuṣṭhino vā,
paryaṭan puṇyatīrthāni devatāyatanāni ca |
adhvāsya sūryanirdiṣṭaḥ kīṭavac ca mahīṃ caret ||
ityādidharmavidhānāt || 2.44 || brahmacaryavidhānān na ṣaṇḍhasya pārivrājyam | brahmacaryaṃ hi maithunavarjanam | na hi ṣaṇḍhasya maithunavarjanam asti tasya sthāṇukalpasya maithune prāptyabhāvāt | prāptasya hi varjanaṃ vratam || 4.45 || tasmād ṛṇaśrutir aviraktaviṣayā | viraktasya tu pārivrājyam eva || 2.46 || tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
pravrajed akṛtodvāhaḥ paraṃ vairāgyam āśritaḥ | iti || 2.47 ||
āha kratuḥ |
brahmacārī gṛhastho vā mṛtadāro vanecaraḥ |
jñātvā Samyak paraṃ brahma tyaktvā saṅgān parivrajet || iti || 2.48 ||
āha śaṅkhaḥ |
sarvasaṅganivṛttātmā brahmacāry api suvrataḥ |
etair guṇair upetaś ced gṛhastho vā prajāpateḥ || 2.49 ||
kṛtveṣṭiṃ vidhivad dattvā sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya dvijo brahmāśramī bhavet || iti || 2.50 ||
āha dattātreyaḥ |
tridaṇḍaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ liṅgaṃ viprāṇāṃ muktisādhanam |
nirvāṇaṃ sarvadharmāṇām iti vedānuśāsanam |
gṛhavāsaviraktānāṃ purā proktaṃ svayaṃbhuvā || iti || 2.51 ||
upakurvāṇasya brahmacāriṇaś caturṣv āśrameṣu icchāvikalpa ity ācāryāḥ || 2.52 ||tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
catvāra āśramāḥ brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ | teṣāṃ vedam adhītya vedau vā vedān vā cīrṇabrahmacaryo yam icchet tam āvased iti || [VaDh 7.1–3] || 2.53 ||
āha cāpastambaḥ |
buddhvā karmāṇi yat kāmayeta tad ārabheteti [ApDh 2.21.5] || 2.54 ||
āha bhagavān vyāsaḥ |
guruśuśrūṣayā vidyāṃ saṃprāpya vidhivad dvijaḥ |
snāyīta gurvanujñāto dattvāsmai dakṣiṇāṃ hi gām || 2.55 ||
naiṣṭhikaṃ vā muner vāpi pārivrājyakam eva vā |
yam icched āśramaṃ vipro vaset tatraiva saṃyataḥ || iti || 2.56 ||
āha gautamaḥ |
tasyāśramavikalpam eke bruvata iti [GDh 3.1] || 2.57 ||
yad uktam ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtyeti tad api śrutismṛtibhyāṃ viraktasya brahmacaryād eva pārivrājyaṃ vidadhatībhyāṃ vyavasthāpyamānaṃ rāgavati puruṣa evāvatiṣṭhate prāptarṇāpakaraṇārthaṃ vā || 2.58 ||
tasmād viraktasya brahmacaryād gṛhād vanād vā pārivrājyaṃ siddham || 2.59 || tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
paramātmani yo rakto virakto ʼparamātmani |
sarvaiṣaṇāvinirmuktaḥ sa bhaikṣaṃ bhoktum arhati || 2.60 ||
āturasyāpi pārivrājyam aṅgirasoktam |
vanavāsāt pariśrāntaḥ pravrajed vidhipūrvakam |
vyādhyāviṣṭo virakto vā brahmavit saṃnyased dvijaḥ || 2.61 ||
pravrajed brahmacaryāc ca pravrajec ca gṛhād api |
vanād vā pravrajed vidvān āturo vātha duḥkhitaḥ || 2.62 ||
utpanne saṃkaṭe ghore coravyāghrādigocare |
bhayabhītasya saṃnyāsam aṅgirā munir abravīt || 2.63 ||
tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
saṃnyastam iti yo brūyāt prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatair api |
sa tu kratusahasreṇa tulyaṃ phalam avāpnuyāt || 2.64 ||
āha dattātreyaḥ |
mukhajānām ayaṃ dharmo viṣṇor yal liṅgadhāraṇam |
bāhujātorujātānām ayaṃ dharmo na vidyate || 2.65 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye pārivrājyādhikāridaśānirūpaṇaṃ nāma
dvitīyaṃ parva || 2.66 ||
tṛtīyaṃ parva
liṅganirūpaṇam
atha liṅgaṃ nirūpyate | tac ca dvividhaṃ bāhyaṃ śārīraṃ ca | tatra śārīraṃ yajñopavītaṃ muṇḍatvaṃ śikhāmātradhāraṇaṃ veti | bāhyaṃ daṇḍakamaṇḍaluprabhṛti | bāhyaṃ liṅgaṃ mātrā ity ucyate || 3.1 ||
tatra śārīram ucyate | āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yajñopavīty udakamaṇḍaluhasta iti [VaDh 10.31] |
muṇḍo ʼmado ʼkrodho ʼparigraha iti ca [VaDh 10.6] || 3.2 ||
āha yamaḥ |
nityaṃ sthaṇḍilaśāyī syād anityāṃ vasatiṃ vaset |
araṇyanityo muṇḍaḥ syān manasā jñānam abhyaset || iti || 3.3 ||
āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
apratiṣṭho munir muṇḍo nirmamo niṣparigrahaḥ iti || 3.4 ||
āha bodhāyanaḥ |
araṇyaṃ gatvā śikhī muṇḍo veti [BDh 2.11.17–18] || 3.5 ||
āha kratuḥ |
stutvā haṃsaṃ tribhiḥ sūktair mano vaiṣṇavapauruṣaiḥ |
muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā kāṣāyī pātrī daṇḍī gṛhād vrajet || 3.6 ||
āha sumantuḥ |
munir muṇḍo ʼparigraha iti || 3.7 ||
āha gautamaḥ |
muṇḍaḥ śikhī veti [GDh 3.21] || 3.8 ||
tasmān mauṇḍyaṃ śikhā veti vikalpaḥ | yajñopavītaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ nityam eva || 3.9 || tathātriḥ pārivrājyam evādhikṛtyāha |
brahmasūtraṃ tridaivatyaṃ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakam |
parityajanti viprā ye mohāt tarkopajīvinaḥ |
svargāpavargamārgābhyāṃ pracyutās te na saṃśayaḥ || iti || 3.10 ||
āhośanāḥ |
brahmasūtraparityāgād brahmacārī gṛhī vanī |
parivrāṭ cāpi patati tasmāt tan na parityajet || iti || 3.11||
atha bāhyaliṅgam ucyate | āha dakṣaḥ |
mekhalājinadaṇḍādyair brahmacārīti lakṣyate |
gṛhastho yaṣṭivedādyair nakharomair vanāśramī |
tridaṇḍena yatiś ceti lakṣaṇāni pṛthak pṛthak || 3.12 ||
āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
evaṃ saṃnyasanaṃ kṛtvā tridaṇḍaṃ sapavitrakam |
keśasaṃmitam ādāya pātraṃ karakam eva ca || iti || 3.13 ||
āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
sarvārambhān utsṛjya tridaṇḍakuṇḍikāpakṣmapavitraprayojanāpekṣī muṇḍaḥ kāṣāyavāsā bhaikṣavṛttir | iti || 3.14 ||
āhāṅgirāḥ |
yater liṅgaṃ pravakṣyāmi yenāsau lakṣyate yatiḥ |
brahmasūtraṃ tridaṇḍaṃ ca vastraṃ jantunivāraṇam || 3.15 ||
śikyaṃ pātraṃ bṛsī caiva kaupīnaṃ kaṭiveṣṭanam |
yasyaitad vidyate liṅgaṃ sa yatir netaro yatiḥ || iti || 3.16 ||
bṛsī kūrmāsanam | āha devalaḥ |
kāṣāyamuṇḍatridaṇḍakamaṇḍalupātrapavitrapādukāsanakanthā mātrā iti || 3.17 ||
āha gālavaḥ |
atha parivrājako liṅgī muṇḍaḥ śikhī vā nityaṃ yajñopavītī kāṣāyakarpaṭaḥ kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ vāso bibhṛyān nottarīyaṃ tridaṇḍaṃ jalapavitraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ kapālaṃ kanthāṃ ceti || 3.18 ||
āha likhitaḥ |
kāṣāyam eva kārpāsaṃ vāsaḥ kanthāṃ ca dhārayet |
valkalaṃ vājinaṃ vāpi kauśaṃ śaṇamayaṃ tu vā || 3.19 ||
śikyaṃ sakavacaṃ pātraṃ triviṣṭabdhena saṃyutam |
baddhaṃ jalapavitreṇa tridaṇḍaṃ vartulāsanam || 3.20 ||
śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ ca śaucārtham api kuṇḍikām |
sarvadā dhārayed gacchann āsīno vāpi vā śuciḥ || iti || 3.21 ||
āha yamaḥ |
sūcī pippalakaṃ caiva triviṣṭabdham upānahau |
kuṇḍikā rajjuśikyaṃ ca bhaikṣabhājanam āsanam |
kanthāṃ karpaṭakaṃ daṇḍaṃ laghumātrāparigrahaḥ || iti || 3.22 ||
āhośanāḥ |
pātraṃ tatkavacaṃ śikyaṃ tridaṇḍaṃ sapavitrakam |
āsanaṃ kaṭisūtraṃ ca kaupīnaṃ kaṭiveṣṭanam |
yajñopavītaṃ kanthāṃ ca saṃgṛhyānyat parityajet || iti || 3.23 ||
āha kratuḥ |
viṃśatiṃ dhārayen mātrā daśa vā pañca nityaśaḥ |
kanthā sūcyajinaṃ chatraṃ pavitraṃ kuṇḍikāsanam || 3.24 ||
pātraṃ śikyākṣasūtrāṇi mṛtkhanitram upānahau |
guhyācchādas tripādī ca pāduke daṇḍapakṣmaṇī |
yogapaṭṭo bahirvāso mātrākhyā viṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.25 ||
āsāṃ viṃśatimātrāṇāṃ pañcamātrā nityāḥ | tāś ca upavītatridaṇḍapavitrapātrakaupīna-kaṭivastrākhyāḥ || 3.26 || tathā viṣṇuḥ |
upavītaṃ tridaṇḍaṃ ca pātraṃ jalapavitrakam |
kaupīnaṃ kaṭivastraṃ ca na tyājyaṃ yāvadāyuṣam || iti || 3.27 ||
pātragrahaṇād eva tadaṅgabhūtaśikyakavacatriviṣṭabdhānām api grahaṇam eva | daśamātrās tu yāḥ smṛtyuktāḥ sūcī pippalakaṃ cetyādi | tatra sūcīpippalayor ekīkaraṇena daśatvam | pippalaṃ sūtram || 3.28 ||
dvidhākāraḥ parivrāṭ | jñānābhyāsaśīlo yogam ārurukṣur ekaḥ aparas tu ārūḍhayogas tattvadarśī avyaktaliṅgo ʼvyaktācāro ʼnunmatta unmattavad ācaran || 3.29 || tathā ca kratunā dvaividhyaṃ darśitam |
pūrvāhṇe vicared yogī samyagjñānī tu paścime | iti || 3.30 ||
tatra samyagjñānam abhipretya kratur evāha |
śīlaṃ vṛttaṃ śrutaṃ jñānaṃ vairāgyaṃ dharmalakṣaṇam |
aiśvaryaṃ vidyate yasya mātrābhis tasya kiṃ phalam || 3.31|
dhyānaṃ śaucaṃ tapaḥ pūjāṃ prāṇāyāmajapastutīḥ |
saṃdhyāṃ bhikṣām upasthānaṃ kuryād āmaraṇāntikam || iti || 3.32 ||
pañca nityaśa iti vidhānāt bhikṣāvidhānāc ca samyagjñāninām api pañcamātrādhāraṇam avaśyaṃ kartavyam eva || 3.33 ||
athaiṣām upavītādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam ucyate1, tatra tāvad yajñopavītalakṣaṇam || 3.34 || pārivrājyam evādhikṛtya dattātreyātrī cāhatuḥ |
tantutrayam adhovṛttaṃ punaś cordhvaṃ trivṛttrivṛt |
kārpāsaṃ tāntavaṃ kāryaṃ tathaikaṃ cottarīyakam || 3.35 ||
adhovṛttais tribhiḥ sūtrais tribhir ūrdhvaṃ trivṛtkṛtam |
brahmasūtraṃ smṛtaṃ brāhmaṃ kārpāsaṃ navatāntavam || 3.36 ||
saṃgraho navasūtrasya dvijānāṃ muktisādhanam |
vinā tena bhavet sadyaḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || iti || 3.37 ||
daṇḍalakṣaṇam ucyate | kapila āha |
aṅguliparimāṇam ṛjuṃ nāsāyataṃ vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍaṃ daṇḍān vā dhārayed ātmasaṃmitaṃ vā || 3.38 || athāpy udāharanti |
aṅkurā yatra dṛśyante śubhāḥ patraviniḥsṛtāḥ |
tat tu parva vijānīyāc cheṣaṃ madhyam ihocyate || 3.39 ||
tripañcasaptanavakaṃ parva caikādaśaṃ tathā |
varjito daṇḍa ity āhur yateḥ sarvatra tasya ca |
adhastāt parvaṇo ʼdhastād ūrdhvād ūrdhvāṅgulidvayam || 3.40 ||
idaṃ gṛhasthānām apy aviruddham | āha dattātreyaḥ |
tridaṇḍān aṅgulisthūlān vaiṇavān mūrdhasaṃmitān |
satvacān avraṇān saumyān samasaṃnataparvakān |
ṣaḍaṣṭadaśaparvāṇo yonihīnāṃś ca nirvraṇān || 3.41 ||
dakṣaḥ |
keśamātrān samagranthīṃs tridaṇḍān vaiṇavān yatiḥ || 3.42 ||
dhārayed iti | vṛddhadakṣaḥ |
tridaṇḍaṃ dhārayed vidvān ūrdhvasthodapavitrakam || 3.43 ||
hārītaḥ |
tridaṇḍaṃ pañcadhā kṛtvā tribhāgā dūrdhvam eva ca |
veṣṭayet kṛṣṇagovālarajjvā tu caturaṅgulam || 3.44 ||
granthidhārātrayopetaṃ jalapūtena copari |
gṛhṇīyād dakṣiṇe haste gurudattaṃ tu mantravat || 3.45 ||
jalapūtaṃ jalapavitram | vṛddhadakṣaḥ |
tridaṇḍāgre ca mūle ca madhye sūtreṇa bandhayet |
agrād dvibhāgaṃ hitvā ca tatraiva caturaṅgulam || 3.46 ||
govāladāmnā badhnīyād atha vā pañcabandhanam |
rajjvaiva bandhanaṃ kuryāt pañcadhā vibhajet punaḥ || 3.47 ||
śrīpāñcarātre tatvasāgarasaṃhitāyām |
tridaṇḍasyāgrabhāgaṃ tu saṃsmaret parameṣṭhinam |
dvitīyaṃ puruṣātmānaṃ viśvātmānaṃ tu madhyamam || 3.48||
turyaṃ nivṛttipuruṣaṃ sarvātmānaṃ tu pañcamam |
tridaṇḍaṃ samudāyena viṣṇurūpaṃ smared yatiḥ || 3.49 ||
hārītaḥ |
viṣṇurūpaṃ tridaṇḍākhyaṃ sarvadā dhāred yatiḥ || 3.50 ||
śaunakaḥ |
jñānadaṇḍo bhaved dhrasva ātmadaṇḍas tu madhyamaḥ |
viṣṇudaṇḍas tu dīrghaḥ syāt tridaṇḍaṃ dhārayed yatiḥ || 3.51 ||
pracetāḥ |
brahmasūtraṃ tridaṇḍaṃ ca śikhāvad bibhṛyāt svayam |
vāhayed itareṇātha prāyaścittī bhaved yatiḥ || 3.52 ||
pauruṣeṇaiva sūktena tridaṇḍaṃ cābhiṣecayet |
saptabhir vaiṣṇavīṛgbhiḥ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 3.53 ||
snāne cāvaśyake caiva bhojane japakarmaṇi |
svādhyāye cāpi nidrāyām upa daṇḍena bandhayet || 3.54 ||
tridaṇḍadhāraṇaṃ caikaṃ vaiṇavaṃ ca tathā yatiḥ |
kaupīnakaṭivastrādi dhāraṇārthaṃ sadā vahet || 3.55 ||
kuryān mūtrapurīṣe cet tridaṇḍasahito yatiḥ |
śuddhyarthaṃ saha daṇḍena cared ācamanakriyām || 3.56 ||
āha vṛddhajābāliḥ |
apāṃ pūrṇāñjaliṃ ninīya daṇḍaṃ daṇḍān vā gṛhītvetyādi || 3.57 ||
āha bodhāyanaḥ |
na cāta ūrdhvaṃ śuklam ambaraṃ bibhṛyāt | ekadaṇḍī tridaṇḍī veti |
[BDh 2.17.44—18.1] || 3.58 ||
āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
eko vā tādṛśo daṇḍo govālarahito bhavet | iti || 3.59 ||
tasmād daṇḍavikalpaḥ | yatnagauravād bhūyasīṣu dṛṣṭatvāc ca aikadaṇḍyāt traidaṇḍyam eva śastam || 3.60 || tathāha bhagavān vyāsaḥ |
tridaṇḍadhāraṇaṃ śastaṃ tathaikāntaniṣevaṇam |
laghvāhāram anāyāso yatīnāṃ muktisādhanam || iti || 3.61 ||
daṇḍamātrābhedād itaramātrāḥ saṃdhyāvandanādayaś ca dharmā na bhidyante | kvacit tridaṇḍanāśe tv ekadaṇḍo vidhīyate || 3.62 || atrijābālāv āhatuḥ |
naṣṭe jalapavitre vā tridaṇḍe vā pramādataḥ |
gṛhṇīyād vaiṇavaṃ caikaṃ pālāśaṃ vāpi madhyamam || 3.63 ||
brahmapatram abhāve ʼpi kuśastambham athāpi vā |
gṛhītvā vicaret tāvad yāvad anyaṃ na labhyate || iti || 3.64 ||
āha hārītaḥ |
naṣṭe jalapavitre vā tridaṇḍe vā pramādataḥ |
ekaṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍaṃ pālāśaṃ madhyamaṃ tu vā |
gṛhītvā vicaret tāvad yāval labdhaṃ tridaṇḍakam || 3.65 ||
yatnenānveṣayen nityam apramattaḥ samāhitaḥ |
labdhe tridaṇḍe gṛhṇīyāj jalapūtam athopari || iti || 3.66 ||
āha medhātithiḥ |
yāvan na syus trayo daṇḍās tāvad ekena paryaṭet |
tatrāpi vastram āsajya tv agradeśe ʼmbuśuddhaye || iti || 3.67 ||
jamadagniḥ |
tridaṇḍe na tu badhnīyāt kaupīnaṃ kaṭiveṣṭanam |
yo mohād bandhanaṃ kuryāt tridaṇḍam avamanyate || 3.68 ||
tridaṇḍam anyadaṇḍena saṃgataṃ cet pramādataḥ |
prakṣālya mṛjjalair bhikṣuḥ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret || 3.69 ||
kalau yuge kuṇḍikā na dhāryā | tathā ca devalaḥ |
dvāparādiyuge caiva pātrabhojī yatir bhavet |
kalau svapātre naivādyād dhārayen na tu kuṇḍikām || 3.70 ||
jalapavitralakṣaṇaṃ medhātithir āha |
vikeśaṃ sitam aspṛṣṭam ubhayaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam |
aspṛṣṭam anupayuktam ity arthaḥ |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vāpi sarvato ʼṣṭāṅgulaṃ tathā || 3.71 ||
prādeśamātraṃ vā sūkṣmaṃ kārpāsakṛtam avraṇam |
caṇḍālādyakṛtaṃ caiva vidyāj jalapavitrakam || 3.72 ||
āhāṅgirāḥ |
yatīnāṃ trīṇi śuklāni bhavanty etāni nityaśaḥ |
yajñopavītaṃ dantāś ca tathā jantunivāraṇam || iti || 3.73 ||
pātrāṇy āhātriḥ |
mṛddārvalābunirvṛttaṃ vaiṇavaṃ tāntavaupalam |
tārṇaṃ pātraṃ tathā kṣaumaṃ pātraṃ parṇapuṭaṃ tathā || 3.74 ||
eteṣām ekam ādeyam ātmaśaktyanurūpataḥ |
lobhāt pātrāntaraṃ gṛhṇan prāṇāyāmān daśācaret || 3.75 ||
āha kratuḥ |
veṇudāruśilālābutṛṇaparṇamayāni ca |
mṛnmayaṃ kṣaumapātrāṇi gṛhṇīyād ātmaśaktitaḥ || iti || 3.76 ||
manuḥ |
alābudārupātrāṇi mṛnmayaṃ vaiṇavaṃ tu vā |
etāni yatipātrāṇi manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ʼbravīt || iti [MDh 6.54] || 3.77 ||
śikyalakṣaṇaṃ hārīta āha |
kṣaumaṃ vā kuśasūtraṃ vā sūtraṃ kārpāsakaṃ tu vā |
tair eva grathitaṃ śikyaṃ padmākārasamanvitam |
ṣaḍbhir vā pañcabhir vāpi muṣṭibhiḥ śikyalakṣaṇam || iti || 3.78 ||
āsanalakṣaṇaṃ sa evāha |
āsanaṃ vartulaṃ proktaṃ sahastaṃ dārujaṃ yateḥ |
śaucārtham āsanārthaṃ ca nirmitaṃ munibhiḥ purā || iti || 3.79 ||
khanitralakṣaṇaṃ kapila āha |
caturaṅgulamātramṛttikākhananaparigrahe na doṣa iti || 3.80 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye liṅganirūpaṇaṃ nāma
tṛtīyaṃ parva || 3.81 |
caturthaṃ parva
saṃnyāsaprayogaḥ
atha prayoga ucyate | athātaḥ śaunakaproktaṃ saṃnyāsavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tatra tasminn anuktāny anyatroktāny apekṣitāni tatra tatra nigadyante prayogāṅgāni || 4.1 || tatra puraścaraṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
kṛcchracatuṣṭayaṃ kāryam anāśramibhiḥ | kṛcchram ekam āśramibhiḥ | evaṃ saṃnyāsayogyatā bhavati | dvādaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vā saṃkalpya vapanaṃ kṛtvā devaśrāddhaṃ prathamam ṛṣiśrāddhaṃ dvitīyaṃ divyaśrāddhaṃ tṛtīyaṃ pitryaṃ caturthaṃ mātṛkaṃ pañcamaṃ mānuṣaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ bhautikaṃ saptamam ātmanaś cāṣṭamam iti || 4.2 ||
evam āha bhagavān vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ | paurṇamāsyām ityādi samānam || 4.3 || āha śaunakaḥ |
pūrvedyur nāndīmukhaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇān bhojayet | atha puṇyāhaṃ svasti ṛddhim iti vācayitvā keśaśmaśrulomanakhāni vāpayed iti || 4.4 ||
tatrāha bhagavān jāmadagnyo ʼpi |
keśaśmaśrulomanakhāni vāpayed iti || 4.5 ||
āha śaunakaḥ |
atha yathāvidhi snātvā homādidravyaṃ varjayitvā sarvasvaṃ dadyād iti || 4.6 ||
āha bodhāyanaḥ |
yaṣṭayaḥ śikyaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ jalapavitraṃ pātram ity etāni |
[BDh 2.17.11–12] || 4.7 ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
tridaṇḍam āsanaṃ kanthāṃ śikyaṃ pātraṃ kamaṇḍalum |
ṣaṇṇāṃ parigrahaṃ kuryāc cheṣaṃ vittaṃ tyajed budhaḥ || 4.8 ||
dravyatyāgaṃ prakurvīta malaśuddhir vidhīyate |
atyāge putravittānāṃ saṃnyāsaṃ naiva kārayet iti || 4.9 ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
tridaṇḍaṃ jalapavitraṃ śikyaṃ pātraṃ kaupīnaṃ kāṣāyavastram asiṃnidhau saṃsādyānagnimān agnim utpādyeti || 4.10 ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
anagnīnām agnyutpādanam ekāgnividhāneneti || 4.11 ||
śaunakaḥ |
atha daṇḍādīn saṃnidhāpya devāyatane grāme grāmasīmānte vā nadīpuline puṇye vā pūrvaṃ manasā brahmāñjaliṃ kṛtvā japet |
brahmaṇe namaḥ | indrāya namaḥ | sūryāya namaḥ | ātmane nama iti |
athāpa upaspṛśya darbhāñjaliṃ kṛtvā vedādīñ japet | atha saktumuṣṭiṃ prāśyācamanaṃ karoti | atha nābhideśam abhimantrayet |
ātmane svāhā | antarātmane svāhā | prajāpataye svāheti |
atha payo dadhi sarpir iti trivṛt prāśyācamyopavaset | apo vā bhakṣayet | japec ca |
oṃ bhūḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | tat savitur vareṇyam |
oṃ bhuvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | bhargo devasya dhīmahi |
oṃ suvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt |
oṃ bhūḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | tat saviturvareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi
|oṃ bhuvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayā
oṃ suvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | tat savitur vareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt |
oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi | tat savitur vareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt |
ity ātmānam āśramāntaram upanīya brahmabhūto bhavatīti vijñāyate | athāpyudāharanti |
āśramād āśramaṃ gatvā hutahomo jitendriyaḥ |
bhikṣābalipariśrāntaḥ paścād bhavati bhikṣukaḥ ||
sa eva bhikṣur ānantyāyeti || 4.12 ||
āha śaunakaḥ |
atha punar ādityasyāstamayād ājyaṃ vilāpya utpūya sruci caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhītvā samiddhe ʼgnau pūrṇāhutiṃ juhoti oṃ svāheti | atha sāyam agnikāryaṃ kṛtvā uttareṇāgnim tṛṇāni saṃstīrya teṣu pātrāṇi sādayitvā dakṣiṇenāgniṃ brahmāyatane darbhān saṃstīrya kṛṣṇājinam antardhāya tāṃ rātriṃ jāgarti | atha brāhme muhūrte utthāya yathāvidhi snātvā prātaragnikāryaṃ karoti | atha vyāhṛtīr japitvāntarjale tiṣṭhan tarat sa mandīti sūktaṃ japet | atha brāhmaṇān annena paritoṣya puṇyāhaṃ svasti ṛddhim iti vācayitvāgnim upasamādhāyājyāhutiṃ juhoti |
prāṇāya svāhā | apānāya svāhā | vyānāya svāhā | udānāya svāhā | samānāya svāheti |
atha puruṣasūktena pratyṛcaṃ samidājyacarūñ juhoti | atha puruṣasūktaṃ japitvā praṇītāpātraṃ saṃstīrya sauviṣṭakṛtena juhoti | yathākāmaṃ jayādisauviṣṭakṛtaṃ juhoti | athācāryāyājyapātraṃ hiraṇyaṃ dhenuṃ ca dattvā anyad api yathāśraddhaṃ dadyāt | athācāryam upatiṣṭheta saṃ mā siñcantv iti | tato yā te agne yajñiyā tanur ity ātmany agnīn samāropayet | athāgnipūrve ʼntarjale vā tiṣṭhan oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayeti trir upāṃśu trir madhyamena svareṇa trir uccaiḥ | atha abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo mattaḥ svāheti prāṅmukho ʼpāṃ pūrṇāñjaliṃ ninayatīti || 4.13 ||
evaṃ bodhāyano ʼpi oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayetyādi apo ninayatītyantam uktvā [BDh 2.17.27–29] |
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo dattvā yaś carate muniḥ |
tasyāpi sarvabhūtebhyo na bhayaṃ jātu vidyate || iti [BDh 2.17.30] || 4.14 ||
vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
saṃnyastaṃ mayeti svarakrameṇa trir vāco vadan |
triṃśat parāṃs triṃśad aparāṃs triṃśac ca parataḥ parān |
sadyaḥ saṃnyasanād eva narakāt trāyate pitṝn || 4.15 ||
saṃnyastaṃ hi dvijaṃ dṛṣṭvā sthānāc calati bhāskaraḥ |
eṣa me maṇḍalaṃ bhitvā paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 4.16 ||
viraktena tu vipreṇa saṃnyāsāśramam icchatā |
yatra tatra mṛtaś cāpi na sa bhūyo bhaviṣyati || 4.17 ||
sa evāha |
anāhitāgnir mṛtapatnīko vānena vidhinā saṃnyāsaṃ kuryād iti || 4.18 ||
atha śaunakaḥ |
atha yuvā suvāsā iti kāṣāyavāso gṛhṇāti | sakhā mā gopāyeti tridaṇḍaṃ gṛhṇāti | tac chaṃ yor āvṛṇīmaha iti yajñopavītaṃ gṛhṇāti | yad asya pāre rajasa iti śikyaṃ gṛhṇāti | oṃ iti pātram | yena devāḥ pavitreṇeti jalapavitram | yena devā jyotiṣordhvā udāyann iti kamaṇḍaluṃ gṛhṇāti || 4.19 ||
saptavyāhṛtibhiḥ pātram iti kātyāyanaḥ || 4.20 || vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yajñopavītam iti upavītaṃ samādāya haṃsaḥ śuciṣat udutyam citram tac cakṣuḥ namo mitrasyeti tridaṇḍādipañca aṅgam ity upasthāya samiddhamiti dvābhyām agniṃ visṛjya grāmasīmāntaṃ gatvā tridhā baddho vṛṣabho roravītīti tridaṇḍān ādadyāt | pavitracatuṣṭayatrikaṃ japitvā trīṇi padā vicakrama iti padatrayaṃ gatvā tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam ity upasthāya svayaṃbhūr iti japet | jale matsyādīnāṃ sthale paśumṛgasarīsṛpādīnāṃ sarvaprāṇinām abhayaṃ dattvā puṣpamūlaphalopādāna-drumacchedamadhumāṃsānṛtadānādānādīn varjayed iti || 4.21 ||
āha kātyāyanaḥ |
svakīyaṃ grāmaṃ bandhujanaṃ ca parityajyānyagrāmaṃ prāpya japadhyānaparāyaṇo bhavet | yācitāyācitābhyāṃ bhaikṣābhyāṃ vartayet | kāmakrodharāgadveṣādi varjayet | yāvadāyur evaṃ vartayan brahma saṃpadyate | na ca punar jāyata iti || 4.22 ||
śaunakaḥ |
athānavekṣamāṇo vācaṃ niyamya prācīm udīcīṃ vā diśam abhipravrajet | śamadamādi yena kuryāt | śravaṇamanananididhyāsanāni kartavyāni | praṇavam abhyasen nityam iti
|| 4.23 ||
iti śaunakīyasaṃnyāsavidhiḥ samāptaḥ || 4.24 || āha kapilaḥ |
anagnis tu muṇḍaḥ śikhī vāhorātram upoṣitaḥ snātvāpa evāpsu pāṇinā juhoti | āpo vai sarvā devatāḥ sarvābhyo devatābhyo juhomi svāheti | putraiṣaṇāyāś ca vittaiṣaṇāyāś ca lokaiṣaṇāyāś cāpy utthito ʼhaṃ svāheti | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayā | iti trir upāṃśūktvā trir madhyamena trir uccais triṣatyā hi devā iti vijñāyate | abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo mattaḥ svāhā | ity apāṃ pūrṇāñjaliṃ bhūmau ninīya daṇḍaṃ daṇḍān vā jalapavitraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ śikyaṃ pātram ity etāni samādāya putramitraśatrujñātīn agnisaṃnidhānaṃ tyajed iti || 4.25 ||
āha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
āhitāgnis tu prātar utthāya vrīhiyavābhyāṃ vaiśvānarīṃ pāthīkṛtiṃ tantumatīṃ vā nirvapet | athāhavanīyaṃ gatvā svargāya lokāya svāheti yajñapātrāṇi juhuyāt | ayaṃ te yonir ṛtviya ity ātmany agniṃ samāropya dakṣiṇāgniṃ gatvā ayam agniṃ purīṣya ity ulūkhalamusale juhuyāt | ayaṃ ta ity agniṃ samāropya gārhapatyaṃ gatvā ayam agnir gṛhapatir ity araṇiṃ juhuyāt | ayaṃ ta ity agniṃ samāropya vedimadhye sthitvā sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa iti japet | adbhyaḥ saṃbhūta ity upasthāya vedāham ity ātmānam upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | prācyāṃ diśi pitaraḥ sendrās tṛpyantām | trātāram indram ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | āgneyyāṃ diśi sāgnikāḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | agnir mūrdhety upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśi sayamāḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | asi yama ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | nairṛtyāṃ diśi sanirṛtayaḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | eṣa te nirṛta ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | pratīcyāṃ diśi savaruṇāḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | uruṃ hi rājā varuṇa ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | vāyavyāṃ diśi savāyavaḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | vāyo śatam ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | udīcyāṃ diśi sasomāḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | somo rājā ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | aiśānyāṃ diśi seśānāḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | īśānam ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | ūrdhvāyāṃ diśi sabrahmāṇaḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | brahma jajñānam ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | adharāyāṃ diśi sasarpāḥ pitaras tṛpyantām | namo ʼstu sarpebhya ity upasthāya pitṛbhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt ||4.26 ||
piṇḍaṃ datvā bandhūn anumodayatety uktvā darbhān anuśataṃ gṛhītvā ata ūrdhvaṃ yajñopavītam asītyādi śaunakīyoktaṃ draṣṭavyam || 4.27 ||
āhitāgner eva kapilo viśeṣam āha |
āhitāgniś cet prājāpatyeṣṭiṃ sarvasvadakṣiṇāṃ vidhivad dattvā ayaṃ te yonir ṛtviya ity ātmany agnīn samāropya vedimadhye sthitvā devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharaṃ pītavāsasaṃ kirīṭakeyūrakuṇḍaladharaṃ hāriṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ dhyātvā gurvanujñātaḥ praiṣamantram udāhared iti || 4.28 ||
jāmadagnyaḥ |
ṛtaṃ ceti dvādaśāham anutiṣṭhanhi mānavaḥ |
vedārthān vidhivac chrutvā saṃnyased anṛṇo dvijaḥ || 4.29 ||
keśaśmaśrūn vāpayitvā puṇye caiva jalāśaye |
vācaṃyamopavasati rātriṃ jāgarti parvaṇi || 4.30 ||
kṛtvā prajāpater iṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya yā te agne ya ity ṛcā || 4.31 ||
ahaṃ vṛkṣasyeti tiṣṭhed antarvedi dvijottamaḥ |
vedo ha vai brahmavṛkṣo brahmabhūyāya kalpate || 4.32 ||
satyaṃ vadety anuśāsti putraṃ vā śiṣyam eva vā |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit || 4.33 |
oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ suvaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayeti trir upāṃśv āha trir madhyamaṃ trir uccaiḥ |
triṣatyā hi devā iti vadanti brahmavādinaḥ || 4.34 ||
trīṇi padeti tridaṇḍaṃ vaiṇavaṃ dhārayed yatiḥ |
mānasaṃ vācikaṃ caiva kāyikaṃ ca vicakṣaṇaḥ || 4.35 ||
agnir devatā gāyatrī chanda iti mantreṇālābudārupātraṃ gṛhṇīyād bhikṣuko dvijaḥ |
jalapavitraṃ gṛhṇīyāt prājāpatyam iti ṛcā |
tena pūtaṃ pibet toyaṃ pavitropacito muniḥ || 4.36 ||
sarvabhūtebhyaḥ svāheti yo dadyād abhayāñjalim |
tasyaiva sarvabhūtebhyo na bhayaṃ jātu vidyate || 4.37 ||
grāmāt prācīm udīcīṃ vā diśaṃ gatvā tu vāgyataḥ |
snānaṃ tu vidhivat kṛtvā prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 4.38 ||
atha yajñopavītam apsu juhoti bhūḥ svāheti | tridaṇḍān ādatte sakhā me gopāyeti | pariśiṣṭe pūrvayajñopavītapratipattividhānavākyam asti || 4.39 ||
athāpatsaṃnyāsa ucyate || 4.40 || tatra jābāliḥ |
yady āturaḥ syad vācā vā manasā vāpi saṃnyaset |
eṣa panthā brahmaṇānām ihānuvihitas tv iti [JU 68–69] || 4.41 ||
āhāṅgirāḥ |
āturāṇāṃ viśeṣo ʼsti na vidhir naiva ca kriya |
praiṣamātras tu saṃnyāsa āturāṇāṃ vidhīyate iti || 4.42 ||
āha likhitaḥ |
vyāghrāgnidasyusarpādyaiḥ saṃpannamaraṇas tu yaḥ |
mahatā vyādhinā vāpi pīḍito yo mumūrṣati |
tadaiva kuryāt saṃnyāsaṃ vidhināvidhināpi vā || 4.43 ||
saṃnyastam iti yo brūyāt kaṇṭhasthaprāṇavān api |
tāritāḥ pitaras tena svayaṃ muktipathe sthitaḥ || 4.44 ||
mukto yaḥ prāṇasaṃdehāt kṛtasaṃnyasano dvijaḥ |
punar nivartate ye ca tena saṃvyavahāriṇaḥ |
sarve te karmacaṇḍālās teṣāṃ dṛṣṭā na niṣkṛtiḥ || 4.45 ||
saṃnyasya cāpadaṃ tīrṇaḥ sakāśe tu guror atha |
labdhvā mantrais tridaṇḍādīn gṛhītvā yogam ācaret || 4.46 ||
teṣāṃ nāśe punaḥ prāpte gṛhītvā mantravat svayam |
samudraṃ gaccha svāheti naṣṭān apsu vinikṣipet |
śikṣeta yatidharmāṃś ca likhitasya vaco yathā iti || 4.47 ||
āpannānāṃ tridhā saṃnyāsa uktaḥ vidhināvidhināpi vetyuktatvāt | kathaṃcit kartuṃ śakto vidhinā kuryāt | aśaktaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayeti vācā praiṣamātram uccārayet | tatrāpy aśaktaś cen manasaiva saṅgatyāgaṃ kuryāt | ity āpatsaṃnyāsavidhiḥ || 4.48 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye saṃnyāsaprayogo nāma
caturthaṃ parva || 4.49 ||
pañcamaṃ parva
mukhyakarmavidhiḥ
atha yatīnāṃ mukhyakarmocyate || 5.1 || tatrāhośanāḥ |
brahmacāry upakurvāṇo naiṣṭhiko ʼpy aparas tathā |
sadāro nyastadāraś ca vānaprastho dvidhā smṛtaḥ || 5.2 ||
bhikṣū dvau bhikṣusaṃnyāsī vedasaṃnyāsikas tathā |
bahudhā gṛhiṇo bhinnāḥ śālīnādaya eva te || 5.3 ||
dvidhaivāśramiṇāṃ bhedaś caturṇāṃ manur abravīt |
teṣāṃ yo bhikṣusaṃnyāsī tasyedaṃ śṛṇutādhunā || 5.4 ||
ihāmutropabhogeṣu viraktaḥ pravrajed dvijaḥ |
vaiśvānarīṃ nirūpyeṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām iti || 5.5 ||
evaṃ parivrājakasya dvidhā bhedo ʼbhyupagataḥ || 5.6 || kecit tu caturvidhatvaṃ varṇayanti |
caturvidhā bhikṣavas tu kuṭīcakabahūdakau |
haṃsaḥ paramahaṃsaś ca yo yaḥ paścāt sa uttamaḥ iti || 5.7 ||
etad api pāñcarātrasiddhaṃ sāṃkhyasiddhaṃ ca na vedasiddham iti kecid āhuḥ || 5.8 || āpastamba imaṃ pakṣaṃ pratiṣedhati |
sarvataḥ parimokṣam eke | satyānṛte sukhaduḥkhe vedān imaṃ lokam amuṃ ca parityajyātmānam anvicchet | buddhe kṣemaprāpaṇam | tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham | buddhe cet kṣemaprāpaṇam ihaiva na duḥkham upalabheta | etena paraṃ vyākhyātam iti [ApDh 2.21.12–17] || 5.9 ||
āhātriḥ |
śikhinaś ca śrutāḥ kecit kecin muṇḍās tu bhikṣukāḥ |
caturdhā bhikṣavo viprāḥ sarve caiva tridaṇḍinaḥ iti || 5.10 ||
āha parāśaraḥ |
tatra parivrājakāś caturvidhā bhavanti | kuṭīcakā bahūdakā haṃsāḥ paramahaṃsāś ceti | tatra kuṭīcakā nāma putrādibhiḥ kuṭīṃ kārayitvā kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamātsaryādīn parityajya vidhivat saṃnyāsaṃ kṛtvā tridaṇḍajalapavitrakāṣāyavastradhāriṇaḥ snānaśaucācamanajapasvādhyāyabrahmacaryadhyānatatparāḥ putrāder eva bhikṣākāle ʼnnaṃ yātrāmātram upayuñjānās tasyāṃ kuṭau nityaṃ vasanta ātmānaṃ mokṣayante | bahūdakā nāma tridaṇḍakamaṇḍalupakṣmapavitraśikyakāṣāyavastradhāriṇo vedāntārthāvabodhakāḥ sādhuvṛtteṣu brāhmaṇeṣu bhikṣācaryaṃ caranta ātmānaṃ mokṣayante | haṃsā nāma tridaṇḍajalapavitraśikyayajñopavītino gomūtragomayāhārā ekarātratrirātramāsopavāsapakṣopavāsakṛcchracāndrāyaṇasāntapanamahāsāntapana-parākatulāpuruṣādīni vratāni caranto godvijāḍhyeṣu puṇyeśu deśeṣu vasanta ātmānaṃ mokṣayante | paramahaṃsā nāma tridaṇḍajalapavitraśikyayajñopavītino ʼntarvāsobahirvāsadhāriṇo grāmaikarātravāsino nagaratīrthāvasatheṣu pañcarātrikā bhikṣākāle śīrṇaparṇapuṭaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇakulād eva bhaikṣaṃ gṛhītvāṣṭau grāsān eva bhuñjānāḥ saṃdhyopāsanatatparā nityaṃ vṛkṣamūle vasanta ātmānaṃ mokṣayanta iti
|| 5.11 ||
tatra manuḥ vedasaṃnyāsikānāmāha |
eṣa dharmo ʼnuśiṣṭo ʼnyo yatīnāṃ niyatātmanām |
vedasaṃnyāsakānāṃ tu karmayogaṃ nibodhata iti [MDh 6.86] || 5.12 ||
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtyeti trayaḥ ślokā asminn avasare paṭhyante keṣucit pradeśeṣu |
brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho ʼtha bhikṣukaḥ |
ete gṛhasthaprabhavāś catvāraḥ pṛthag āśramāḥ || 5.13 ||
sarve ʼpi kramaśas tv ete yathāśāstraṃ niṣevitāḥ |
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim || 5.14 ||
sarveṣām api caiteṣāṃ vedaśrutividhānataḥ |
gṛhastha ucyate śreṣṭhaḥ sa trīn etān bibharti hi || 5.15 ||
yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃsthitim |
tathaivāśramiṇaḥ sarve gṛhasthe yānti saṃsthitim || 5.16 ||
caturbhir etair api tu nityam āśramibhir dvijaiḥ |
daśalakṣaṇako dharmaḥ sevitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 5.17 ||
dhṛtiḥ kṣamā damo ʼsteyaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
hrīr vidyā satyam akrodho daśakaṃ dharmalakṣaṇam || 5.18 ||
daśa cihnāni dharmasya ye viprāḥ samadhīyate |
adhītya cānuvartante te yānti paramāṃ gatim || 5.19 ||
daśalakṣaṇakaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhanhi mānavaḥ |
vedāntān vidhivac chrutvā saṃnyased anṛṇo dvijaḥ || 5.20 ||
saṃnyasya sarvakarmāṇi karmadoṣān apānudan |
niyato vedam abhyasya putraiśvarye sukhaṃ vaset iti || 5.21 ||
[MDh 6.87–96]
evam eva kuṭīcaka iti kecid āhuḥ | daśa cihnāni dharmasyeti cihnagrahaṇam āśramadharmaṃ prati śeṣatvakhyāpanārtham | tataś ca sarvair āśramibhiḥ svaṃ svaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhadbhir dhṛtyādidaśadharmopetair bhavitavyam iti bhāvaḥ || 5.22 ||
tatra bhikṣusaṃnyāsinām āśramadharma ucyate | āha kratuḥ |
sāṃkhyaṃ yogo harer bhaktir apramādo virāgatā |
etat sāram ataḥ śeṣa āśramo dharma ucyate iti || 5.23 ||
etad uktaṃ bhavati | sāṃkhyaṃ tattvajñānam | yogo ʼṣṭāṅgaḥ ṣaḍaṅgo vā | harer bhaktir īśvaropāsanaṃ paramātmani snehaḥ | apramādaḥ sadā pratibuddhatāḥ | virāgatā viṣayeṣv asnehatā || 5.24 || tathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
paramātmani yo rakto virakto ʼparamātmani |
sarvaiṣaṇāvinirmuktaḥ sa bhaikṣaṃ bhoktum arhati iti || 5.25 ||
etat sāram ataḥ śeṣa iti | eṣa eva pradhānadharma āśramasya | śeṣas tu dharmaḥ saṃdhyāvandanādilakṣaṇa āśrama eva āśramadharmaḥ svarūpam eva | tanniṣṭha āśramaniṣṭha eva syāt nāśramadharmaniṣṭhaḥ | saṃdhyāvandanādayo vyāpārā āśramadharmāṅgatvam āpannā na svayam āśramadharmāḥ || 5.26 || tathāha dakṣaḥ |
saṃdhyāhīno ʼśucir nityam anarhaḥ sarvakarmasu |
yad anyat kurute karma na tasya phalabhāg bhavet iti || 5.27 ||
tatra sāṃkhyamucyate | āha yamaḥ |
pañcaviṃśatitattvajño yatra tatrāśrame rataḥ |
prakṛtijño vikārajñaḥ sa duḥkhāt parimucyate || 5.28 ||
mano buddhir ahaṅkāraḥ khānilāgnijalāni bhūḥ |
etāḥ prakṛtayas tv aṣṭau vikārāḥ ṣoḍaśāpare || 5.29 ||
śrotrākṣirasanāghrāṇaṃ tvak ca saṅkalpa eva ca |
śabdarūparasasparśagandhavākpāṇipāyavaḥ |
upasthapādāv iti ca vikārāḥ śoḍaśa smṛtāḥ || 5.30 ||
saṅkalpaḥ antaḥkaraṇānāṃ vṛttayaḥ |
carturviṃśakam ity etaj jñānam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
pañcaviṃśakam avyaktaṃ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ puruṣottamaḥ |
etaj jñātvā tu mucyante yatayaḥ śāntabuddhayaḥ || 5.31 ||
aśabdam arasasparśam arūpaṃ gandhavarjitam |
nirduḥkhaṃ susukhaṃ śuddhaṃ tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 5.32 ||
ajaṃ nirañjanaṃ śāntam avyaktaṃ jñānam akṣaram |
anādinidhanaṃ brahma tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam iti || 5.33 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
tad etat paramaṃ bhūtaṃ yasmād bhūtāny anekaśaḥ |
pṛthivyā iva bhāṇḍāni prabhavanti viśanti ca || 5.34 ||
tad etat paramaṃ jyotis tasyānye visphuliṅgavat |
vikṣiptaṃ sūryacandrāgnividyuttārāgrahādiṣu || 5.35 ||
sa eṣa paramānando yāś cānyāḥ sukhavipruṣaḥ |
pibanti puṇyakarmāṇo brahmendrapitṛmānavāḥ || 5.36 ||
nānātvaikatvabhāvena sarvabhāvasthitaṃ prabhum |
idaṃ tu paramaikatvaṃ parā sattā parā gatiḥ || 5.37 ||
ity upakramyānte ca |
ya evaṃ brahma jānāti saṃsārāt sa vimucyate iti || 5.38 ||
āha śaunakaḥ |
pṛthivy āpas tathā tejo vāyur ākāśa eva ca |
pañcaitāni vijānīyān mahābhūtāni paṇḍitaḥ || 5.39 ||
śrotraṃ cakṣuḥ sparśanaṃ ca rasanaṃ ghrāṇam eva ca |
buddhīndriyāṇi jānīyāt pañcaivāsmiñ charīrake || 5.40 ||
mano buddhis tathaivātmā hy avyaktaṃ ca tathāparam |
indriyebhyaḥ parāṇy āhuś catvāri kathitāni tu || 5.41 ||
śabdaṃ rūpaṃ tathā sparśaṃ rasaṃ gandhaṃ tathaiva ca |
indriyārthān vijānīyāt pañcaiva satataṃ budhaḥ || 5.42 ||
hastapādāv upasthaṃ ca jihvā pāyus tathaiva ca |
karmendriyāṇi pañcaiva nityam asmiñ charīrake || 5.43 ||
carturviṃśatir etāni tattvāni kathitāni tu |
matvātmānaṃ tadrahitaṃ puruṣaṃ pañcaviṃśakam || 5.44 ||
yaj jñātvā tu vimucyante yatayaḥ śāntabuddhayaḥ |
etat tat paramaṃ guhyam etad akṣaram uttamam || 5.45 ||
aśabdam arasasparśam ityādi yāmyapāṭha eva | evam anyatrāpītihāsapurāṇeṣu mānavādiṣu vedānteṣu ca sāṃkhyaśabdavācyaṃ tattvajñānam anveṣaṇīyam || 5.46 ||
atha jñānatattvasya yoga ucyate | so ʼṣṭāṅga uktaḥ pātañjale |
yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayo ʼṣṭāṅgāni | tatra ahiṃsāsatyāsteyabrahmacaryāparigrahā yamāḥ | śaucasaṃtoṣatapaḥsvādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāḥ | sthirasukham āsanam | prāṇagativicchedaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ | svaviṣayāsaṃprayoge cittasvarūpānukāra evendriyāṇāṃ pratyāhāraḥ | deśabandhaś cittasya dhāraṇā | tatra pratyayaikatānatā dhyānam | tad evārthamātranirbhāsaṃ svarūpaśūnyam iva samādhiḥ
|| 5.47 || [Ys 2.29—3.3]
eṣām aṣṭānām aṅgānāṃ pratyāhārādīni svarūpāṅgāni devadattasyeva hastādīni itarāṇi sādhanatvenāṅgāni | teṣām antaraṅgavirodhe ʼntaraṅgāṇy eva pālanīyāni || 5.48 ||
tathā ca bhagavān vyāsaḥ |
yamān yateta satataṃ bādhitvāpītarān budhaḥ |
yamān pataty akurvāṇo niyamān kevalaṃ bhajan iti || 5.49 ||
tānīmāny aṅgāni vyākhyāyante smṛtiṣu nānāprakāreṇa | tatra tāvat kratuḥ |
yogāṅgair vā yamādibhiḥ [see 6.23] |
ity uktvāha |
dhṛtiḥ kṣamā damo ʼsteyaṃ śaucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
hrīr vidyā satyam akrodha etad dharmasya lakṣaṇam || 5.50 ||
ahiṃsā samatāsteyaṃ kṣamādamaśamādayaḥ |
tuṣṭir adrohatākalkaṃ nirvairatvaṃ yamāḥ smṛtāḥ || 5.51 ||
vikalpo dambha ālasyaṃ saṅgaḥ karmaphalaṃ bhayam |
parigraho mamatvaṃ ca tyāgaś copayamāḥ smṛtāḥ || 5.52 ||
eṣāṃ tyāgas tyāga ity arthaḥ |
kāmarāgadvaṃdvanidrāmohakṣutpāpacetasām |
dveṣāhaṅkāralobhānāṃ vijayāḥ saṃyamāḥ smṛtāḥ || 5.53 ||
saṃnatiḥ śīlam āstikyaṃ yogo vṛttaṃ śrutaṃ kriya |
audāsīnyaṃ mṛdu sthairyaṃ vairāgyaṃ ca parā yamāḥ || 5.54 ||
aprārthanam asaṃkalpam asparśanam adarśanam |
dhyānaṃ prasādamādhuryaṃ maunaṃ saṃniyamān viduḥ || 5.55 ||
śaunakaḥ |
daśa vratāni sarveṣāṃ yatīnāṃ vihitāni vai |
ahiṃsā satyam asteyaṃ brahmacaryāparigrahau || 5.56 ||
akrodho guruśuśrūṣā śaucaṃ durvṛttivarjanam |
vāṅmanaḥkāyaceṣṭāsu pramādasya ca varjanam iti || 5.57 ||
āha bodhāyanaḥ |
athaitāni vratāni bhavanti | ahiṃsā satyam asteyaṃ maithunasya ca varjanaṃ tyāga iti | pañcaivopavratāni bhavanti | akrodho guruśuśrūṣāpramādaḥ śaucam āhāraśuddhiś ceti [BDh 2.18.2–3] || 5.58 ||
yamaḥ |
athādityo yamo rājā yatīnāṃ bhāvitātmanām |
jagāda vihitān dharmān vedasaṃnyāsinām api || 5.59 ||
ahiṃsā satyam akrodho brahmacaryaṃ tapaḥ śrutam |
asteyāvyabhicārau ca dharmo daśavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 5.60 ||
tapaso vāṅmanaḥkāyabhedena bhinnatvād daśavidhatvam |
saṃtoṣo guruśuśrūṣā hy apramādaḥ kṣamā dayā |
maunam āhāraśuddhiś ca śaucam aṣṭau vratāni ca || 5.61 ||
nanu hiṃsābhedād ahiṃsāpi bhidyate | hiṃsā ca daśavidhā | tathā hi udvegakaraṇaṃ santāpakaraṇaṃ rujākaraṇaṃ śoṇitotpādanaṃ paiśunyaṃ sukhāpanayanam atikramaḥ saṅkoco hitapratiṣedho vadha iti || 5.62 || aprasiddhāṃś ca bhedān vṛddhajābālir āha |
hantā caivānumantā ca viśastā krayavikrayī |
saṃskartā copakartā ca khādakaś cāṣṭa ghātakāḥ iti || 5.63 ||
satyaṃ vāyur āha |
na dharmayuktam anṛtaṃ hinastīti manīṣiṇaḥ |
tathāpi tan na vaktavyaṃ prasaṅgo ʼpy eṣa dāruṇaḥ || 5.64 ||
asteyaṃ ca sa evāha |
asadvādo na kartavyo yatinā dharmalipsunā |
na caivāpadgatenāpi steyaṃ kāryaṃ kadācana || 5.65 ||
brahmacaryabhedaṃ bṛhaspatir āha |
smaraṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ keliḥ prekṣaṇaṃ guhyabhāṣaṇam |
saṃkalpo ʼdhyavasāyaś ca kriyānivṛttir eva ca || 5.66 ||
etan maithunam aṣṭāṅgaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
viparītaṃ brahmacaryam etad evāṣṭalakṣaṇam || 5.67 ||
aparigrahaṃ likhita āha |
saṃskṛtāṃ na vaded vāṇīṃ bālavan mūkavac caret |
na dravyasaṃcayaṃ kuryān mātrām apy anyakālikām || 5.68 ||
śaucam upari vyākhyāsyate gargeṇa [see 6.5] || 5.69 || svādhyāyaṃ yama āhaḥ |
japyenāpi nayet kālaṃ dhyānaśrānto yatiḥ sadā |
dhyānena japaviśrāntaḥ punar dhyāyet punar japet || 5.70 ||
catvāraḥ pākayajñās tu vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 5.71 ||
vidhiyajñāj japo yajño viśiṣṭo daśabhir guṇaiḥ |
upāṃśu syāc chataguṇaḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ iti || 5.72 ||
manuḥ |
adhiyajñaṃ brahma japed ādhidaivikam eva ca |
ādhyātmikaṃ ca satataṃ vedāntābhihitaṃ ca yat || 5.73 || [MDh 6.83] vedāntābhihitaṃ praṇavaḥ | āpastambaḥ |
svādhyāya evotsṛjamāno vācam iti || 5.74 || [ApDh 2.21.10]
bodhāyanaḥ |
sāyaṃprātar agnihotramantrāñ japet [BDh 2.18.20] | yo ʼgnihotrī bhūtvā pravrajati tasyāyaṃ vidhiḥ || 5.75 ||
īśvarapraṇidhānaṃ kapila āha |
prātaḥsnānaṃ japo maunaṃ nityam ekāntaśīlatā |
namaskāropavāsau ca bhaktir viṣṇau tathā gurau || 5.76 ||
āstikyaṃ brahmasaṃsparśaḥ prāṇāyāmaratis tathā |
traikālyam arcanaṃ viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ muktisādhanam || 5.77 ||
atriḥ |
traikālyam arcanaṃ viṣṇor devānāṃ ca tadātmanām |
namaskārārcanādīni kuryān nānyasya kasyacit iti || 5.78 ||
āha kratuḥ |
īśvaro bhagavān viṣṇuḥ paramātmā mahān ajaḥ |
śāstā carācarasyaiko yatīnāṃ paramā gatiḥ || 5.79 ||
punaḥ sa evāha |
dhyāyate ʼrcayate yo ʼnyaṃ viṣṇuliṅgam upāśritaḥ |
kalpakoṭiśataiś cāpi gatis tasya na vidyate || 5.80 ||
athāpi sa evāha |
pūjāṃ puruṣasūktena vedaguhyastavaiḥ stutim |
om ity ekākṣaraṃ dhyāyed adhiyajñaṃ tathā japet |
trikaṃ puruṣasūktaṃ ca catuṣkaṃ prāṇasaṃyamaiḥ || 5.81 ||
trikaṃ praṇavavyāhṛtigāyatryaḥ | tā eva saśiraskāś catuṣkam | āsanaṃ śaṅkha āha |
śūnye gṛhe devagṛhe guhāyāṃ girigahvare |
yatra vā ramate buddhis tatrāsīta prasannadhīḥ || 5.82 ||
yogaśāstroktamārgeṇa kṛtāsanaparigrahaḥ |
yogaṃ yuñjīta satataṃ saṃdhyāsu tu viśeṣataḥ || 5.83 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
tato nivātake ramye nimnonnatavivarjite |
kuśājinasucelānām upary upari vinyaset || 5.84 ||
padmāsanaṃ tu tatraiva kṛtvāṅgāni samaṃ nayet |
prāṇāyāmāṃs tataḥ kuryād yāvat kālaṃ samāhitaḥ || 5.85 ||
prāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śaṅkha āha |
prāṇāyāmair dahed doṣān dhāraṇābhiś ca kilbiṣān |
pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānaiśvarān guṇān || 5.86 ||
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate || 5.87 ||
saṃyamaś cendriyāṇāṃ hi pratyāhāraḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
manaḥsaṃyamanaṃ tajjñair dhāraṇeti nigadyate || 5.88 ||
brahmaṇi dhyānayogena devadevasya darśanam |
dhyānaṃ proktaṃ pravakṣyāmi dhyānayogam ataḥ śubham || 5.89 ||
dhyānaṃ samādhiḥ | dhyānayogas tu dhyānam | tadāha |
hṛdisthā devatāḥ sarvā hṛdi prāṇāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
hṛdi jyotīṃṣi sūryaś ca hṛdi sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 5.90 ||
svadeham araṇiṃ kṛtvā praṇavaṃ cottarāraṇim |
dhyānanirmathanābhyāsād viṣṇuṃ paśyed dhṛdi sthitam || 5.91 ||
oṃkāreṇa brahma dhyāyed anyāṃs tyaktvā śabdādīn ity abhiprāyaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ |
om ity ātmānaṃ yuñjīteti || 5.92 || [MNU 540]
tathā |
anyā vāco vimuñcatha | [MuU 2.2.5]
ityuktvāha |
om ity eva dhyāyatātmānam iti | [MuU 2.2.6]
nānudhyāyed bahūñ chabdān | [BaU 4.4.21]
iti ca || 5.93 || āha yamaḥ |
dve brahmaṇī veditavye śabdabrahma paraṃ ca yat |
śabdabrahmaṇi niṣṇātaḥ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 5.94 ||
śabdabrahma praṇavaḥ | yamaḥ śaṅkhaś ca |
ravimadhye sthitaḥ somas tasya madhye hutāśanaḥ |
tejomadhye sthitaṃ tattvaṃ tattvamadhye sthito ʼcyutaḥ || 5.95 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
prasīdati mano yāvat tāvac cittaṃ niyamya tu |
cintayed dhṛdi madhye tu pradīptaṃ sūryamaṇḍalam || 5.96 ||
tasya madhye sthitaṃ somam agniṃ brahma mahacchikham |
sarvavighnapraśāntyarthaṃ hṛtpadme devam acyutam || 5.97 ||
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ caturbāhum anusmaret |
tataḥ sa niṣkalaṃ dhyāyed brahmapūrvam atandritaḥ || 5.98 ||
brahmapūrvaṃ praṇavapūrvaṃ nārāyaṇam ity arthaḥ |
sarvaṃ sarvātmakaṃ tattvaṃ viṣṇvākhyaṃ paramaṃ padam |
punaḥ punaḥ svacittasthaṃ nirmalaṃ yan nirāmayam || 5.99 ||
tad brahma cāham evāsmi sarvagaṃ tv ātmanaḥ param |
nirasya viṣayāsaktiṃ saṃnirudhya mano hṛdi || 5.100 ||
yadā yasya manobhāvaṃ tad evaṃ paramaṃ padam |
vīkṣitāṃ buddhim ādadyāt punar apy ātmanīśvare || 5.101 ||
na nirvedaṃ munir gacchen nirundhyād eva mānasam |
tāvad eva niroddhavyaṃ hṛdi yāvat kṣayaṃ gatam || 5.102 ||
etaj jñānaṃ ca dhyānaṃ ca śeṣo ʼnyo granthavistaraḥ |
indriyeṣu niruddheṣu manasi spandavarjite |
ātmā prakāśate śuddho bodhākāro nirākulaḥ iti || 5.103 ||
likhitaḥ |
vicārya ca purāṇārthān vedāntān bhaktim āsthitaḥ |
viṣṇuṃ sadā hṛdi dhyāyet sākāraṃ niṣkalaṃ tu vā || 5.104 ||
idaṃ tu sarvadharmāṇāṃ yad yogenātmadarśanam |
śreṣṭham etad virodhy anyat tyajed āvaśyakād ṛte || 5.105 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṣamā tīrthaṃ damas tīrthaṃ tīrtham indriyanigrahaḥ
sarvabhūtadayā tīrthaṃ dhyānatīrtham anuttamam || 5.106 ||
etāni pañca tīrthāni satyaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu sarvadā |
dehe tiṣṭhanti sarvatra teṣu snānaṃ samācaret || 5.107 ||
na tathā puṣkare snāto gaṅgāyāṃ kurujāṅgale |
mucyate puruṣaḥ pāpair yathā snātaḥ kṣamādiṣu || 5.108 ||
nigṛhītendriyagrāmo yatra yatra vasen naraḥ |
tatra tatra kurukṣetraṃ naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā || 5.109 ||
idaṃ tīrtham idaṃ neti ye narā bhedavādinaḥ |
teṣāṃ vidhīyate tīrthagamanaṃ tatphalaṃ ca yat || 5.110 ||
sarvaṃ brahmeti yo vetti nātīrthaṃ tasya kiṃcana |
jāgratsvapnasuṣupteṣu brahmaṇy eva sa vartate || 5.111 ||
sa eva paramo dharmo ye tv anye tasya bindavaḥ |
yajñaṃ dānaṃ tapo vedās tīrtharūpeṇa saṃsthitāḥ || 5.112 ||
yogaṃ yogāṅgāni ca prāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhīn kratuḥ stauti |
ratnair brahmāṇḍam āpūrya vedavidbhyo nivedite |
kṣayiṣṇu tatphalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ na viṣṇoḥ smaraṇaṃ sakṛt || 5.113 ||
pañcāgnijalamadhyasthāḥ khasthāś caivāgnidhūmapāḥ |
prāṇāyāmasthitasyaite kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 5.114 ||
tvakpatrapuṣpaśākāmbuphalamūlānilādanaiḥ |
dvādaśābdais tattvaśuddhiḥ pratyāhāreṇa vatsarāt || 5.115 ||
sustheyaṃ śakticakrāsibāṇāśrayahitaiṣiṇā |
dhāraṇāyāṃ tu yogasya duḥstheyam akṛtātmabhiḥ || 5.116 ||
śakyate ʼgnau hime yuddhe svādhyāye tapasi vrate |
pāpadhvaṃsiny avasthātuṃ na sthāne kṣaṇam apy alam || 5.117 ||
kuṣṭhajvaragaravyādhīn marṣayantīha mānavāḥ |
ye janā duḥsahā loke yoganiścalakārakāḥ || 5.118 ||
tyaktabandhasya śuddhasya samādhisthasya yoginaḥ |
virajākāśakalpasya sā kāṣṭhā vaiṣṇavī gatiḥ || 5.119 ||
ravīnduvahnidyutayaḥ śuddhasphaṭikanirmalāḥ |
taranti tamasaḥ pāraṃ yogāṅgais toṣite harau || 5.120 ||
manovāgdehajān doṣān ajñānotthān pramādataḥ |
sarvān dahati yogāgnis tūlarāśim ivānalaḥ |
bhaktiyukto hariṃ paśyed yogadīpikayā sadā || 5.121 ||
yogāvasānaṃ hārīta āha |
ātmalābhasukhaṃ yāvat tāvad dhyānam udāhṛtam |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ karma tadūrdhvaṃ ca samācaret || 5.122 ||
niṣkaladhyānāśaktau dhyānaṃ bṛhaspatir āha |
brahmaṇy eva nirākāre dhyānaṃ kartum aśaknuvan |
prāṇāyāmena saṃśuddhas tanmūrtidhyānam ācaret || 5.123 ||
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate || 5.124 ||
prāṇāyāmair dahed doṣān manovākkāyasaṃbhavān |
pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānīśvarān guṇān || 5.125 ||
dhmāyamānaṃ yathā bhraśyed dhātūnāṃ saṃbhṛtaṃ malam |
tathendriyakṛto doṣaḥ prāṇāyāmena dahyate || 5.126 ||
ādhānaṃ rodham utsargaṃ vāyos tris triḥ samabhyaset |
brahmāṇaṃ keśavaṃ śaṃbhuṃ dhyāyan mucyeta bandhanāt || 5.127 ||
raktaṃ pitāmahaṃ dhyāyed viṣṇuṃ nīlotpalaprabham |
śvetaṃ trilocanaṃ śaṃbhuṃ saṃsārārṇavatāraṇam || 5.128 ||
yogābhyāsaparo nityam evaṃ yo vartate yatiḥ |
prāptavairāgyasarvasvaḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 5.129 ||
vāsudevaḥ paraṃ brahma paramātmā jaganmayaḥ |
tasyaiva mūrtayas tv etā bhidyante hy ākhyayā tridhā || 5.130 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ jagadyoniṃ sarvalokeśvaraṃ harim |
cintayed anvahaṃ bhaktyā sarvakāraṇakāraṇam || 5.131 ||
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ saumyaṃ caturbāhuṃ kirīṭinam |
śrīvatsakaustubhoraskaṃ divyaratnavibhūṣitam || 5.132 ||
pītāmbaram udārāṅgaṃ prasannendunibhānanam |
śyāmalaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ sphuranmakarakuṇḍalam || 5.133 ||
saṃtāpanāśanaṃ sadyaḥ kālamegham ivoditam |
sarvatejomayaṃ puṇyaṃ śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇārthinām || 5.134 ||
cintayet puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ nārāyaṇam anāmayam |
manasā ṣoḍaśaiḥ samyag upacāraiḥ prayatnataḥ iti || 5.135 ||
āha dattātreyaḥ |
aṣṭādaśopacāreṇa bhagavān puruṣottamaḥ |
hṛdaye ʼgnau tathāditye pūjyate ca mumukṣubhiḥ || 5.136 ||
palāśaiḥ padmakusumair arcanaṃ śasyate hareḥ |
evam abhyarcya deveśaṃ nārāyaṇam atandritaḥ || 5.137 ||
tadrūpaṃ cintayed yogī praṇavena samāhitaḥ |
sākāraṃ vā yathāyogaṃ nityaṃ manasi dhārayet || 5.138 ||
ādityavarṇaṃ puruṣaṃ puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇam |
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇiṃ pītanirmalavāsasam || 5.139 ||
kirīṭacārukeyūrakaṭakādivibhūṣitam |
śrīvatsavakṣasaṃ śrīśaṃ kaustubhodbhrājivakṣasam || 5.140 ||
śyāmalaṃ vā hṛṣīkeśaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣam acyutam |
hṛtpadmakarṇikāmadhye sthitam āsīnam eva vā || 5.141 ||
evaṃ saṃcintayed devaṃ sarvalokamaheśvaram |
samastakleśahantāraṃ kāryakāraṇasaṃsthitam || 5.142 ||
yogābhyāsaphalaṃ śaṅkha āha |
sarveṣu yogaśāstreṣu vedānteṣu ca niṣṭhitaḥ |
sadā tad bhāvayed yogī tais tair brahma viśeṣaṇaiḥ || 5.143 ||
punaḥ punar anirviṇṇo jyotis tal labhate dhruvam |
saumyam ānandam advaitaṃ bahuśas tatprakāśitam |
tanmayatvena labdhvāsau paraṃ nirvāṇam ṛcchati || 5.144 ||
āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ijyācāradamāhiṃsādānasvādhyāyakarmaṇām |
ayaṃ tu paramo dharmo yad yogenātmadarśanam || 5.145 ||
āha viśvāmitraḥ |
evaṃ hi dhyāyatas tasya brahmaniṣṭhasya yoginaḥ |
sāṃkhyam ānandam advaitaṃ paraṃ brahma prakāśate || 5.146 ||
prakāśitena tattvena muktir uktā manīṣiṇām |
nānyena yogahīnena karmahīnena labhyate || 5.147 ||
tasmāt karma ca kartavyaṃ yathākālaṃ dine dine |
jñānayuktaṃ ca tat karma kartavyaṃ mokṣakāṅkṣibhiḥ || 5.148 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
evaṃ bhāvayatas tasya vyapaity ajñānavāsanā |
jñānadīpodayaś ca syāt tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā || 5.149 ||
kuryāc chuddhim aśuddhasya sarveṇānena cetasaḥ |
tasmiṃs tu vimalībhūte layo brahmaṇi nānyathā || 5.150 ||
muktasvarūpaṃ medhātithir āha |
vimuktaḥ param apyeti saritaḥ sāgaraṃ yathā |
tadākhyas tanmayo bhūtvā sa punar neha jāyate || 5.151 ||
sādhibhūtādhidaivasya sādhiyajñasya caiva hi |
ātmā bhavati sarvasya tathaivādhyātmikasya ca || 5.152 ||
sa brahmā sa śivo viṣṇuḥ śakraḥ so ʼharpatir yamaḥ |
hutāśo nirṛtir vāyur īśānaḥ sarvabhūtarāṭ || 5.153 ||
vedā yajñās tathā devāḥ paśavo dakṣiṇādayaḥ |
śraddhādayo haviḥ kālaḥ kriyā kartā dhṛtir vidhiḥ |
sarvātmā sarvabhūteśaḥ sarvasyāyatanaṃ ca saḥ iti || 5.154 ||
sāṃkhyayogaṃ tatphalaṃ ca kratur āha |
karmendriyāṇi manasā buddhiṃ jñānena śodhayet |
vṛttiṃ saṃśodhanenaiva vidyāvidye guror girā || 5.155 ||
rājñī buddhir guṇo ʼmātyaḥ pratihāro mano matam |
senāpatim ahaṅkāraṃ kārayed abhimānataḥ || 5.156 ||
daśagrāmādhipatayas teṣāṃ saṃkalpanaṃ vinā |
tribhir āvaraṇair yuktaṃ pāñcabhūtaṃ mahat puram || 5.157 ||
yogaiśvaryāsanārūḍho rājeva puruṣaḥ sthitaḥ |
paśyate guṇavaiṣamyaṃ sarvaṃ jñātvā tu sākṣivat || 5.158 ||
bhāvanākalpanācintābhayavikṣepavarjitaḥ |
saṃbandham arthadharmāṃś ca deśajātiguṇāśrayān || 5.159 ||
sarvabhāvān parityajya sad asmīty eva bhāvayet |
divārātrau sadaikākī bhūtvā manasi cintayet |
tato rāgakṣayād ātmā pare viśati tatkṣaṇāt iti || 5.160 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye mukhyakarmavidhirnāma
pañcamaṃ parva || 5.161 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ parva
ahorātrakriyā
atha yatīnām ahorātrakriyocyate | tatrānuktam apekṣitaṃ brahmacāridharmād gṛhītavyam |
itareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi | [GDh 3.10]
ity anujñānāt | tatrāpy asaṃbhave gṛhasthadharmebhyo gṛhītavyam || 6.1 || tathā ca bhagavān vyāsaḥ |
sarvāśramāṇāṃ dharmāḥ syur ye ʼviruddhāḥ parasparam |
sarvāśramāṇāṃ te sarve sādhāraṇyam avāpnuyuḥ iti || 6.2 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
uṣaḥkāle samutthāya śaucaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ |
dantakāṣṭham athācamya parvavarjam atandritaḥ || 6.3 ||
kṛtasnāno japet tāvad yāvad ādityadarśanam |
uttiṣṭha brahmaṇetyādir utthāne mantra īritaḥ || 6.4 ||
gargaḥ |
atha bhikṣūṇāṃ niyamasaṃyamaśaucaprāyaścittaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tatra pūrvaṃ mūtraśaucam | mṛttikayā parvatrayapūraṇaṃ kṛtvā liṅgaṃ catasṛbhiḥ prakṣālayet savyahastaṃ dvādaśabhiḥ dvau hastāv aṣṭābhir mṛttikābhiḥ | evaṃ yathoktaṃ liṅgamūtraśaucaṃ kṛtvā paścād gudaśaucam ācaret | prathamā prasṛtir ekā dātavyā dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā ca tadardhārdhena | evaṃ yathoktakrameṇa viṃśatyā mṛttikābhir gudaśaucaṃ kṛtvā dviguṇena savyahastaṃ prakṣālayet dvau hastāv aṣṭāviṃśatyā | mṛttikātrayeṇa pādau prakṣālya hastau cācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punarācāmet
|| 6.5 ||
mṛttikābhāve vālukābhir yadā śaucaṃ karoti tadā dviguṇaṃ kuryāt | svasthāvastho bhikṣuḥ śaucaśaithilyaṃ na kuryāt | śaucaśaithilye pratyavāyo bhavati | tatpratyavāyanirākaraṇāya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet || 6.6 ||
brāhme muhūrte utthāya vasur aṇvo vibhur iti |
anena paramātmānaṃ dhyāyed ahar ahar hṛdi || 6.7 ||
tata āvaśyakaṃ kṛtvā pūrvam ādāya mṛttikām |
śaucaṃ caturguṇaṃ kuryād vihitād gṛhamedhinaḥ |
ādadānas tu vai paścāt sacelo jalam āviśet || 6.8 ||
devalaḥ |
śodhayec ca caturliṅgam aṅgulināṃ triparvakam |
yathā mṛttikayā pūrṇaṃ sā saṃkhyaikā mṛdaḥ smṛtā || 6.9 ||
hastaṃ dvādaśabhiḥ savyam aṣṭābhiḥ saṃhatau karau |
gudaśaucaṃ tataḥ kuryād viṃśatis tu mṛdo gude || 6.10 ||
prathamā prasṛtir deyā uttare tu tadardhake |
catvāriṃśat kare savye karayoḥ sāṣṭaviṃśatiḥ || 6.11 ||
pādayoś ca trir ekasmin dviguṇaṃ vālukā yadi |
dvir ācamya trir ācamya prāṇān apy ācamet punaḥ iti || 6.12 ||
sa evāha |
viṇmūtrakaraṇāt pūrvam ādadyān mṛttikāṃ sadā |
ādadānas tu vai paścāt sacelo jalam āviśet || 6.13 ||
āha jamadagniḥ |
anyad eva diva śaucam anyad rātrau vidhīyate |
anyathā bādhamāneṣu svasthādiṣu yathoditam || 6.14 ||
yathoditaṃ diva śaucam ardhaṃ rātrau vidhīyate |
āturasya tadardhaṃ syāt tadardhaṃ pathi gacchataḥ || 6.15 ||
devalaḥ |
eko dvau vā trayo mūtre purīṣe cātha vā punaḥ |
prāṇāyāmān yatiḥ kuryād deśakālādyapekṣayā || 6.16 ||
sa evāha |
dhyāne daivatapūjāyāṃ bhojane dantadhāvane |
avaśyakārye snāne ca ṣaṭsu maunaṃ vidhīyate || 6.17 ||
vāyuḥ |
ākarṇaprāvṛtaśirā apsu yānagatas tathā |
adhautapādas tiṣṭhan vā jalpan muktaśikho ʼpi vā |
yajñopavītena vinā svācānto ʼpy aśucir bhavet || 6.18 ||
devalaḥ |
viriktavāntaviṇmūtrakaraṇeṣv atha saṃdhyayoḥ |
snātvāntarāntarādityaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret || 6.19 ||
ekena praṇavenaiva prāṇāyāmā niśi smṛtāḥ |
madhyāhnakāle naikena kāṣṭhastho nācamed yatiḥ || 6.20 ||
gargaḥ |
brahmacārī yatiś caiva antarliṅgaṃ na śodhayet |
antarliṅgasya śaucena tayoś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 6.21 ||
tato dantadhāvanaṃ kṛtvā snānaṃ kuryāt | kratur āha |
yatir yogī brahmacārī śatāyuḥ satyavāg vratī |
satī ca dānaśīlaś ca snātāḥ śuddhāś ca te sadā || 6.22 ||
prākśaucaṃ naiva ca snāyād brāhmaṃ vā snānam iṣyate |
vṛddhāturāṇām uṣṇābhir yogāṅgair vā yamādibhiḥ |
abliṅgair vāruṇaṃ snānaṃ kuryān mantraiḥ sadā yatiḥ || 6.23 ||
dakṣaḥ |
uṣaḥkāle tu saṃprāpte śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi |
tataḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta dantadhāvanapūrvakam || 6.24 ||
atyantamalinaḥ kāyo navadvārasamanvitaḥ |
sravaty eva divārātraṃ prātaḥsnānaṃ viśodhanam || 6.25 ||
upary uṣasi yat snānaṃ saṃdhyāyām udite ʼpi vā |
prājāpatyena tat tulyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 6.26 ||
prātar utthāya yo vipraḥ saṃdhyāsnānaṃ samācaret |
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir varṣair vyapohati || 6.27 ||
dhyāyen nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ snānādiṣu ca karmasu |
prāyaścittaṃ hi sarvasya duṣkṛtasyeti vai śrutiḥ || 6.28 ||
guṇā daśa snānaparasya sādho
rūpaṃ ca tejaś ca balaṃ ca śaucam |
āyuṣyam ārogyam alolupatvaṃ
duḥsvapnanāśaś ca tapaś ca medhā || 6.29 ||
kapilaḥ |
prātaḥ snāne cāsamartho nābher adhaḥ prakṣālya jalārdreṇa karpaṭena saśiraskam aṅgam amalaṃ kṛtvāntarvāso viparidhāya saṃdhyām upāsīta | nityaṃ madhyāhnād arvāg ūrdhvaṃ vā snāyāt | athācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet || 6.30 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
prātaḥsnāne tv aśaktaś cet kāpilasnānam ācaret |
tasminn apy asamarthaś cen mantrasnānaṃ samācaret || 6.31 ||
aṅgirāḥ |
snānakāle tu saṃprāpte vastrārdhaṃ paridhāpayet |
tasyārdhaṃ japakāle tu kaṭiveṣṭanaśāṭikām || 6.32 ||
evaṃ savanatraye snānaṃ kuryāt | mārjanamantrāḥ paṭhyante ava te heḍa ud uttamaṃ dadhikrāvṇa āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ | hiraṇyavarṇā iti catasraḥ pavamānānuvākaś ca mārjane vihitāḥ || 6.33 ||
atha mantrācamanam | tatra kratuḥ |
catuṣkeṇāpa ācamya drupadādyaghamarṣaṇaiḥ |
mārjayitvā ca pūtādbhiḥ pitṛdevāṃś ca tarpayet iti || 6.34 ||
asyārthaḥ catuṣkeṇaiva mantreṇa catuṣkṛtvaḥ pibed apa iti | manuḥ |
daśabhiḥ praṇavair yuktāṃ saptavyāhṛtipūrvikām |
gāyatrīṃ śirasopetām āhuḥ pāpapraṇāśinīm iti || 6.35 ||
ayaṃ vedaguhyo ʼnuvākaś catuṣkaḥ | praṇavavyāhṛtigāyatryaḥ saśiraskāś catuṣka ity eke | devalaḥ |
tripadety anayaivarcā sāghamarṣaṇasūktayā |
prokṣyāṅgaṃ culukair viṣṇus tarpyo dvādaśanāmabhiḥ || 6.36 ||
praṇavādyair namo ʼntais tu pratimantraṃ yatiḥ śuciḥ |
tarpayed ekahastena pūrvaṃ dvādaśanāmabhiḥ || 6.37 ||
oṃ keśavāya nama ityādi | atha devatarpaṇaṃ pitṛtarpaṇaṃ ca |
gośṛṅgamātram uddhṛtyāñjalinā tarpayet surān |
pitṝṃś ca tarpayet paścāt pūrvavat sukṛtāñjaliḥ || 6.38 ||
paitṛkaṃ devatīrthena tathā yajñopavītinā |
ante mahar namo vācyaṃ tarpayāmīti coccaret iti || 6.39 ||
oṃ bhūs tarpayāmi | oṃ bhuvas tarpayāmi | oṃ suvas tarpayāmi | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsuvarmaharnamas tarpayāmīti | oṃ bhūḥ svadhom | oṃ bhuvaḥ svadhom | oṃ suvaḥ svadhom | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥsuvarmaharnamaḥ svadhom | svadhāntam etat || 6.40 || snānaṃ samāpya trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā mantrācamanādi saṃdhyopāsanaṃ pūrvavat | ācamanādiṣu pañcasu jalapavitrasya saṃbandhaḥ | vidyamāne jalapavitre ʼnyenācamanamārjana-tarpaṇādityopasthānakuṇḍikāpūraṇasāvitrījapān yaḥ karoti tadāsya sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ
bhavati || 6.41 || medhātithiḥ |
sa tenācamanaṃ kuryān mārjanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ tathā |
upasthānaṃ sahasrāṃśoḥ kuṇḍikāyāś ca pūraṇam iti || 6.42 ||
gālavaḥ |
sadā jalapavitreṇa mārjanācāmatarpaṇam |
upasthānaṃ raves cāpi yo yatiḥ kurute na ca |
sarvaṃ tan niṣphalaṃ tasya prāyaścittam athācaret || 6.43 ||
atriḥ |
uddhṛtāsu sravantīṣu hradeṣu vimaleṣu ca |
vastrapūtā na kartavyā āpaḥ śuddhā iti śrutiḥ || 6.44 ||
hārītaḥ |
snātvā cācamya vidhivat tiṣṭhann āsīna eva vā |
bibhrajjalapavitraṃ cāpy akṣasūtraṃ karadvaye || 6.45 ||
tadvat pavitre govālakṛte duṣkṛtanāśane |
udayād vidhivat saṃdhyām upāsya trikajapyavat || 6.46 ||
mitrasya carṣaṇītyādyair upasthāya raviṃ tribhiḥ |
prātaḥ sāyaṃ copatiṣṭhed vāruṇībhiś ca pañcabhiḥ || 6.47 ||
trikālaṃ dadyād arghyaṃ tu lohitārkāya so yatiḥ |
bhuktottare ca tad dadyāt paramātmaparāyaṇaḥ || 6.48 ||
bhuktopasthānam udvayena bhikṣākāle haṃsamantreṇa |
divākarodaye dadyāt sāvitryārghyaṃ samāhitaḥ |
japamadhyagate bhānau japānte ʼrghyaṃ pradāpayet || 6.49 ||
devalaḥ |
upasthānaṃ prakurvīta bhāskarasya trisaṃdhyakam |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kuryāj jānubhyāṃ śirasā namet || 6.50 ||
na nāmagrahaṇaṃ kuryād ātmanaḥ praṇamed ravim |
pūrvavat tarpayitvātha japet Samyak samāhitaḥ iti || 6.51 ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
vāruṇībhiḥ sāyaṃsaṃdhyām upatiṣṭheta maitrībhiḥ prātaḥ || 6.52 || [BDh 2.18.21] śaunakaḥ |
suptotthito ʼbhidhyāyet | atha kṛtāvaśyakriyaḥ śuddha ācānto dantadhāvanapūrvakam upary uṣasi yathāvidhi snātvāpomantravad āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir mārjayitvā pūrvābhimukho gāyatryāpa ūrdhvaṃ vikṣipya darbhapāṇir ādityodayāt tiṣṭhan gāyatrīm abhyaset | atha japadhyānastutibhir ahaḥśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 6.53 ||
kapilaḥ |
bhūmyāṃ pādau pratiṣṭhāpya japyaṃ bhojanam arcanaṃ ca kuryāt | atha tiṣṭhan prāṅmukha āsīno vā pavitrapāṇir akṣasūtrair aṅgulibhir vā yathāśakti trikaṃ japet | trisaṃdhyaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt | ajñātaprāṇivadhaśuddhaye jalapavitreṇācamanamārjanatarpaṇakamaṇḍalupūraṇādi kuryāt | sajalāñjalir ādityopasthānam iti || 6.54 ||
evaṃ saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ pūrvoktaṃ yogamācaret | viśvāmitraḥ |
kṛtasnāno japet tāvad yāvad ādityadarśanam |
tato nivātake ramye nimnonnatavivarjite ||
ityādi || 6.55 || evaṃ yogaṃ kṛtvā tasyaivaṃ viduṣo yajñasyety uktakarmasaṃpādanaṃ ca kṛtvā tato yogād viramya japitvā gatvā grāmaikarātracārī ced grāmāntaraṃ gacchet | tadāha śaṅkhaḥ |
ātmalābhasukhaṃ yāvat tāvad dhyātvā śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
viramya yogāt praṇavaṃ japet svādhyāyam eva ca |
anyatra vihared grāmād grāmāntaram iti sthitiḥ || 6.56 ||
yadi grāmāntaraṃ na gacchet tadā stutijapadhyānādi kurvann āsīta | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
uśasaḥ pūrvam utthāya kṛtāvaśyakriyaḥ śuciḥ |
gāyatrīm abhyaset tāvad yāvad ādityadarśanam || 6.57 ||
prāṇāyāmair japair dhyānaiḥ stutibhiś cetihāsakaiḥ |
purāṇair vedadṛṣṭaiś ca kiṃcid ahno ʼvaśeṣayet || 6.58 ||
iti prātaḥkālyaṃ samāptam || 6.59 ||
atha madhyāhnakarmocyate | tatrāha śaunakaḥ |
atha yathāvidhi snātvācamya āpo hi ṣṭhā mayo bhuva iti tisṛbhir mārjayitvāntarjalagato ʼghamarṣaṇena ṣoḍaśaprāṇāyāmān dhārayitvottīrya vāsaḥ pīḍayitvā mṛdambhasā jaṅghayoḥ śaucaṃ kṛtvā anyat prayataṃ kaupīnaṃ bahirvāsaś ca paridhāya apa ācamya oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar iti jalapavitram ādāya tarpayati oṃ bhūs tarpayāmīti || 6.60 ||
pūrvavat sukṛtāñjalir ityantam [see 6.38] | atha ud u tyaṃ citram iti dvābhyām ādityam upatiṣṭhate | prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ dhārayitvā gāyatryāṣṭaśataṃ japed iti || 6.61 || bodhāyanaḥ |
ud u tyaṃ citram iti dvābhyām ādityam upatiṣṭhate | oṃ iti brahma brahma vā etaj jyotiḥ ya eṣa tapati eṣa vedo ya eṣa tapati vedyam etad ya eṣa tapati | evam evaiṣa ātmānaṃ tarpayati | ātmane namaskaroti | ātmā brahma | ātmā jyotir iti | atha sāvitrīṃ sahasrakṛtva āvartayet śatakṛtvo ʼparimitakṛtvaḥ | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar iti jalapavitram ādāyāpo gṛhṇāti [BDh 2.17.39–42] || 6.62 ||
hārītaḥ |
snātvā hy ācamya vidhivaj jalapūtāsraveṇa vai |
vāriṇā tarpayitvā tu mantravad bhāskaraṃ namet || 6.63 ||
kratuḥ |
ud u tyaṃ citraṃ tac cakṣuḥ śivasaṃkalpaṃ pauruṣam |
sūktaṃ ca vaiṣṇavaṃ bhikṣuḥ snātvārkasyāgrato japet || 6.64 ||
prātaḥ sāyaṃ copatiṣṭhet sūryaṃ maitraiś ca vāruṇaiḥ |
bhukte grāme ʼhni bhikṣārthī haṃsamantreṇa paryaṭet || 6.65 ||
tribhis tribhir vācyaliṅgaiḥ pāvamānyaghamarṣaṇaiḥ |
mārjayitvā supūtādbhir gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet || 6.66 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
madhyāhne ca tataḥ snātvā sāvitrīṃ ca samāhitaḥ |
japaṃ kṛtvā tu medhāvī vaidikaṃ kiṃcid ācaret || 6.67 ||
vaiṣṇavaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca vāruṇaṃ raudram eva ca |
sauraṃ copaniṣac caiva japtvā śuddhyartham ātmanaḥ |
dhyānaṃ ca vidhivat kṛtvā tato bhikṣāṃ samācaret || 6.68 ||
hārītaḥ |
āsīnaḥ prāṅmukho maunī prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret |
gāyatrīṃ ca yathāśakti japtvā dhyāyet paraṃ padam |
sthityartham ātmano nityaṃ bhikṣāṭanam athācaret || 6.69 ||
devalaḥ |
ṛgvedādīñ japen mantrān stokāṃś ca prāṅmukho yatiḥ |
teṣāṃ ca nasty anadhyāyo nityakarma hi tat smṛtam || 6.70 ||
vaidikena vidhānena pūjāṃ kuryād dhares tataḥ |
abhāve vedamantrāṇāṃ pāñcarātroditena vā || 6.71 ||
manuḥ |
ahni rātrau ca yān jantūn hinasty ajñānato yatiḥ |
teṣāṃ snātvā viśuddhyarthaṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 6.72 || [MDh 6.69]
kathaṃ pūjāṃ prakurvīteti | puruṣasūktena vedaguhyair anyair mantraiś ca phalapuṣpotpāṭane prāyaścittavidhānāt adbhiḥ kartavyam anyāhṛtair vā || 6.73 || āpastambaḥ |
svādhyāya evotsṛjamāno vācaṃ grāme prāṇavṛttiṃ pratilabhyāniho ʼnamutraś caret | tasya muktam ācchādanaṃ vihitam | sarvataḥ parimokṣam eke || 6.74 ||
[ApDh 2.21.10–12]
manuḥ |
adhiyajñaṃ brahma japed ādhidaivikam eva ca |
ādhyātmikaṃ ca satataṃ vedāntābhihitaṃ ca yat || 6.75 || [MDh 6.83]
saṃyogaṃ ca viyogaṃ ca viyogasya ca sādhanam |
jīveśvarapradhānānāṃ svarūpāṇi vicintayet || 6.76 ||
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ dhyānādhyayanasaṃpadā |
āmadhyāhnadinaṃ prāpya madhyāhne ʼpy eṣa vai vidhiḥ || 6.77 ||
snānasya vidhikarmānte pradāyārghyaṃ yathoditam
ud u tyaṃ citram ity ābhyām upasthāya tato japet || 6.78 ||
iti madhyāhnasnānaṃ samāptam || 6.79 ||
atha bhikṣāṭanam ucyate | tatrośanāḥ |
mādhūkaram asaṃkḷptaṃ prākpraṇītam ayācitam |
tātkālikopapannaṃ ca bhaikṣaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || 6.80 ||
manaḥsaṅkalparahitān gṛhāṃs trīn pañca sapta vā |
gatvā madhuvad uddhāro yat tan mādhūkaraṃ smṛtam || 6.81 ||
śayanotthānataḥ prāg yat prārthitaṃ bhaktisaṃyutaiḥ |
tat prākpraṇītam ity āha bhagavān uśanā muniḥ || 6.82 ||
bhikṣāṭanasamudyogāt prāg yenāpi nimantritam |
ayācitaṃ ca tad bhaikṣaṃ bhoktavyaṃ manur abravīt || 6.83 ||
upasthāne ca yat proktaṃ bhikṣārthaṃ brāhmaṇena hi |
tātkālikam iti proktaṃ tac ca prāśyaṃ mumukṣuṇā || 6.84 ||
siddham annaṃ bhaktajanair ānītaṃ yan maṭhaṃ prati |
upapannaṃ tad ity āhur munayo mokṣakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 6.85 ||
bhikṣāḥ pañcavidhā hy etāḥ somapānasamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
āsām ihaikatamayā vartayañ chuddhim āpnuyāt || 6.86 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
bhaikṣapratikṛtīr etā vidur mādhūkare sthite |
śakto mādhūkare tv etāḥ kurvan kilbiṣam āpnuyāt || 6.87 ||
ekānnaṃ madhu māṃsaṃ cety evamādayo niṣedhā anenaikavākyatvān mādhūkarasamarthasyaiva draṣṭavyāḥ [see 10.59] | samarthasyāpy anugrahārtham ekānnaṃ na duṣyati || 6.88 || āha medhātithiḥ |
ekānnaṃ na tu bhuñjīta na śūdrānnaṃ na bahv api |
aśakto ʼnugrahārthaṃ vā viprād ekānnabhug bhavet || 6.89 ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
yācitāyācitābhyāṃ tu bhaikṣābhyāṃ vartayed yatiḥ || 6.90 ||
aṅgirāḥ |
vyādhitānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu vṛddhānāṃ dīrgharogiṇām |
ekānnāśanadoṣeṇa nainas teṣāṃ tu vidyate || 6.91 ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
yātrāmātraṃ bhuñjīteti || 6.92 || [BDh 2.18.12]
kapilaḥ |
asāṅketikatātkālikādipraśastānnaṃ bhuñjīta | pretānnaṃ varjayed yatir iti || 6.93 ||
yamaḥ |
caren mādhūkaraṃ bhaikṣaṃ yatir mlecchakulād api |
ekānnaṃ na tu bhuñjīta bṛhaspatisamād api || 6.94 ||
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ paraṃ nāsti vāsudevāt paro na ca |
varṇāśramāt paro nāsti nāsti vedasamā śrutiḥ |
nāsti bhaikṣasamā śuddhis tapo nānaśanāt param || 6.95 ||
bhaikṣaṃ mādhūkaraṃ yat tan mahāpātakanāśanam |
tasmāt tenaiva varteta śuddhe pātre ʼtha nirvraṇe iti || 6.96 ||
atha mādhūkaradharmāḥ | tatra kālaṃ manur āha |
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavarjite |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiś caret || 6.97 || [MDh 6.56]
kratuḥ |
pūrvāhṇe vicared yogī samyagjñānī tu paścime |
madhyāhne bhikṣayan bhikṣus tena hy āpnoti kilbiṣam || 6.98 ||
pañcadaśaghaṭikāyā ūrdhvaṃ pādonacatasro madhyāhnaḥ yat pratīcīnaṃ saṅgavāt prācīnaṃ madhyandinād iti liṅgadarśanāt || 6.99 || kapilaḥ |
atyantaṃ bubhukṣito na jaghanyaṃ kalam udīkṣeteti || 6.100 ||
bhaikṣadeśaniyame bodhāyanaḥ |
atha bhaikṣacaryā | brāhmaṇānāṃ śālīnayāyāvarāṇāṃ pravṛtte vaiśvadeve bhikṣāṃ lipseteti || 6.101 || [BDh 2.18.4]
viṣṇuḥ |
bhaikṣaṃ cared gṛhastheṣu yāyāvaragṛheṣu ca |
śreṣṭhā tu prathamā ceyaṃ vṛttir atropadiśyate || 6.102 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ gṛhastheṣu sāgnikeṣu cared yatiḥ |
śraddadhāneṣu dānteṣu śrotriyeṣu mahātmasu || 6.103 ||
ata ūrdhvam aśūdreṣu tv aduṣṭāpatiteṣu ca |
bhaikṣacaryā tv iyaṃ tasya triprakārā prakīrtitā || 6.104 ||
medhātithiḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ madhye tad bhaikṣam ācaret |
tatrāpy asaṃbhave pūrvād ādadītottarottarāt || 6.105 ||
sarveṣām apy abhāve tu bhaktatrayam anaśnataḥ |
bhaikṣaṃ śūdrād api grāhyaṃ rakṣyāḥ prāṇās tu bhikṣayā || 6.106 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ prāṇāḥ saṃsthitihetavaḥ |
tān nighnatāṃ kiṃ na hataṃ rakṣatāṃ kiṃ na rakṣitam || 6.107 ||
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāc chatārdhaṃ pādam eva vā |
śūdraviṭkṣatrabhaikṣeṣu hy āpatsu ca viśuddhaye |
ajñānād ardham etasya prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye || 6.108 ||
kratuḥ |
na kriyāyogacaraṇaṃ śaucāśaucaṃ śubhāśubham |
pṛcchen mādhūkare bhaikṣe śīlaṃ vṛttaṃ śrutaṃ kulam |
pātre tu patite bhaikṣe śaucāśaucaṃ vinaśyati || 5.109 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
na varṇavicayaṃ kuryān na ca pṛcchec chubhāśubham |
pātre tu patite bhaikṣe śaucāśaucaṃ na cintayet || 5.110 ||
vinaśyatīti vā pāṭhaḥ | atriḥ |
na jñātīnāṃ kule bhikṣet | antevāsiparipanthijñātīśvarapretasūtakagṛheṣu bhikṣām upalabhya na gṛhṇīyāt | ātmanā saṃskṛtaṃ parabādhākaraṃ ca varjayet || 5.111 ||
kapilaḥ |
sujīrṇaś ca kṛśo rogī praśānto vikalendriyaḥ |
śiṣyabandhusutabhrātṛsakhibhyo bhaikṣam ācaret || 9.112 ||
aśvaśayyāsanādīnāṃ bāhyānāṃ sparśalakṣaṇaiḥ |
yājanādhyāpanavyākhyāvastrabhāṇḍaparigrahaiḥ || 9.113 ||
maithunekṣaṇasaṅkalpaiḥ saṅkarī syād yatiḥ kṛtaiḥ |
tasmāt putrād bhaikṣam icched gṛhe ʼraṇye ʼpi vāśrayan || 9.114 ||
sa evāha |
sujīrṇo ʼtikṛśo rogī śatābdo vikalendriyaḥ |
śiṣyabandhusutabhrātṛsuhṛdbhyo bhaikṣam ācaret || 9.115 ||
kubjavāmanakuṇḍāś ca chinnapaṃgvandharogiṇaḥ |
bhikṣādoṣair na lipyante yadi rājā na pālayet || 9.116 ||
gṛhasaṃkhyāṃ vasiṣṭha āha |
saptāgārāṇy asaṃkalpitāni cared vidhūme sannamusale ekaśāṭīparivṛto ʼjinena vā
|| 9.117 || [VaDh 10.7–9]
devalaḥ |
sarvaṃ vā vicared grāmaṃ pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave |
niyamya prayato vācam abhiśastāṃś ca varjayet || 9.118 ||
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārddhuṣeḥ |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan || 9.119 ||
tān prajāpatir ity āha mā kṛdhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
śraddhāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatam aśraddhayetarat || 9.120 ||
udyataṃ cāpy abhojyānnaṃ yac ca vyādhiprakopanam |
nirākṛtānnaṃ vānyena nādadīta vicakṣaṇaḥ || 9.121 ||
atriḥ |
atha bhaikṣavidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ vakṣye varjyāṃś ca nityaśaḥ |
yam akurvan pataty āśu yatiḥ kurvan vimucyate || 9.122 ||
mādhūkareṇa varteta na kuryād itarāḥ sadā |
tāḥ sarvāḥ kutsitā vṛttir anindyā mantraṇād ṛte || 9.123 ||
mādhūkaram asaṃkḷptam upapannaṃ yadṛcchayā |
tad aśnīyād anindyānnaṃ niḥspṛheṇaiva cetasā || 9.124 ||
ṣaṭ trīn vāpi ca saptāṣṭau nityaṃ bhikṣur gṛhāṃś caret |
mādhūkaram idaṃ śreṣṭham ūrdhvaṃ vā kāmataś caret || 9.125 ||
kaṇayāvakapiṇyākaśākatakrapayodadhi |
bhikṣāṃ sarvarasopetāṃ hiṃsāvarjaṃ samāharet || 9.126 ||
vratāviruddhaṃ yad yasya tasya tad bhakṣaṇaṃ bhavet |
hitaṃ mitaṃ sadāśnīyād yats ukhenaiva jīryate |
dhātuḥ prakupyate yena tad annaṃ varjayed yatiḥ || 9.127 ||
kuṭumbasaṅkaṭaṃ caiva lobhenopahataṃ gṛham |
pīḍayānnaṃ prayaccheta tasmāt tad varjayed gṛham || 9.128 ||
śāṭhyaṃ vāpy atha dainyaṃ vā lakṣyate yad gṛhe kvacit |
varjayet tad gṛhaṃ bhikṣuḥ kākajagdhaṃ yathā haviḥ || 9.129 ||
udakyācoditaṃ hy annaṃ dvijānnaṃ śūdracoditam |
prāṇyaṅge cāyase vāpi kḷptānnaṃ varjayed yatiḥ || 9.130 ||
pitrarthaṃ kalpitaṃ pūrvaṃ devārthaṃ vānyakāraṇāt |
varjayet tādṛśīṃ bhikṣāṃ parabādhākarīṃ tathā || 9.131 ||
notpannā saṃtatiḥ kācid yatra strīpuṃsayor gṛhe |
nāśnīyāt tadgṛhe bhikṣur bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 9.132 ||
dṛṣṭaṃ bhrūṇahapāṣaṇḍaiḥ kuṣṭhyādibhir udakyayā |
śvaṣaṇḍakākacaṇḍālair anyair vānteʼvasāyibhiḥ || 9.133 ||
paśyaṃś caitān na bhikṣeta bhikṣaṃś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
etair na cāpi saṃbhāṣen mārgapraśnād bhayād vinā || 9.134 ||
mahāpātakinaḥ sarvān patitān parivarjayet |
sarveṣu vyavahāreṣu bhojanārthe viśeṣataḥ || 9.135 ||
kṛte deśaṃ tyajed grāmaṃ tretāyāṃ dvāpare kulam |
kartāraṃ tu kalau duṣṭaṃ tyaktvā bhaikṣyaṃ cared yatiḥ || 9.136 ||
kartā caivānumantā ca prerakaś cānumodakaḥ |
puṇye caiva tathā pāpe bhavanti phalabhāginaḥ || 9.137 ||
sagotram asagotraṃ vā yadgṛhe sūtakaṃ bhavet |
na tāvac chudhyate bhūmir yāvat tat syād anirdaśam || 9.138 ||
na tatra karma kurute pitṛdevādikaṃ kvacit |
bhikṣāṃ bhikṣur na bhikṣeta bhikṣaṃś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 9.139 ||
na jñātīnāṃ kule bhikṣur na ceśvaragṛhe kvacit |
na śiṣyabandhumitrādīn bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit || 9.140 ||
nāśnanti pitaro devā nāśnanti vṛṣalīpateḥ |
strījitasyāpi nāśnanti tadannaṃ varjayed yatiḥ || 9.141 ||
varṇānām āśramāṇāṃ ca svadharmo vīrasaṃjñitaḥ |
ye tu tasmin pravartante vīrās te viprakīrtitāḥ |
yas taṃ lopayate dharmaṃ taṃ vīraghnaṃ yatis tyajet || 9.142 ||
atha digvāsasīṃ kanyāṃ garbhiṇīṃ malināṃ tathā |
pramattāṃ muditāṃ kruddhām annaṃ bhoktuṃ samudyatām || 9.143 ||
garbhabhartṛdruhāṃ caiva svairiṇīṃ kāmacāriṇīm |
avīrāṃ kuṣṭhinīṃ cāndhāṃ punarbhūṃ kṛtrimāṃ tathā || 9.144 ||
muktakeśīṃ sukhāsīnāṃ sarāgāṃ vismayānvitām |
śaṭhāṃ dīnāṃ durācārāṃ sakhedāṃ vyādhipīḍitām || 9.145 ||
nirlajjāṃ vardhakīṃ śrāntāṃ preṣyāṃ klībāṃ suniṣṭhurām |
bhikṣāṃ bhikṣur na bhikṣeta pāyayantīṃ stanaṃ śiśum || 9.146 ||
kapilaḥ |
kuṭumbasaṅkaṭaṃ soparodhaṃ vā gṛhaṃ na viśet | suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ pramuditāṃ sakhedāṃ sukhāsīnām antarvatnīṃ vyādhitāṃ bhartṛgatāṃ sarāgām annaṃ bhoktum udyatāṃ bālalagnastanāṃ bhikṣāṃ na yāceta | rajasvalāprayuktāṃ nagnavadhūhastagatām avajñopahatāṃ kumāryānītāṃ bhikṣāṃ na yāceta na gṛhṇīyāc ca
|| 9.147 ||
kratuḥ |
pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ māṃsaṃ madhu śāvakasautakam |
mūrkhābhiśastapatitaklībānnāni ca varjayet || 9.148 ||
adhīrāṃ svairiṇīṃ kuṣṭhīṃ garbhabhartṛdruhām api |
kanyāṃ dhātrīṃ punarbhūṃ ca nagnāṃ bhikṣeta na kvacit || 9.149 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
dvāv evāśramiṇau bhojyau vānaprastho gṛhī tathā |
abhojyam annam anyeṣām itareṣāṃ ca liṅginām iti || 9.150 ||
atha bhikṣācaryā | tatrāha śaunakaḥ |
atha saptavyāhṛtibhir govālarajjvā sodakayā pātraṃ prakṣālya haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ādityam upasthāya vāgyataḥ prasannātmā ca gacched iti || 9.151 ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
kāṣṭhālābuvidalapārthivānām ekaikaṃ bhaikṣabhājanam | tasya govālarajjvā sodakapāṇinā parigharṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā sādhuvṛtteṣu brāhmaṇeṣu bhaikṣacaryāṃ caret
|| 6.152 ||
atriḥ |
savyenādāya pātraṃ tu tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
upasthānaṃ raveḥ kuryād dhyātvā caikatvam ātmanaḥ || 6.153 ||
japed virajasaṃ mantraṃ devaṃ dhyāyan samāhitaḥ |
yo ʼsau viṣṇvākhya āditye puruṣo ʼntarhṛdi sthitaḥ || 6.154 ||
so ʼhaṃ nārāyaṇo devaḥ puruṣo lokasākṣikaḥ |
evaṃ dhyātvātmanātmānaṃ puruṣaṃ viśvabhāvanam || 6.155 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā namasyet timirāpaham |
namaskṛtya punar bhūmau samārohed upānahau || 6.156 ||
śaucārthaṃ vicared bhikṣuḥ pādatrāṇe tu na tyajet |
udapātraṃ tathā bhikṣāṃ vinā duṣyaty upānahau || 6.157 ||
upānahāv avaṣṭabhya vrajed bhikṣuḥ smaran harim |
viṣṇus tiryag adhordhvaṃ me vaikuṇṭho vidiśo diśaḥ || 6.158 ||
pātu māṃ sarvato rāmo dhanvī cakrī ca keśavaḥ |
sa māṃ rakṣatu govindaḥ somapo havyakavyabhuk || 6.159 ||
viṣṇus tārkṣyāsano ʼnantaś cakrī vāmanarūpadhṛk |
yo ʼsau sarvagataḥ sūkṣmaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ || 6.160 ||
nirguṇo niṣkalaś caiva śvetamūrtidharo ʼvyayaḥ |
anādyantaḥ sadānante phaṇāmaṇivibhūṣite |
śvete kṣīrārṇave madhye sa māṃ rakṣatu mādhavaḥ || 6.161 ||
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ deham āpādatalamastakam |
