sarvāyudhaḥ sarvaśaktiḥ pātu māṃ garuḍadhvajaḥ || 6.162 ||
sa māṃ rakṣatu sarvātmā mādhavaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
yasya nābhyambujāj jāto brahmā sargakaraḥ prabhuḥ || 6.163 ||
evaṃ dhyāyann ekavāsā upavītī ca paryaṭet |
vāso hi brahmaṇā sṛṣṭaṃ sarvadharmaprasiddhaye |
vastrābhāve kriyāḥ sarvā niṣphalāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 6.164 ||
devalaḥ |
ātmānaṃ brahmasvarūpasaṃsthānaṃ kṛtvā namo brahmaṇa ity etair namaskāraṃ kṛtvā virajasaṃ mantraṃ japitvā ā satyeneti pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā ye te panthāna iti japitvā vāgyataḥ parivrajet || 6.165 ||
yamaḥ |
medhyaṃ bhaikṣaṃ caren nityaṃ sāyāhne vāgyataḥ śuciḥ |
ekavāsā avāsā vā mandagāmī yugāntadṛk || 6.166 ||
eka eva cared bhaikṣam ekadṛṣṭir alolupaḥ |
tṛptiprayojano na syād yo ‘nagnir aniketanaḥ || 6.167 ||
medhātithiḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ jānvor adho nābheḥ paridhāyaikam ambaram |
dvitīyam antarvāsaś ca paridhāya raviṃ tataḥ || 6.168 ||
upasthāyātha bhaikṣārthaṃ pātrī daṇḍī gṛhān vrajet |
tiṣṭhet saṃdarśane dvāri yācya bhikṣām adhomukhaḥ || 6.169 ||
vāgyataḥ suprasannātmā svapan dhyāyan divāpi ca |
adṛṣṭopakrame kāle tūṣṇīṃ godohasaṃmitam || 6.170 ||
yadi nodyamate kaścid dṛṣṭvāpy anyagṛhaṃ vrajet |
tāvat pratiśrute tiṣṭhed yāvad bhaikṣaṃ pradīyate || 6.171 ||
kratuḥ |
dṛṣṭipūtapadanyāsī maunī tu sumatiḥ śamī |
ekavastry ekapātrī ca niḥsaṅgaḥ suptavac caret || 6.172 ||
catuḥṣaḍaṣṭakāle vā yatiś cāndrāyaṇena vā |
pañcasaptagṛhebhyo vā bhikṣām icchet kriyāvatām || 6.173 ||
bhavatpūrvaṃ sakṛd yācet tiṣṭhan bhikṣām adhomukhaḥ |
godohamātram ākāṅkṣen niṣkrānto na punar vrajet || 6.174 ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
bhavatpūrvaṃ pracodayāt | godohanam ākāṅkṣet || 6.175 || [BDh 2.18.5–6]
devalaḥ |
bhikṣākāle sadā kāryaṃ yatīnāṃ chatradhāraṇam |
varṣamāṇe tu parjanye na tu gharmārkatāpanāt || 6.176 ||
atriḥ |
ekavastry ekapātrī ca sadaikākī vrajed yatiḥ |
ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ na jaghanyaṃ kathaṃcana || 6.177 ||
ūrdhvaṃ na tiryag dūraṃ vā nirīkṣet paryaṭan yatiḥ |
yugamātraṃ mahīpṛṣṭhaṃ bhikṣur gacched vilokayan || 6.178 ||
na vīkṣed dvārarandhreṇa bhikṣālipsur gṛhāntaram |
na kuryād ghoṣaṇaṃ tatra na dvāraṃ tāḍayed yatiḥ || 6.179 ||
atikramya gṛhaṃ pūrvaṃ praviśen na punas tu tat |
praviśya bhikṣām ādāya triṃśat prāṇāyamāṃś caret || 6.180 ||
naiva savyāpasavyena bhikṣākāle cared gṛham |
anindyātikramaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 6.181 ||
pūrvāsaṃkalpitagṛhaṃ svayaṃ vivṛtam āśrayet |
deśakālavibhāgena sāmarthyāpekṣayāpi ca || 6.182 ||
anāgate pare bhikṣau bhikṣārthaṃ praviśed gṛham |
praviśya bhaikṣabhug gehaṃ niṣprakampena cetasā || 6.183 ||
gṛhiṇīdarśane vāpi vahnyāgāre ʼpi vā sthitaḥ |
savyenādāya pātraṃ tu tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇena tu || 6.184 ||
praṇavādi bhavatpūrvaṃ bhikṣāṃ dehīti yācayet |
bhikṣārthaṃ codanaṃ kṛtvā śakto nopaviśet kvacit || 6.185 ||
na cānyad vastu yāceta tṛptilobhena kiṃcana |
kṛtvā bhikṣāṃ dṛṣṭipūtāṃ dātuś ca karasaṃsthitām || 6.186 ||
tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe tv aṃse yatiḥ saṃdhāya pāṇinā |
utpāṭya pātrakavacaṃ dakṣiṇena kareṇa tu |
pātraṃ vāmakare sthāpyaṃ na kvacid dakṣiṇe kare || 6.187 ||
likhitaḥ |
naikānnaṃ mriyamāṇo ʼpi bhuñjītāpi niṣādajāt |
mādhūkareṇa varteta sattvaśuddhikaraṃ hi tat || 6.188 ||
naikasmin nāpi kutrāpi pratyākhyāto gṛhe punaḥ |
praviśed bhaikṣasamaye na ca gehāntareṇa vā |
anyārthaṃ pūrvakḷptaṃ vā bhikṣāṃ naivādadīta ca || 6.189 ||
kāśyapaḥ |
caturaṅgulāntaritaḥ samapādo niścalas tiṣṭhet | kāṣṭhapāṣāṇamṛṇmayavarjam aśucir iti na jugupseta śucir iti matvā gṛhṇīyāt | mukhamaṇḍalaṃ na kadācin nirīkṣeta | hastamātraṃ cakṣuṣā paśyet | tathāgrato yugamātrāvalokī | pārśvaṃ pṛṣṭhataś ca muhur muhur nāvalokayet | vivṛtadvārāṇi praviśet | apadvārāṇi na praviśet | dvāraṃ na hanyāt na ghoṣayet | kṣipraṃ na nirgacchet | ciraṃ na tiṣṭhed iti || 6.190 ||
devalaḥ |
grāme praviṣṭe bhikṣārthe yadi vighno bhavet kvacit |
punar bhaikṣaṃ na bhikṣeta bahir grāmād vinirgataḥ || 6.191 ||
jamadagniḥ |
pañcāhaṃ saptarātraṃ vā yasmin bhikṣā na labhyate |
tad gṛhaṃ varjayen nityaṃ caṇḍālagṛhavad yatiḥ || 6.192 ||
taddināni gṛhasthasya nirāśo bhikṣuko vrajet |
svādhyāyaṃ homadānaṃ ca sarvam ādāya gacchati || 6.193 ||
sa evāha |
āhāramātrād adhikaṃ yatir bhaikṣaṃ tu nāharet |
anyāyena tu pakvānnaṃ coravad daṇḍam arhati || 6.194 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
ekapātreṇa bhuñjīta nirdiṣṭaṃ vratam uttamam |
naiva doṣo dvipātreṇa tv aśakto vyādhipīḍane || 6.195 ||
śaktaś ca nīrujaś caiva naikapātreṇa vartayan |
pātralobhī sa duṣṭātmā narakaṃ pratipadyate || 6.196 ||
lobhamānas tu yo bhikṣuḥ pātrapūraṇam icchati |
dātā svargam avāpnoti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam || 6.197 ||
nimantritas tu saṃnyāsī yadā bhikṣāṃ samācaret |
lobhaṃ tatra prakuryāc cet pataty eva na saṃśayaḥ || 6.198 ||
dehi dehīti yo brūyāl lavaṇavyañjanādi vā |
gomāṃsatulyaṃ tad bhaikṣaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 6.199 ||
yat kiṃcid dīyamānaṃ tu gṛhiṇīkarasaṃsthitam |
na gṛhṇāty alpabuddhyā cet kākayoniṣu jāyate || 6.200 ||
kapilaḥ |
bhikṣitvā na ciram āsīta | nety ukte cādoṣamanāḥ parivarteta | naikapātrapūraṇam anvicchet || 6.201 ||
bharadvājaḥ |
pūrṇā darvi parāpata iti japitvā punar nama iti parivarteta || 6.202 ||
atha bhojanavidhiḥ | tatra śaunakaḥ |
tata upāvṛtya śucau deśe pātraṃ nyasya pādau prakṣālyācamya trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayitvāpa ācamya om iti vāriṇā bhaikṣyaṃ prokṣya gāyatryābhimantrya idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrama iti viṣṇave havir nivedayet | ud u tyaṃ citram iti dvābhyām ādityāya brahma jajñānam iti brahmaṇe bhūtebhyo nama iti bhūtebhyo nivedayet | atha śeṣam auṣadhavat prāśnīyāt || 6.203 ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
atha bhaikṣacaryāyā upāvṛtya pātraṃ śucau deśe nyasya hastaṃ pādaṃ ca prakṣālyācamya ud u tyaṃ citram ity ādityasyāgre nivedayet | brahma jajñānam iti brahmaṇe idaṃ viṣṇur iti viṣṇave bhūtebhyo nama iti bhūtebhyo nivedayed iti | vijñāyate | ādhānaprabhṛti yajamāna evāgnayo bhavanti | tasya prāṇo gārhapatyaḥ apāno ʼnvāhāryapacanaḥ vyāna āhavanīyaḥ samānodānau sabhyāvasathyau | pañca vā ete ʼya ātmasthā ātmany eva juhoti | sa evātmayajña ātmaniṣṭha ātmapratiṣṭhita ātmānaṃ kṣemaṃ nayatīti vijñāyate | bhūtebhyo dayāpūrvaṃ saṃsṛjya śeṣam adbhiḥ saṃspṛśya auṣadhavat prāśnīyāt || 6.204 || [BDh 2.18.7–10]
yamaḥ |
na bhaikṣapātre bhuñjīta pātre nācamanaṃ caret |
kṛtatretādvāpareṣu pātrabhojī yatir bhavet |
kalau na pātre bhuñjīta dhārayen na ca kuṇḍikām || 6.205 ||
bharadvājaḥ |
yat kiṃcij jīrṇaparṇaṃ patitaṃ pātraṃ labdhvā tatrāśnīyān na taijase kadācana | taijasapātre bhojanaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret | grāmānte vijane ʼraṇye taṭāke vijane nadīpuline puṣkariṇīṣu gatvā tad annam upayuñjīta | tīrthadeśe prakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā sāntardhānaṃ pātraṃ sthāpayet | mṛttikātrayeṇa tu pādau prakṣālya hastau ca tathaiva prakṣālyācamya prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret | praṇavena gāyatryā annasūktaṃ japitvā paramātmasvarūpaṃ dhyātvā amṛtopastaraṇam asity apaḥ prāśya prāṇāhutiṃ juhoti | bhojanamadhye yat kiṃcit sūkṣmakeśamakṣikābhihataṃ ca tad annaṃ piṇḍamātram uddhṛtya hastaprakṣālaṇaṃ kṛtvā bhojanaṃ karoti | mukhe keśaṃ lagnaṃ tatkṣaṇāt kṣiped grāsamāsyena dvādaśagaṇḍūṣair mukhaprakṣālaṇaṃ kṛtvā bhojanaṃ karoti
|| 6.206 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
suguptavāṅmanaścakṣur guptaguhyaḥ kṛtāhnikaḥ |
suguptabhaikṣam āhṛtya suguptaṃ bhojanaṃ caret || 6.207 ||
na spṛśed vāmahastena bhuñjāno ʼnnaṃ kadācana |
na pādau na śiropasthaṃ na padbhyāṃ bhojanaṃ spṛśet || 6.208 ||
na kiṃcid bheṣajād anyat tat tathā dantadhāvanāt |
vinā bhojanakālena na bhakṣyān bhakṣayed yatiḥ || 6.209 ||
viṣṇuḥ |
bhaikṣaṃ yavāgraṃ bhuñjīta payo ghṛtam athāpi vā |
supakvaṃ phalamūlaṃ vā śākapiṇyākasaktu vā || 6.210 ||
ity ete hy aśanākārā yoginaḥ siddhikārakāḥ |
tat prayuñjyād ahar ahaḥ parameṇa samādhinā || 6.211 ||
apaḥ pītvā sakṛt tūṣṇīṃ bhuñjānaḥ prāk samāhitaḥ |
prāṇāyeti tu bhuñjīta prathamām āhutiṃ yatiḥ || 6.212 ||
apānāya dvitīyā syād vyānāyeti tataḥ parā |
udānāya caturthī syāt samānāyeti pañcamī || 6.213 ||
itthaṃ prāṇāhutīr hutvā śeṣaṃ bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ |
apaḥ punaḥ sakṛt prāśya tv ācamya hṛdayaṃ spṛśet || 6.214 ||
yamaḥ |
vidhūme sannamusale vṛtte vā yajñasaṃstare |
na śvavṛttisamākīrṇe na tāpasasamākule || 6.215 ||
nābhuktavati loke vā bhaikṣaṃ bhikṣeta bhikṣukaḥ |
yātrāmātraṃ tu gṛhṇīyāt kevalaṃ prāṇadhāraṇam |
yathālabdhaṃ ca nāśnīyāt kāmaṃ bhuñjīta na kvacit || 6.216 ||
bhaikṣasyāgamaśuddhasya prokṣitasya hutasya ca |
yāvato grasate grāsāṃs tāvadbhiḥ kratubhiḥ samāḥ || 6.217 ||
prokṣitaṃ praṇavenaiva hutam ādhyātmikāgniṣu |
prāṇādiṣu daśārdheṣu svāhākāraiḥ pṛthak pṛthak || 6.218 ||
prāṇaś cakṣur divaṃ sūryo vyānaḥ śrotraṃ niśā śaśī |
apāno vāṅ mano vahniḥ samāno ʼpo mano ʼśaniḥ || 6.219 ||
udāno vāyur ākāśas tṛpyantv annaṃ samāhitaḥ |
datvaiṣāṃ bhakṣayaṃs tṛptiṃ mucyate sa ṛṇatrayāt || 6.220 ||
bhaikṣasya bhojanaṃ sarvatroktaṃ prakṣālya bhojanam | āha saṃvartaḥ |
aṣṭau bhikṣāḥ samādāya sa muniḥ pañca sapta vā |
adbhiḥ prakṣālya tat sarvaṃ bhuñjīta susamāhitaḥ || 6.221 ||
kapilaḥ |
atha grāmād āgatya ekapātreṇa bhaikṣam udakena plāvya sakṛd bhuñjīta | na kenacit saha saṃbhāṣeta kiṃcana | na kiṃcana yāced auṣadhād anyat | evaṃ vartamāno yāvajjīvaṃ bhikṣuko mukto bhavet || 6.222 ||
doṣāśaṅkāyāmidaṃ draṣṭavyam | kratuḥ |
catuṣkaiḥ prokṣayed bhikṣāṃ talliṅgair balim āharet |
viṣṇubrahmārkabhūtebhyo dadyād vā kiṃcid arthine || 6.223 ||
padmam ūrdhvamukhaṃ kṛtvā prāṇādyaiḥ pūrakādibhiḥ |
prādeśamātre brahmāgnau hutvānnaṃ mucyate malaiḥ || 6.224 ||
na svādayīta bhuñjānaḥ ṣaḍ rasān naiva kīrtayet |
satkṛtāsatkṛte tulyo nābhinandeta na dviṣet || 6.225 ||
sa evāha |
notsṛjen nātibhuñjīta na ninden nādhikaṃ haret |
gobrāhmaṇārkasomāgnimarudāpo na kutsayet || 6.226 ||
kapilaḥ |
vratāviruddhaṃ yat kiṃcit paryuṣitam api gṛhṇīyād āpyāyayitukāmaḥ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet trir dvir vā prakṣālya deśaṃ pātraṃ vā yantram āropya praṇavenābhyukṣya trikeṇābhimantrayed bhikṣām | idaṃ viṣṇur iti grāsamātram api pūjayet | nānivedyaṃ bhuñjīta | bhūtabalidānādikam arthine saṃvibhāgaśīlatāpi praśastā
|| 6.227 ||
sukhaṃ jīvanti munayo bhaikṣavṛttim upāśritāḥ |
adrohāḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sāraṅgā iva ṣaṭpadāḥ || 6.228 ||
soma evānnam aśnīyād iti dhyātvā yo ʼnnam atti so ʼmṛtam atti | prāṇāya svāheti prāṇe ʼnnam atti | apānāya svāhety apāneʼnnam atti | vyānāya svāheti vyāne ʼnnam atti | udānāya svāhety udāne ʼnnam atti | samānāya svāheti samāne ʼnnam atti | yo yo ʼnnam atti sa so ‘gniḥ | yad annam adyate tat sarvaṃ somaḥ | prakṣālya pātraṃ trikeṇābhimantritaṃ pratigṛhṇīta | pratimucyate bhuktvā pāpād iti || 6.229 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
mādhūkare yathāpūrvaṃ caturthāyotsṛjed yatiḥ |
śeṣeṣu ca caturṣv eva yatis tan na visarjayet || 6.230 ||
ud u tyaṃ citram ity ābhyām ādityāya nivedayet |
brahma jajñānam ity uktvā brahmaṇe ʼnnaṃ nivedayet |
idaṃ viṣṇur ity anena viṣṇave ʼtha nivedayet || 6.231 ||
tatas tu sarvabhūtebhyo baliṃ dattvā caturthakam |
grāsamātraṃ tu dātavyaṃ sūryādīnāṃ yathākramam || 6.232 ||
yaḥ parānnaṃ samāhṛtya tyajaty annaṃ dine dine |
tasya doṣeṇa yujyeta prāyaścittī bhaved yatiḥ || 6.233 ||
api cāsya kuto dānaṃ dattvā cāsya kutaḥ phalam |
dātā pratigrahītā ca pretya vai narakaṃ vrajet || 6.234 ||
hārītaḥ |
utpāṭayec ca kavacaṃ dakṣiṇena kareṇa vai |
pātraṃ vāmakare sthāpya dakṣiṇenānuśleṣayet || 6.235 ||
yāvad annakṣudhārtiḥ syāt tāvad bhaikṣaṃ samācaret |
tato nivṛtya tat pātraṃ saṃsthāpyācamya saṃyamī || 6.236 ||
caturaṅgulaiḥ pracchādya grāsamātraṃ samāhitaḥ |
sarvavyañjanasaṃyuktaṃ pṛthak pātre nivedayet || 6.237 ||
sūryādidevabhūtebhyo dattvānnaṃ prokṣya vāriṇā |
bhuñjīta parṇapuṭake na pātre vāgyato yatiḥ || 6.238 ||
vaṭārkāśvatthapatreṣu kumbhītindukapatrayoḥ |
kovidārakarañjeṣu na bhuñjīta kadācana || 6.239 ||
na ca kāṃsye tu bhuñjīta tv āpady api kadācana |
palāśāḥ sarva ucyante yatayaḥ kāṃsyabhojinaḥ || 6.240 ||
kāṃsyakasya ca yat pāpaṃ gṛhasthasya tathaiva ca |
kāṃsyabhojī yatiḥ sarvaṃ prāpnuyāt kilbiṣaṃ tayoḥ || 6.241 ||
yamaḥ |
hiraṇmayāni pātrāṇi kṛṣṇāyasamayāni ca |
yatīnāṃ tāny apātrāṇi bhaikṣe vā bhojane ʼpi vā || 6.242 ||
suvarṇarūpyapātreṣu tāmrakāṃsyāyaseṣu ca |
bhikṣāṃ dattvā na dharmo ʼsti gṛhītvā narakaṃ vrajet || 6.243 ||
likhitaḥ |
na bhaikṣapātre bhuñjīta pātre nācamanaṃ caret |
kṛtatretādvāpareṣu pātrabhojī yatir bhavet || 6.244 ||
kalau na pātre bhuñjīta dhārayen na ca kuṇḍikām |
bhuñjīta parṇapuṭake parṇapṛṣṭhe na jātucit || 6.245 ||
palāśapadmakadalīdalābhāve hy abhojanam |
abhāve ʼpy anyapātrāṇāṃ na kāṃsye naiva rājate || 6.246 ||
nāpi kārṣṇāyase nāpi sauvarṇe nāpi tāmrake |
na prāṇyaṅge naiva bhūmau na kuśāditṛṇeṣu vā || 6.247 ||
palāśaparṇe bhuñjīta pāṇipātre ʼtha vāpadi |
palāśapadmapatreṣu gṛhī bhuktvaindavaṃ caret |
yatir vanasthaś ca tato labhate caindavaṃ phalam || 6.248 ||
medhātithiḥ |
suvarṇakāṃsyarūpyeṣu tāmrābjāśmamayeṣu ca |
bhuñjan bhikṣur na duṣyeta duṣyate tu parigrahāt || 6.249 ||
suvarṇādīni pātrāṇi bhaikṣāya bhojanāya vā svayaṃ na parigrāhyāṇīti yama-likhitayor abhiprāyaḥ | medhātithes tu svayaṃ parigrahe ʼpi parakīyeṣu teṣv āpadi bhuñjāno na duṣyatīti matam | parakīyānām apy abhāve pāṇipātrabhojanam | kāṃsyādiṣu parṇābhāvamātreṇa na bhoktavyaṃ kiṃ tu mumūrṣāvasthāprāptau hārītena tv āpady api kadācanety uktatvāt || 6.250 || balidānasya viniyogam atrir āha |
nivedyaṃ nārthine dadyād yatheṣṭaṃ notsṛjeta vā |
prakṣipet taj jale vidvān nikhaned vā mahītale || 6.251 ||
bhikṣānnasya dānaṃ bhojanavighne samutpanne tasyānnasya viniyogaṃ ca kratur āha |
mādhūkaraṃ samāhṛtya brāhmaṇebhyo dadāti yaḥ |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ bhoktā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 6.252 ||
prāyaścittavratāpatsu bhaikṣam uktaṃ dvijātiṣu |
tad deyaṃ steyam anyeṣāṃ yatīnāṃ tu makhaiḥ samam || 6.253 ||
yateḥ sakṛdbhojanaṃ śāṇḍilya āha |
grāmaṃ gatvā tu sāyāhne bhaikṣam āhṛtya vāgyataḥ |
aṣṭau grāsān samaśnīyāt pratyahaṃ sakṛd eva tu || 6.254 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
na kvacin niśi bhuñjīta bhuktvā bhuñjīta no punaḥ |
bhuñjāno niśi saṃnyāsī patate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 6.255 ||
saṃdhyākāle tu saṃprāpte niśi bhuñjīta yo yatiḥ |
upasthānaṃ ca japyaṃ ca prāṇāyāmo balis tathā |
sarve te niṣphalās tasya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || 6.256 ||
dvirbhojane vasiṣṭhaḥ |
brāhmaṇakule yal labhate tad bhuñjīta sāyaṃ prātar madhumāṃsavarjaṃ na ca tṛpyed iti | (VaDh 10.24–25)
tad etad aśaktaviṣayam || 6.257 ||
atha bhojanottarakriyā | tatra śaunakaḥ |
prāśyāpa ācamya vāṅma āsannasoḥ prāṇa iti japitvācamya ud vayaṃ tamasas parity ādityam upasthāya saptavyāhṛtibhir govālarajjvā sodakena pātraṃ prakṣālya trīn prāṇāyāmān dhārayitvā gāyatryāṣṭaśataṃ japet | japadhyānābhyāṃ śiṣṭakālaṃ nayet | athāstamitabhāskarāṃ sāyaṃsaṃdhyām ātārakodayam upāsīno gāyatrījapaṃ kuryāt | atha yāvad avaśam upāgatas tāvad dhyāyet | suptotthito ʼpi dhyāyet || 6.258 ||
yamaḥ |
suvarṇasyātha rūpyasya yajñeṣu camasasya ca |
yatīnāṃ caiva pātrāṇām adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate || 6.259 ||
likhitaḥ |
bhuktvopasthāya mārtāṇḍaṃ purāṇāni sadā paṭhet |
bhāvaśuddhyā harau bhaktiḥ purāṇaśravaṇād bhavet || 6.260 ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
kāṣṭhālābuvidalapārthivānām ekaṃ bhaikṣabhājanam | tasya govālarajjvā sodakayā parigharṣaṇam | pratyāhnikaṃ śaucam | nātrācāmet | na bhūmau kṣipet | nāśucibhiḥ saṃspṛśet | bhuktvācāntaḥ punar ācamya dravyāṇy abhyukṣet | rajjuśikyaṃ yathānyad aviruddhaṃ syāt || 6.261 ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
bhuktvā sūryam upasthāya prāṇān āyamya cāsane |
gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japtvā dhyāyen nārāyaṇaṃ punaḥ || 6.262 ||
dinaśeṣaṃ purāṇādyair nītvopāsya ca paścimām |
saṃdhyāṃ tato ʼbhyased yogaṃ nidrānte ʼpi punas tathā || 6.263 ||
evaṃ dine dine kurvan dhyānam eva pradhānataḥ |
karmāṇy api ca kurvāṇo brahmabhūyāya kalpate || 6.264 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
bhuktvā punar upasthānam ud vayaṃ tamasas pari |
bhikṣāṭanavidhir bhikṣoḥ proktaḥ pañcavidho mayā || 6.265 ||
ya evaṃ vartate bhikṣuḥ sa yatiḥ sāśramī sa ca |
saṃdhyām upāsya vidhivad dhyānakarma samācaret || 6.266 ||
maitrībhir ṛgbhiḥ pūrvāyāṃ saṃdhyāyām upatiṣṭhate |
madhyāhnasaṃdhyāṃ saurībhiḥ sāyaṃ varuṇadevataiḥ || 6.267 ||
seyaṃ sārasvatī jñeyā saṃdhyā yābhyudayāśrayā |
śāṅkarī caiva madhyāhne aparāhṇe tu vaiṣṇavī || 6.268 ||
evaṃ dhyānaṃ sadā kāryaṃ brāhmaṇena vipaścitā |
japayajñārthasiddhyarthaṃ dhyānam etan mayoditam || 6.269 ||
gavāṃ vālapavitrais tu yaḥ saṃdhyāṃ samupāsate |
yat kṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ tena tasya sadyo vinaśyati || 6.270 ||
darbheṣu darbhapāṇiḥ san prāṅmukhaḥ susamāhitaḥ |
sahasrakṛtvaḥ sāvitrīṃ śatakṛtvo ʼpi vā japet || 6.271 ||
oṃkārapūrvikās tisro mahāvyāhṛtayo ʼmalāḥ |
tripadā caiva gāyatrī vijñeyā paramaṃ padam || 6.272 ||
pavitraṃ dhārayen nityaṃ kauśeyaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
bhuñjānas tu viśeṣeṇa so ʼnnadoṣair na lipyate || 6.273 ||
upavītī tridaṇḍī ca śikhī pīṭhī kusumbhavān |
śrutismṛtyuktakarmāṇi yāni tāni samācaret |
anutiṣṭhan sadā yogaṃ yatiḥ sidhyati nānyathā || 6.274 ||
medhātithiḥ |
govālarajjvā sodakyā pātraṃ prakṣālayet svayam |
bhuktvā vyāhṛtibhiś caiva gāyatryā praṇavena vā || 6.275 ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet |
arthamātraṃ vadet kiṃcid vaktavyaṃ yadi vidyate || 6.276 ||
dhyānena dinaśeṣaṃ yat tato ʼvyagraḥ samāpayet |
lohitārkām upāsīta saṃdhyām ā tārakodayāt || 6.277 ||
tāvad dhyāyet punar yāvan nidrāvaśam upāgataḥ |
suptotthitaḥ punar dhyāyet tiṣṭhan saṃdhyāṃ punar japet || 6.278 ||
bharadvājaḥ |
amṛtāpidhānam asīti culukaṃ gṛhītvā praṇavena dantadhāvanaṃ karoti | ucchiṣṭam annaṃ pātraṃ cāpsu prakṣipyācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācamya vāṅma āsann iti japitvā āpo hi ṣṭheti mārjayitvā ud vayaṃ tamasas parity upasthānaṃ kuryāt | praṇavena pātraṃ prakṣālayet | gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet | govālaṃ gṛhītvā praṇavenaiva śuṣkaṃ pātraṃ mārjayet | diśaḥ pradiśa iti bandhanaṃ kṛtvā gāyatryā sthāpayet | lohitārkam upāsīta | evaṃ yathoktavidhānena bhojanaṃ kṛtvā bhikṣuḥ parāṃ gatiṃ prāpnotīti || 6.279 ||
atha sāyaṃsaṃdhyopāsanam | tatra śaunakaḥ |
snānādi sāyam evaṃ syāt samāpya sakalaṃ vidhim |
arghyaṃ ca pūrvavad dadyāl lohitāyati bhāskare || 6.280 ||
vāruṇībhir upasthānaṃ tataḥ saṃdhyāvidhikriyā |
dhyāyann āsīta caitāvad yāvan nidrāvaśaṃ gataḥ |
dhyāyet punaḥ prabuddhaś ced brahmabhūyāya kalpate || 6.281 ||
rātrivasanadeśaṃ hārīta āha |
bhuktvā pātre yatir nityaṃ kṣālayen mantrapūrvakam |
na duṣyet tasya tat pātraṃ yajñeṣu camasaṃ yathā || 6.282 ||
athācamya niruddhāsur upatiṣṭheta bhāskaram |
japadhyānetihāsais tu dinaśeṣaṃ samāpayet |
kṛtasaṃdhyas tato rātriṃ nayed devagṛhādiṣu || 6.283 ||
śāṇḍilyaḥ |
vedaṃ vihāya karmāṇi sarvāṇy eva tu saṃnyaset |
grāmād bahiḥ kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vṛkṣamūle ʼpi vā vaset || 6.284 ||
kratuḥ |
śūnyāgāre guhāyāṃ vā vahnyāgāre surālaye |
vased asaṅgo niḥśaṅko nirmalo yogam āsthitaḥ || 6.285 ||
khaṭvādiniṣedham āstaraṇaṃ sthaṇḍilaśāyitvaṃ vāsadeśaṃ ca vasiṣṭha āha |
ekaśāṭīparivṛto ʼjinena vā gopralūnais tṛṇair avastṛtaśarīraḥ sthaṇḍilaśāyī nityaṃ vasatiṃ vaset | grāmānte devagṛhe śūnyāgāre vṛkṣamūle vā manasā svādhyāyam adhīyāno ʼraṇyanityo na grāmyapaśūnāṃ saṃdarśane vicaret || 6.286 || [VaDh 10.9-16]
sa evāha |
na ca tṛpyet | grāme vaset | ajihmo ʼśaṭho ʼsaṅkusukaḥ || 6.287 || [VaDh 10.25-27]
asahavāsam āvasathārhatvānarhatve ca dakṣa āha |
eko bhikṣur yathoktas tu dvāv eva mithunaṃ smṛtam |
trayo grāmaḥ samākhyāta ūrdhvaṃ tu nagarāyate || 6.288 ||
nagaraṃ tu na kartavyaṃ grāmo vā mithunaṃ tu vā |
etat trayaṃ prakurvāṇaḥ svadharmāc cyavate yatiḥ || 6.289 ||
rājavārtādi teṣāṃ ca bhikṣāvārtā parasparam |
snehapaiśunyamātsaryaṃ saṃnikarṣād bhaviṣyati || 6.290 ||
tapojapaiḥ kṛśībhūtā vyādhitā vasathārhakāḥ |
vṛddhā grahagṛhītāś ca ye cānye vikalendriyāḥ || 6.291 ||
nīrujaś ca yuvā caiva bhikṣur nāvasathārhakaḥ |
vasann āvasathe bhikṣur maithunaṃ yadi sevate |
sa dūṣayati tat sthānaṃ vṛddhān saṃpīḍayed api || 6.292 ||
kapilas tu sahavāsam anumodate |
guruvṛddhatapasvina āgatān abhivadet | caturbhujavat paśyet | karaṇe yathāśakti saṃvibhāgaśīlatāpi praśastā |anudvegakāribhis tapasvibhiḥ samānaśīlaiḥ saha saṃvaset
|| 6.293 ||
śaunakaḥ |
śaraṇaṃ na kuryān na ca kārayet | kṛtaṃ praviśet | atha brahmopāsīta | taccitta eva śayīta | anindake devagṛhe vā praviṣṭo yathākathaṃcic charvarīṃ nayet jāgrad eva suṣuptaḥ | āsīno ʼthācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet | kāṣṭhāropitakamaṇḍalunā rātrāv ācāmet divā sravantyā | abhirucitaṃ prabhūtodakaṃ puṇyasthānaṃ na tyajet | na tīrthalolupaḥ || 6.294 ||
vāyuḥ |
niśānte ʼbhyuddhṛtā āpaḥ śuddhe pātre yathāvidhi |
ahorātroṣitās tās tu tyājyā medhyā api svayam || 6.295 ||
atha grāsavāsādinimittaṃ bhikṣāv āgate gṛhasthasya kartavyam ucyate | tatra vṛddhajābāliḥ |
caturvedo ʼpi yo vipraḥ somayājī śatakratuḥ |
tasmād api yatiḥ śreṣṭhas tilameruvad antaram || 6.296 ||
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ vāpi yatiṃ dṛṣṭvā tyajen na yaḥ |
sa mūḍhātmā mṛtas tasmāt tiryagyoniṣu jāyate || 6.297 ||
durvṛtte vā suvṛtte vā yatau nindāṃ na kārayet |
yatīnāṃ dviṣyamāṇas tu narakaṃ pratipadyate || 6.298 ||
dattātreyaḥ |
tridaṇḍarūpadhṛg vipraḥ sākṣān nārāyaṇātmakaḥ |
yas taṃ pūjayate bhaktyā viṣṇus tena prapūjitaḥ || 6.299 ||
saguṇo nirguṇo vāpi vratajyeṣṭho yuvāpi saḥ |
saṃpūjyo ʼbhikṣubhir bhikṣuḥ svadharme saṃsthito yatiḥ || 6.300 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa mantreṇa nityaṃ nārāyaṇātmanā |
namasyo bhaktibhāvena viṣṇurūpī tridaṇḍadhṛk || 6.301 ||
tridaṇḍaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ liṅgaṃ viprāṇāṃ muktisādhanam |
nirvāṇaṃ sarvadharmāṇām iti vedānuśāsanam || 6.302 ||
kāṇādaśākyapāṣaṇḍais trayīdharmo vilopitaḥ |
tridaṇḍadhāriṇā pūrvaṃ viṣṇunā rakṣitā trayī || 6.303 ||
jamadagniḥ |
śuṣkam annaṃ pṛthak pākaṃ yataye yaḥ prayacchati |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti tena pāpena karmaṇā || 6.304 ||
yatiś ca brahmacārī ca pakvānnasvāmināv ubhau |
tayor annam adatvā tu bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 6.305 ||
yatihaste jalaṃ dadyād bhaikṣaṃ dadyāt punar jalam |
tad bhaikṣaṃ meruṇā tulyaṃ taj jalaṃ sāgaropamam || 6.306 ||
yatir yasya gṛhe bhuṅkte tatra bhuṅkte hariḥ svayam |
harir yasya gṛhe bhuṅkte tatra bhuṅkte jagat trayam || 6.307 ||
saṃcitaṃ yad gṛhasthasya pāpam āmaraṇāntikam |
nirdahiṣyati tat sarvam ekarātroṣito yatiḥ || 6.308 ||
apacantaṃ parityajya pacantaṃ yas tu bhojayet |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca vṛthā tasya pariśramaḥ || 6.309 ||
aduṣṭāpatitaṃ sādhuṃ yo yatiṃ parivarjayet |
sa tasya sukṛtaṃ datvā duṣkṛtaṃ pratipadyate || 6.310 ||
durvṛtto vā suvṛtto vā mūrkhaḥ paṇḍita eva vā |
kāṣāyadaṇḍamātreṇa yatiḥ pūjyo yudhiṣṭhira || 6.311 ||
atriḥ |
bhaikṣadātā sadā bhikṣoḥ salilaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
datvā bhikṣāṃ prayaccheta pātre savye kare sthite || 6.312 ||
tṛptyarthaṃ pitṛdevānāṃ tatpradāne ʼmbu kalpitam |
tadannenaiva dattena bhavet tad upapāditam || 6.313 ||
bhaikṣaṃ saṃskṛtya yo dadyād viṣṇurūpāya bhikṣave |
kṛtsnāṃ vā pṛthivīṃ dadyāt tena tulyaṃ na tad bhavet || 6.314 ||
pātrāsanaṃ tathā daṇḍaṃ śikyaṃ jalapavitrakam |
saṃnyāsine pradāyaiva godānaphalam āpnuyāt || 6.315 ||
vyādhitānāṃ ca bhikṣūṇāṃ kuryād āśramamaṇḍapam |
pathyādyair vā paricaret so ʼnantaphalam aśnute || 6.316 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
pṛthakkṛtā tu yā bhikṣā ghrātā mārjāramūṣakaiḥ |
vātopavātā śuṣkānnā madhumāṃsasamā bhavet || 6.317 ||
paryuṣitā tu yā bhikṣā yā ca naivedyakalpitā |
sā gṛhṇato na doṣāya dātā tu narakaṃ vrajet || 6.318 ||
bhikṣoḥ pāṇau jalaṃ dadyād bhaikṣaṃ dadyāt punar jalam |
sā bhikṣā parvatais tulyā taj jalaṃ sāgaropamam |
evaṃ dattaṃ tu yadb haikṣaṃ tena tṛpyanti devatāḥ || 6.319 ||
jābāliḥ |
yatihastagataṃ dravyaṃ yo gṛhṇāti naraḥ kvacit |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti tena pāpena karmaṇā || 6.320 ||
tridaṇḍam appavitraṃ ca bhaikṣabhājanam āsanam |
saṃnyāsine dadāno hi gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 6.321 ||
pātraṃ caturvidhaṃ yuktaṃ yo dadāti tridaṇḍine |
pūrayitvā haviṣyānnair ātmānaṃ ca nivedayet || 6.322 ||
ye ke ca narakasthāne pacyante ʼpy atipāpinaḥ |
uddharaṃś cātmanātmānaṃ sa gacchet paramāṃ gatim || 6.323 ||
yāvanti yatipātrāṇi yāvanty annāni tatra ca |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate || 6.324 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye ahorātrakriyā nāma
ṣaṣṭhaṃ parva || 6.325 ||
saptamaṃ parva
ācāraḥ
athācāraḥ | tatra manuḥ |
anagnir aniketaḥ syād grāmam annārtham āśrayet |
upekṣako ʼsaṃcayiko munir bhāvasamanvitaḥ || 7.1 || [MDh 6.43]
kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucelam asahāyatā |
samatā caiva sarvasminn etan muktasya lakṣaṇam || 7.2 ||[MDh 6.44]
nābhinandeta maraṇaṃ nābhinandeta jīvitam |
kalam eva pratīkṣeta nirveśaṃ bhṛtako yathā || 7.3 || [MDh 6.45]
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vaded vācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret || 7.4 || [MDh 6.46]
ativādāṃs titikṣeta nāvamanyeta kaṃcana |
na cainaṃ deham āśritya vairaṃ kurvīta kenacit || 7.5 || [MDh 6.47]
krudhyantaṃ na pratikrudhyed ākruṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ vadet |
saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācam anṛtāṃ vadet || 7.6 || [MDh 6.48]
adhyātmaratir āsīno nirapekṣo nirāmiṣaḥ |
ātmanaiva sahāyena sukhārthaṃ vicared diva || 7.7 || [MDh 6.49]
na cotpātanimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit || 7.8 || [MDh 6.50]
na tāpasair brāhmaṇair vā vayobhir api vā śvabhiḥ |
ākīrṇaṃ bhikṣukair vānyair agāram upasaṃvrajet || 7.9 || [MDh 6.51]
abhipūjitalābhāṃs tu jugupsetaiva sarvaśaḥ |
abhipūjitalābhais tu yatir mukto ʼpi badhyate || 7.10 || [MDh 6.58]         alpānnābhyavahāreṇa rahaḥsthenāsanena ca |
hriyamāṇāni viṣayair indriyāṇi nivartayet || 7.11 || [MDh 6.59]
indriyāṇāṃ nirodhena rāgadveṣakṣayeṇa ca |
ahiṃsayā ca bhūtānām amṛtatvāya kalpate || 7.12 || [MDh 6.60]
avekṣeta gatīr nṝṇāṃ karmadoṣasamudbhavāḥ |
niraye caiva patanaṃ yātanāṃ ca yamakṣaye || 7.13 || [MDh 6.61]
viprayogaṃ priyaiś caiva saṃprayogam athāpriyaiḥ |
jarayā cābhibhavanaṃ vyādhibhiś copapīḍanam || 7.14 || [MDh 6.62]
dehād utkramaṇaṃ cāsmāt punar garbhe ca saṃbhavam |
yonikoṭisahasreṣu sṛtīś cāsyāntarātmanaḥ || 7.15 || [MDh 6.63]
adharmaprabhavaṃ caiva duḥkhayogaṃ śarīriṇām |
dharmārthaprabhavaṃ caiva sukhasaṃyogam akṣayam || 7.16 || [MDh 6.64]
sūkṣmatāṃ cānvavekṣeta yogena paramātmanaḥ |
deheṣu caivopapattim uttameṣv adhameṣu ca || 7.17 || [MDh 6.65]
viṣṇuḥ |
mānāvamānau yāv etau prītyudvegakarau nṛṇām |
tāv eva viparītārthau yoginaḥ siddhikārakau || || 7.18 ||
mānāvamānau yāv etau tāv evāhur viṣāmṛte |
avamāno ʼmṛtaṃ tatra mānas tu paramaṃ viṣam || 7.19 ||
uttaratra |
yathā caivāvamanyante janāḥ parivadanti ca |
tathā yuktaś cared yogī satāṃ dharmam adūṣayan || 7.20 ||
punas tatraiva |
sārabhūtam upāsīta yena muktim avāpnuyāt |
kriyānyā bahudhā yā tu yogavighnakarī hi sā || 7.21 ||
kāmyāni yāni karmāṇi pratiṣiddhāni yāni ca |
tāni tāni parityajya nityaṃ karma samācaret || 7.22 ||
yamaḥ |
yaś cāsya dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ candanenānulimpati |
savyaṃ vāsya ca yas takṣet samau tāv avadhārayet || 7.23 ||
evam asya bhaved bhāvo nityam eva priyāpriye |
iṣṭe vā yadi vā dveṣye samaḥ sarvatra vartayet || 7.24 ||
samaḥ sādhau ca pāpe ca śatrau mitre ca vai samaḥ |
śītoṣṇasukhaduḥkheṣu samaḥ saṅgavivarjitaḥ || 7.25 ||
na prahṛṣyati sanmāne nāvamāne ca kupyati |
rāgadveṣau samau yasya sa muniḥ sarvaniḥspṛhaḥ || 7.26 ||
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || 7.27 ||
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo bhavet |
na ca vākcapalaś ca syād iti śiṣṭasya lakṣaṇam || 7.28 ||
tathā |
annāc chuddhāgamāc chuddhād viśeṣarasavarjitāt |
nirmalaṃ jāyate sattvaṃ malinaṃ malinād bhavet || 7.29 ||
māṃsaśoṇitasaṃghātaṃ medomajjāsthisaṃcayam |
nakharomasamāyuktaṃ vasāprabhṛtikardamam || 7.30 ||
pūrṇaṃ mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ durgandhaṃ carmabandhanam |
deho ʼnilamayaṃ dhāma malādhāram asārakam || 7.31 ||
citrabhittipratīkāśam apāṃ phenopamaṃ param |
nityānityasamāyuktaṃ rāgadveṣasamuccayam || 7.32 ||
ekastambhaṃ navadvāraṃ tristhūṇaṃ pāñcabhautikam |
prītiprākārasaṃyuktaṃ manaḥprabalanāyakam || 7.33 ||
daśendriyabhaṭākīrṇaṃ vyādhiśvāpadasevitam |
viśeṣaviṣayākrāntaṃ cetanādhiṣṭhitaṃ puram || 7.34 ||
jarāśokasamāviṣṭaṃ rogāyatanam āturam |
rajasvalam anityaṃ ca bhūtāvāsam imaṃ tyajet || 7.35 ||
snānaṃ japaṃ tathā dhyānaṃ śaucam āhāralāghavam |
ya etat pañcakaṃ kuryān mukto bhavati nānyathā || 7.36 ||
purāṇaśravaṇābhyāsaṃ bhaktito nityam ācaret |
purāṇaśravaṇe bhaktir mūrkhasyāpi prajāyate || 7.37 ||
bhaktyāvicchinnayā muktis tasmātpaurāṇam abhyaset |
tadabhyāsāt paraṃ brahmabhāvam āpadyate muniḥ iti || 7.38 ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
parivrājakaḥ sarvabhūtābhayaṃ datvā pratiṣṭheta | [VaDh 10.1]
tathāpy udāharanti |
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo datvā carati yo muniḥ |
tasyāpi sarvabhūtebhyo na bhayaṃ jātu vidyate || 7.39 || [VaDh 10.2]
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo ʼdatvā yas tu nivartate |
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca [dravyāṇi pratigṛhya ca] || 7.40 || [VaDh 10.3]
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṃ tapaḥ |
upavāsāt paraṃ bhaikṣaṃ dayā dānād viśiṣyate || 7.41 || [VaDh 10.5]
tathottaratra |
avyaktaliṅgo ʼvyaktācāro ʼnunmatta unmattaveṣaḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
na śabdaśāstrābhiratasya mokṣo
na lokacittagrahaṇe ratasya |
na bhojanācchādanatatparasya
na caiva ramyāvasathapriyasya || 7.42 || [VaDh 10.18–20]
nādhyetavyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ na śrotavyaṃ kathaṃcana |
etaiḥ pūrvaṃ suniṣpanno yatir bhavati nānyathā || 7.43 ||
pārivrājyaṃ gṛhītvā tu yaḥ svadharme na tiṣṭhati |
śvapadenāṅkayitvā taṃ rājā rāṣṭrāt pravāsayet || 7.44 ||
uttaratra|
lābhapūjānimittaṃ hi vyākhyānaṃ śiṣyasaṃgrahaḥ |
ete cānye ca bahavaḥ prapañcāḥ kutapasvinām || 7.45 ||
dhyānaṃ śaucaṃ tathā bhikṣā nityam ekāntaśīlatā |
bhikṣoś catvāri karmāṇi pañcamaṃ nopapadyate || 7.46 ||
atrātmavyatirekeṇa dvitīyaṃ yadi paśyati |
tataḥ śāstrāṇy adhīyante śrūyate granthavistaraḥ || 7.47 ||
agniṃ ātmani saṃsthāpya dvijaḥ pravrajito bhavet |
vedābhyāsaparo nityam ātmavidyāparāyaṇaḥ || 7.48 ||
saṃsevya cāśramān sarvān jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ |
brahmalokam avāpnoti vedaśāstrārthavid dvijaḥ |
āśrameṣu tu sarveṣu proktaḥ prāṇāntiko vidhiḥ || 7.49 ||
śātātapaḥ |
ārūḍho naiṣṭhikaṃ dharmaṃ yas tu pracyavate yatiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ na paśyāmi yena śuddhyet sa ātmahā || 7.50 ||
pārivrājyaṃ gṛhītvā tu gṛhastho yo bhavet punaḥ |
ṣaṣṭir varṣasahasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ || 7.51 ||
iha pūrvaṃ pratijñāya saṃnyāsaṃ na karoti yaḥ |
mithyāvacanadagdho ʼsau so ʼndhe tamasi majjati || 7.52 ||
yas tu pravrajito bhūtvā punaḥ seveta maithunam |
ṣaṣṭir varṣasahasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ || 7.53 ||
śūnyāgāreṣu ghoreṣu bhavaty ākhuḥ sudāruṇaḥ |
so ʼcirāj jāyate garbhe śvā syād dvādaśajanmasu || 7.54 ||
bhāso viṃśativarṣāṇi navavarṣāṇi sūkaraḥ |
apuṣpo viphalo vṛkṣo jāyate kaṇṭakāvṛtaḥ || 7.55 ||
tato dāvāgninā dagdhaḥ sthāṇur bhavati kāmukaḥ |
tato varṣaśatāny aṣṭau dve ca tiṣṭhaty acetanaḥ || 7.56 ||
pūrṇe varṣasahasre tu jāyate brahmarākṣasaḥ |
kṣutpipāsāpariśrāntaḥ kravyādo rudhirāśanaḥ |
krameṇa labhate mokṣaṃ kulasyotsādanena ca || 7.57 ||
saṃnyāsaṃ pātayed yas tu patitaḥ saṃnyasec ca yaḥ |
saṃnyāsavighnakartā ca trayas te patitāḥ smṛtāḥ || 7.58 ||
bindulā nāma caṇḍālāḥ parivrājakarūpiṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ jātāny apatyāni caṇḍālaiḥ saha vāsayet ||7.59 ||
hārītaḥ |
vidhinā buddhipūrveṇa yaḥ saṃnyasya gṛhī bhavet |
sa evārūḍhapatitaḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || 7.60 ||
pārivrājyaṃ parityajya gṛhastho yo bhavet punaḥ |
ṣaṣṭir varṣasahasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ || 7.61 ||
ityādiparaṃ śātātapeyapāṭhavat |
ārūḍhapatito hanyād daśa pūrvān daśāparān |
tān eva tārayed vipro yatiḥ samyag vyavasthitaḥ || 7.62 ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena tridaṇḍaṃ dhārayed yatiḥ |
neha bhūyaḥ sa jāyeta yatidharmasya pālanāt || 7.63 ||
sarveṣām āśramāṇāṃ tu tridaṇḍī hy uttamāśramī |
sa evāsya namasyaḥ syād bhaktyā sanmārgavartinaḥ || 7.64 ||
tridaṇḍarūpadhṛg vipraḥ sākṣān nārāyaṇātmakaḥ |
yas taṃ pūjayate bhaktyā viṣṇus tena prapūjitaḥ || 7.65 ||
aṣṭākṣareṇa mantreṇa nityaṃ nārāyaṇātmanā |
namasyo bhaktibhāvena viṣṇurūpī tridaṇḍadhṛk || 7.66 ||
evaṃ vaḥ kathito viprāḥ sanmārgo ʼyaṃ sanātanaḥ |
prāpte kaliyuge ghore ito ʼnye kṣudraliṅginaḥ || 7.67 ||
saṃbhaviṣyanti te sarve gautamāgnividīpitāḥ |
vedārthaṃ cānyathā sarvaṃ pravakṣyanti kalau yuge || 7.68 ||
nagnāḥ pracchannabauddhāś ca vinindanti trayīmayam |
rātrau vedarahasyāni paṭhiṣyanty alpamedhasaḥ || 7.69 ||
bhakṣayiṣyanti śūdrānnaṃ vayaṃ muktā iti sthitāḥ |
anyān api svadharmasthān prerayiṣyanti te kalua || 7.70 ||
ekaveṇuṃ samāśritya jīvanti bahavo dvijāḥ |
narake raurave ghore karmatyāgāt patanti te || 7.71 ||
tasmāt tān nālapet prājño na nirīkṣeta vāpi tān |
ālokane ca teṣāṃ tu śudhyate bhānudarśanāt || 7.72 ||
nagnaṃ vānupanītaṃ vā yaḥ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇi sa pitṛbhyaḥ prayacchati || 7.73 ||
preraṇād atha vā lobhād ekadaṇḍaṃ samāśritaḥ |
bhūyo nirvedam āpannas tridaṇḍaṃ dhārayed budhaḥ || 7.74 ||
parāśaraḥ |
dvāv imau puruṣau yāto bhittvā vai sūryamaṇḍalam |
parivrāḍ yogayuktaś ca raṇe cābhimukho hataḥ || 7.75 ||
saṃnyastaṃ taṃ dvijaṃ dṛṣṭvā sthānāc calati bhāskaraḥ |
eṣa me maṇḍalaṃ bhittvā paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 7.76 ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kāmakrodhādikān doṣān varjayed yāvadāyuṣam |
evaṃ saṃvartayan nityaṃ brahma saṃpadyate yatiḥ || 7.77 ||
uśanāḥ |
tyaktasaṅgo jitakrodho laghvāhāro jitendriyaḥ |
pidhāya buddhyā dvārāṇi mano dhyāne niveśayet || 7.78 ||
vyāsaḥ |
yat pūrvam abhinirdiṣṭam ātmajñānaṃ mayā purā |
tat sarvaṃ yatnato ʼbhyasyet sarvavit saṅgavarjitaḥ || 7.79 ||
lokayātrāṃ prayatnena varjayed ātmacintakaḥ |
nānopaniṣado yatnād abhyasyen muktihetukīḥ || 7.80 ||
āpastambaḥ |
atha parivrājakaḥ | ata eva brahmacaryāt parivrajati | tasyopadiśanti | anagnir aniketaḥ syād aśarmāśaraṇo muniḥ | svādhyāya evotsṛjamāno vācaṃ grāme prāṇavṛttiṃ pratilabhyāniho ʼnamutraś caret || 7.81 || [ApDh 2.21.7–10]
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ātyantikaṃ brahmacaryam āśramatrayavāsinām |
svadāraniyamād eva gṛhastho brahmacaryavān || 7.82 ||
uttaratra |
na jñātiputramitrādīn na praśastān dvijātiṣu |
deśāntare ʼpi tān dṛṣṭvā na ca taiḥ saha saṃviśet |
avaśyaṃ bhāvinī tatra rāgadveṣādiṣu sthitiḥ || 7.83 ||
tathaiva |
na tīrthavāsī nityaṃ syān nopavāsaparo yatiḥ |
na cādhyāpanaśīlaḥ syān na vyākhyānaparo bhavet || 7.84 ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
candanenokṣitaṃ bāhuṃ vāsyaikaṃ vāpi takṣataḥ |
kalyāṇaṃ vāpy akalyāṇaṃ tayor api na cintayet |
sarvabhūtahito maitraḥ samaloṣṭhāśmakāñcanaḥ || 7.85 ||
likhitaḥ |
nānadhyātmagrantharatir na ca jyautiṣiko bhavet |
na vyādhiviṣavetālāṃś cikitsed dharmato ʼpi ca || 7.86 ||
na śiṣyasaṃgrahaṃ kuryān na svavidyāṃ prakāśayet |
saṃskṛtāṃ na vaded vāṇīṃ bālavan mūkavac caret || 7.87 ||
uttaratra |
saṃnyasya yatidharmāṃś ca kurvāṇo vatsaratrayam |
yoganiṣṭhaḥ sadā bhūtvā mokṣaṃ pretya sa vindati || 7.88 ||
tasmād aiśvaryam ākāṅkṣet tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam |
kaivalyāśramam āsādya cared dinam api dvijaḥ || 7.89 ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
bhaikṣavṛttir asaṃvibhāgarucir abhiśastapatitavarjaṃ nindakopahatānnajīvī kaupīnācchādanamātrāvaraṇaparituṣṭaḥ paripakvakaṣāyaḥ kālakāṅkṣī sadāyukta ātmaratir asaṃnidhānaśīlaḥ kuhakakalkadambhavarjitaḥ sadā naikagrāmavāsī saṅgadoṣān parityajya paryaṭanaśīlaḥ syāt | ātmajñānavivekārthakuśala ūrdhvaṃ vārṣikābhyāṃ naikasthānavāsābhirucir uddhṛtābhir adbhir mūtrapurīṣaśaucācamanādi kuryāt
|| 7.90 ||
tathā |
vāgbāṇān durjanair muktān sahate yo na kupyati |
bhāvena ca susaṃhṛṣṭaḥ sa damas tasya buddhijaḥ || 7.91 ||
śītoṣṇādīni ca dvandvān durjanaprahitāni ca |
yathā dhārayate buddhyā sā titikṣā kṣamā smṛtā || 7.92 ||
iṣṭāniṣṭeṣu bhūteṣu vyasanābhyudayeṣu ca |
upaśāntaḥ parāṃ prajñāṃ prāptaḥ śamadamātmakaḥ || 7.93 ||
tapojñānadamaiśvaryaiḥ sarvadā yo na kupyati |
vāgbāṇābhihatas tīkṣṇair dhīraḥ so ʼtra na muhyati || 7.94 ||
yo yatir mṛdubhāvena mokṣadharmaṃ cared iha |
bhavabandhaṃ vihāyāśu so ʼmṛtatvāya kalpate || 7.95 ||
tathaiva |
cāturvarṇyaṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ patiteṣu ca varjayet |
niyatendriyavākcakṣuḥ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayet || 7.96 ||
vṛddhadakṣaḥ |
vāgdaṇḍo ʼtha manodaṇḍaḥ karmadaṇḍas tathāparaḥ |
etair daṇḍais tu daṇḍī syān na tu vaiṇavadhāraṇāt || 7.97 ||
vāgdaṇḍo maunasaṃyogaḥ karmadaṇḍas tv ahiṃsanam |
manasaḥ sa tu daṇḍaḥ syāt prāṇāyamasya dhāraṇam || 7.98 ||
kapilaḥ |
svastyāśiṣaṃ varjayet | śaraṇaṃ na kuryān na kārayet | kṛtaṃ praviśet | atha brahmopāsīta | taccitta eva śayīta | anindake devagṛhe vā praviṣṭo yathā kathaṃcic charvarīṃ nayet jāgrad eva suṣuptaḥ | āsīno ʼthācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet | kāṣṭhāropitakamaṇḍalunā rātrāv ācāmet divā sravantyā | abhirucitaṃ prabhūtodakaṃ puṇyasthānaṃ na tyajet | na lolupo netihāsapurāṇavyākhyānaparaḥ || 7.99 ||
uttaratra |
nirjantukaiḥ svapatitair indhanair asahiṣṇur agnim indhīta | bījaśūnyamūlaphalabhakṣaṇe na doṣaḥ | cetanācetanam alpaprayojanāya na druhyet | kamaṇḍalujalapavitrakusīda-taijasādisaṃgrahe na doṣaḥ | kopito ʼpi na kupyet | toṣito ʼpi na tuṣyet | martuṃ necchen na jīvituṃ vā | adagdhaṃ pātraṃ na tyajet | śaucārthaṃ pūtikākaraṇāt kṣaṇam api sadyaḥ saṃnihitodake japanīyaṃ japet | tataś caturbhujaṃ cintayet | naikadevatāpakṣapātitvaṃ darśayet | saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ sāśrayaṃ śaraṇaṃ saṃtyajet | surūpāṃ striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā na kvacit prekṣeta | saṃkocayed akṣiṇī | om iti naiva kampayet || 7.100 ||
tathaiva |
sarvendriyāṇāṃ saṃkoco dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakaro yateḥ |
apramattena kartavyaḥ sukham atyantam icchatā || 7.101 ||
gītānāṃ gānaśīlasya prāṇāyāmaparasya ca |
naivokṣanti ca pāpāni janmāntarakṛtāny api || 7.102 ||
kratuḥ |
bījaghnaṃ taijasaṃ pātraṃ śuklotsargaḥ sitāmbaram |
divāsvāpo vṛthājalpo yatīnāṃ patanāni ṣaṭ || 7.103 ||
cirasthitāni dravyāṇi saṃcayādīni sarvadā |
tṛṇamṛddārutoyāśmapatramūlaphalāni ca |
aprayuktaṃ na gṛhṇīta prayuktaṃ na ca saṃcayet || 7.104 ||
dāsīdāsaṃ gṛhaṃ yānaṃ gobhūdhānyadhanaṃ rasam |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ hanyāt kulaśatadvayam || 7.105 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
ātithyaśrāddhayajñeṣu devayātrotsavādiṣu |
kutūhaleṣu sarveṣu na gacched yatir ātmavān || 7.106 ||
uttaratra |
yas tu dānaparo bhikṣur vastrādīnāṃ ca saṃgrahī |
ubhau tau mandabuddhitvāt pūtīnarakapātinau || 7.107 ||
dattātreyaḥ |
ādāya vaiṣṇavaṃ liṅgaṃ yas tu tyajati mūḍhadhīḥ |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ tīvraṃ saṅkaṭadāruṇam || 7.108 ||
devalaḥ |
tulyārimitro niṣparigraho brahmacārī maṅgalyavyavahāro saṃskāro jīvaśikṣāratnadhanadhānyaviṣayopabhogasaṃparkerṣyādarpamāyāmohaharṣa-virodhavismayavivādatrāsavitarkādivarjanam iti yatidharmāḥ || 7.109 ||
āhāram auṣadhaṃ caiva sadgṛhasthagṛhe yatiḥ |
pratyahaṃ caiva bhikṣeta yathā śeṣaṃ na tiṣṭhati || 7.110 ||
āhāram auṣadhaṃ caiva tatkālārthādhikaṃ yatiḥ |
ātmārthaṃ dhārayen mohān narakaṃ pratipadyate || 7.111 ||
ādadīta pravṛttebhyaḥ sādhubhyo dharmasādhanam |
nādadīta nivṛttebhyaḥ pramādād api kiṃcana || 7.112 ||
gargaḥ |
sarvadharmān parityajya yatidharmaṃ samāśrayet |
yatidharmāt paribhraṣṭo na dharmaphalam aśnute || 7.113 ||
vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
puṣpamūlaphalapāṭanāgracchedanamadhumāṃsadānādānāni varjayet || 7.114 ||
medhātithiḥ |
tridaṇḍaṃ kuṇḍikāṃ śikyaṃ bhaikṣabhājanam āsanam |
kaupīnācchādanaṃ vāsaḥ ṣaḍ etāni parigrahaḥ || 7.115 ||
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ bījaṃ taijasaṃ viṣam āyudham |
ṣaḍ etāni na gṛhṇīyād yatir mūtrapurīṣavat || 7.116 ||
bhikṣāṭanaṃ japaḥ snānaṃ dhyānaṃ śaucaṃ surārcanam |
kartavyāni ṣaḍ etāni sarvadā nṛpadaṇḍavat || 7.117 ||
rasajñānaṃ kriyāvādo jyotiṣaṃ krayavikrayau |
vividhāni ca śilpāni varjayet paradāravat || 7.118 ||
naṭādiprekṣaṇaṃ dyūtaṃ pramadāṃ suhṛdas tathā |
bhakṣyabhojyam udakyāṃ ca ṣaṇ ṇa paśyet purīṣavat || 7.119 ||
skandhāvāre pure sārthe khale goṣṭhe tathā gṛhe |
na vaseta yatiḥ ṣaṭsu sthāneṣv eteṣu karhicit || 7.120 ||
rātryadhvānaṃ ca yānaṃ ca strīkathā laulyam eva ca |
mañcakaṃ śuklavastraṃ ca yatīnāṃ patanāni ṣaṭ || 7.121 ||
rāgadveṣau madaṃ mānaṃ dambhaṃ drohaṃ parātmasu |
ṣaḍ etāni yatir nityaṃ manasāpi na cintayet || 7.122 ||
āsanaṃ pātralopaś ca saṃcayaḥ śiṣyasaṃgrahaḥ |
divāsvāpo vṛthājalpo yater bandhakarāṇi ṣaṭ || 7.123 ||
ekāhāt parato grāme pañcāhāt parataḥ pure |
varṣābhyo ʼnyatra yat sthānam uktaṃ tad duṣṭam āsanam || 7.124 ||
uktānāṃ yatipātrāṇām ekasyāpi na saṃgrahaḥ |
bhikṣor bhaikṣabhujaś cāpi pātralopaḥ sa ucyate || 7.125 ||
pātrasyāpi tridaṇḍāder dvitīyasya parigrahaḥ |
kālāntaropabhogārthaṃ so ʼpi syād dravyasaṃcayaḥ || 7.126 ||
śuśrūṣālābhapūjārthaṃ yaśo ʼrthaṃ vā parigrahaḥ |
śiṣyāṇāṃ na tu kāruṇyāt sa jñeyaḥ śiṣyasaṃgrahaḥ || 7.127 ||
vidyā divā prakāśatvād avidyā rātrir ucyate |
vidyābhyāsapramādo yaḥ sa divāsvāpa ucyate || 7.128 ||
ādhyātmikāṃ kathāṃ muktvā bhikṣāyātrāṃ surastutim |
anugrahaṃ pathipraśnaṃ vṛthājalpo ʼnya ucyate || 7.129 ||
ajihvaḥ ṣaṇḍhakaḥ paṅgur andho badhira eva ca |
mugdhaḥ sa ucyate bhikṣuḥ ṣaḍbhir etair na saṃśayaḥ || 7.130 ||
idaṃ mṛṣṭam idaṃ neti yo ʼśnann api na sajjate |
idaṃ tīkṣṇam idaṃ svādu tam ajihvaṃ pracakṣate || 7.131 ||
adyajātāṃ yathā nārīṃ tathā ṣoḍaśavārṣikīm |
śatavarṣāṃ ca yo dṛṣṭvā nirvikāraḥ sa ṣaṇḍhakaḥ || 7.132 ||
bhikṣārtham aṭanaṃ yasya viṇmūtrakaraṇāya ca |
yojanān na paraṃ yāti ya sadā paṅgur eva saḥ || 7.133 ||
tiṣṭhato vrajato vāpi yasya cakṣur na dūragam |
caturyugād bhayaṃ muktvā parivrāṭ so ʼndha ucyate || 7.134 ||
hitāhitaṃ manorāmaṃ vacaḥ śokāvahaṃ ca yat |
śrutvā yo na śṛṇotīva badhiraḥ sa tu kīrtitaḥ || 7.135 ||
saṃnidhau parakīyāṇāṃ samauno ʼvikalendriyaḥ |
suptavad vartate yo ʼpi sa bhikṣur mugdha ucyate || 7.136 ||
śraddhayā parayopetaḥ paramātmaparāyaṇaḥ |
sthūlasūkṣmaśarīrābhyāṃ mucyate daśaṣaṭkavit || 7.137 ||
jamadagniḥ |
dhānyavṛkṣalatāvallīḥ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā |
utpāṭayed yo mūḍhātmā narakaṃ pratipadyate || 7.138 ||
ekānnī dvipratī caiva bhaikṣajīvas trisaṃgrahī |
catvāraḥ patitā hy ete yatayo nātra saṃśayaḥ || 7.139 ||
tāmbūlābhyañjane caiva kāṃsyapātre ca bhojanam |
yatiś ca brahmacārī ca vidhavā ca vivarjayet || 7.140 ||
dve ete samavīrye tu surā tāmbūlam eva ca |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena tāmbūlaṃ varjayed yatiḥ || 7.141 ||
śaunakaḥ |
na svaguṇaṃ kīrtayet | trāsito ʼpi na kupyet | pūjito ʼpi na mudaṃ vrajet | triṣavaṇaṃ daṇḍavat snānam ācaret | tathā na laṅghayeta nadīṃ na bāhubhyāṃ taret | mṛtyuṃ jīvitaṃ vā na kāṅkṣet | rātrau na caret saṃdhyayoś ca | tāmbūlabhakṣaṇaṃ varjayet | abhyaṅgaṃ dvirannaṃ na lipseta | ātithyaśrāddhayajñeṣu devayātrotsaveṣu na gacchet | sārthasamavāyeṣu vānaprasthagṛhasthābhyāṃ prītiṃ ca varjayet || 7.142 ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
sthūyamāno na tuṣyeta | nindyamāno na kupyet | samam eva varteta vidyayā tapasā ca tad ubhayaṃ prāpyeti || 7.143 ||
satyakāmaḥ |
sarvadharmān parityajya mokṣadharmaṃ samāśrayet |
mokṣadharmāt paribhraṣṭaḥ saṃskāraṃ pratipadyate || 7.144 ||
nādṛṣṭāṃ bhūmim ātiṣṭhen nāsūryaṃ mārgam āvrajet |
na hiṃsyāt sarvabhūtāni karmaṇā manasā girā || 7.145 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye ācāro nāma
saptamaṃ parva..
aṣṭamaṃ parva
liṅgadharmaprāyaścittavidhiḥ
atha liṅgadharmā vapanādayaḥ saprāyaścittā ucyante | tatra śaunakaḥ |
śikhāvarjam ṛtusaṃdhiṣu vāpayet | na romāṇi vāpayet || 8.1 ||
devalaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ sitacaturdaśyāḥ pratipatto ʼdharā tithiḥ |
ṛtusaṃdhiḥ sa vijñeyo vapanaṃ tatra kārayet || 8.2 ||
likhitaḥ |
ṛtusaṃdhiṣu sarvatra cāturmāsyāntaraṃ vinā |
paurṇamāsyāṃ śikhāvarjaṃ muṇḍayeta śiro yatiḥ || 8.3 ||
alābhe vyādhipīḍāyāṃ rājacorādyupadrave |
vāpayet paurṇamāsyādi yāvat syāt kṛṣṇapañcamī || 8.4 ||
ṛtusaṃdhikālaḥ sitacaturdaśyā ūrdhvaṃ pratipadyadharā tithiḥ yā sā jñeyā | aparapakṣādi-pratipatpañcadaśyoḥ saṃdhimadahorātraṃ yat tasmin vapanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 8.5 ||
devalaḥ |
cāturmāsyānantaraṃ keśaśmaśrūṇy apanayet nādhoromāṇi || 8.6 ||
manuḥ |
kṛttakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ pātrī daṇḍī kusumbhavān |
vicaren niyato nityaṃ sarvabhūtāny apīḍayan || 8.7 || [MDh 6.52]
devalaḥ |
mauṇḍyaṃ śikhāvikalpo vā yadi lumpet pramādataḥ |
prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 8.8 ||
gālavaḥ |
tristhānalomavapanaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt || 8.9 ||
jamadagniḥ |
kakṣopasthādivapane prāyaścittī bhaved yatiḥ |
prājāpatyatrayaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaśatāni ca || 8.10 ||
idaṃ kakṣopasthādivapanasamuccaye prāyaścittam | medhātithiḥ |
kakṣopasthaśikhāvarjam ṛtusaṃdhiṣu vāpayet |
na traimuṇḍyam atikrāmed bhikṣuḥ saṃvatsare kvacit || 8.11 ||
vapanānantaraṃ snātvā mṛdbhir dvādaśabhir yatiḥ |
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā vāruṇībhiś ca mārjayet || 8.12 ||
snātvottīrya ca śeṣeṇa jaṅghāśaucaṃ mṛdambhasā |
apavitrīkṛto bhikṣuḥ kaupīnasrāvivāriṇā || 8.13 ||
kuryān mūtrapurīṣe ced dviguṇaṃ snānam ācaret |
muhūrtadvayam asnātvā piban khādaṃś ca ṣaḍguṇam || 8.14 ||
gṛhītaṃ yadi daṇḍādyam avinaṣṭaṃ tu na tyajet |
samudraṃ gaccha svāheti vinaṣṭaṃ prakṣipej jale || 8.15 ||
prakṣipya cānyad ādadyād gāyatryā praṇavena vā |
tac chaṃ yor ity upavītam abliṅgābhiḥ kamaṇḍalum || 8.16 ||
kuṇḍikām iṣumātrordhvāṃ nikṣipec ca vihāyasi |
sodakāṃ rajjusaṃlagnām anyathā duṣyate yatiḥ || 8.17 ||
araṇye trīn iṣukṣepān tridaṇḍena vinā vrajet |
na duṣyed duṣyati grāme gṛhād gṛham api vrajan || 8.18 ||
tathā |
pragṛhītān tridaṇḍādīn avinaṣṭān yadi tyajet |
ekarātroṣitaḥ kuryāt prāṇāyāmaśatāni ca || 8.19 ||
gālavaḥ |
vidyamānena jalapavitreṇācamanatarpaṇopasthānāni na karoti yaḥ tasya sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet | tasya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate | prāṇāyāmāṃs triśataṃ kṛtvā japet trikaśatatrayam iti | jalapavitraṃ vināpaḥ prāśya daśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet || 8.20 ||
atriḥ |
gandhavarṇarasair duṣṭam asvacchaṃ keśadūṣitam |
vinā vastraṃ pibann ambhaḥ prāṇāyāmān daśācaret || 8.21 ||
tatra |
uddhṛtāsu sravantīṣu hradeṣu vimaleṣu ca |
vastrapūtā na kartavyā āpaḥ śuddhā iti śrutiḥ ||
iti tenaiva pūrvam eva parihṛtam [see 6.44] || 8.22 ||
gālavaḥ |
jalapavitrādānaṃ bandhanaṃ ca praṇavena kuryāt | tridaṇḍajalapavitraviyogaṃ na kuryāt | pramādāt karaṇe ācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet | snānabhojanakāle tu tridaṇḍaviyogaṃ na kuryāt | pramādāt karaṇe ācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet | prāgudakśirasaṃ viṣṇoḥ sthānama sīti tridaṇḍaṃ sthāpayet | adhaḥpatane uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspata iti japet | anyadaṇḍeṣu daṇḍān spṛṣṭvā viṣṇor manasā pūtestha iti prakṣālya prāṇāyāmāṃs trīn kuryāt | ajñānāt karaṇe praṇavena prakṣālya praṇavaṃ daśadhāvartayet || 8.23 ||
gargaḥ |
daṇḍasaṃgharṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā vanaspate viḍvāṅga iti japitvā viṣṇor manasā pūtestha iti prakṣālya prāṇāyāmāṃs trīn kuryāt | ajñānāt kṛtvā praṇavena prakṣālayet | praṇavaṃ daśakṛtva āvartayet || 8.24 ||
gālavaḥ |
kamaṇḍalujalapavitrapātrasaṃgharṣaṇe sadyas tyāgo vidhīyate || 8.25 ||
atriḥ |
pātrasnehasaṃgharṣaṇaṃ na kuryāt | ajñānāt kṛte ʼpavitratāṃ vrajet || 8.26 ||
sa evāha |
abhinnam aduṣṭaṃ vā pātraṃ tridaṇḍādīn vā tyaktvā ahorātram upoṣya gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet | bhinnapātraṃ dhārayitvopavāsatrayaṃ caret || 8.27 ||
jamadagniḥ |
śaucabhede śataṃ kuryāt pātrabhede tathaiva ca |
bhinne tv anyadhṛte pātre upavāsatrayaṃ caret || 8.28 ||
uttaratra |
pūyaśoṇitamāṃsais tu viṇmūtrābhyāṃ tathāpi vā |
retoʼśruśleṣmabhiḥ spṛṣṭaṃ pātraṃ yadi yatis tyajet || 8.29 ||
śvakākākhugavoṣṭrāṃś ca jambukaṃ vānaraṃ tathā |
caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ spṛṣṭvā hastasthaṃ pātram utsṛjet || 8.30 ||
majjāmūtrapurīṣais tu śuklakīlālaśoṇitaiḥ |
spṛṣṭaṃ daṇḍaṃ tyajed bhikṣuḥ śeṣaṃ saṃskāram arhati || 8.31 ||
na daṇḍena vinā grāme gṛhād gṛham api vrajet |
prāṇāyāmāṃs tathā kuryād vane cādhvavyatikrame || 8.32 ||
yady adaṇḍo gṛhaṃ gacched vanaṃ vā trīṣumātrataḥ |
nīḍaṃ pratinivarteta prāṇāyāmāṃs trayastrayaḥ || 8.33 ||
tridaṇḍāsanapātrāder na viyogaḥ kathaṃcana |
viyogāt patate bhikṣuḥ prāyaścittaṃ tad ucyate || 8.34 ||
tridaṇḍasya viyoge tu prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret |
śatārdhaṃ kuṇḍikāyāḥ syāt pātre vai ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ || 8.35 ||
pavitre cāṣṭa vijñeyāḥ śikye ṣaṭ ca prakīrtitāḥ |
catvāraś cāsane jñeyāḥ pavitrīkaraṇaṃ tv idam || 8.36 ||
satyakāma-jābālau |
atha bhikṣūṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | pātraṃ krośamātraṃ tyaktvā vrajati cet prāyaścittena yujyate | triṃśat prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā japet trikaśatatrayam | tridaṇḍaṃ pātrāsanaṃ vā tyaktvā samudragāyā nadyāḥ pāraṃ vrajati ced upavāsatrayaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 8.37 ||
uttaratra |
strīśūdre daṇḍāntaraṃ gate prāṇāyāmāṃs trīn kuryāt | kharoṣṭre ʼntaraṃ gate prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret | śvānasūkaracaṇḍāleṣv antaraṃ gateṣu prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet | vihitātikramaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet || 8.38 ||
dakṣaḥ |
daṇḍān pātraṃ parityajya ekarātroṣito yatiḥ |
daṇḍān mantreṇa gṛhṇīyād bhikṣāpātraṃ parityajet || 8.39 ||
atriḥ |
yadi bhūmau kṣiped daṇḍān pātraṃ vāpi kadācana |
ṣoḍaśāsuyamān kuryād aṣṭau ṣaḍ dvādaśāpi vā || 8.40 ||
devalaḥ |
daṇḍādau dūṣite viṣṭhākravyādādyair mahāmune |
kiṃ tatra vada kartavyaṃ saṃskāro yena jāyate || 8.41 ||
daṇḍān mṛdbhir dvādaśabhir adbhiḥ saṃśodhya yatnataḥ |
japtvātha pauruṣaṃ sūktam adbhiḥ saṃśodhayed api |
itareṣāṃ tu rājendra sadyas tyāgo vidhīyate || 8.42 ||
śuklocchiṣṭāśruviṇmūtraśleṣmaśūdragavānalaiḥ |
tyajet pātrapavitrādīn spṛṣṭvā vā taskarādibhiḥ || 8.43 ||
gṛhya śūdrādivarṇebhyaḥ kramād daṇḍādikaṃ yatiḥ |
śataṃ śatārdhaṃ pañca ca prāṇāyāmān daśāpi ca || 8.44 ||
atriḥ |
upānacchatravastrādi bhikṣāyāḥ sādhanaṃ tathā |
gṛhya śūdrāc chataṃ kuryād vaiśyād ardhaśataṃ tathā |
kṣatriyāt pañcaviṃśac ca hīnaviprād daśaiva tu || 8.45 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
tridaṇḍam appavitraṃ ca tyaktvā bhikṣur gṛhāntare |
japet puruṣasūktaṃ ca prāṇāyāmān daśācaret || 8.46 ||
devalaḥ |
gṛhasthaḥ parapākāśī pātralopī ca bhikṣukaḥ |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi tiryagyoniṣu jāyate || 8.47 ||
pātrābhāve yater nityaṃ pātraṃ parṇapuṭaṃ bhavet |
tat pātraṃ tāvad eva syād yāvan mukhyaṃ na labhyate || 8.48 ||
kapilaḥ |
sūcīnakhanikṛntane jalapavitrapātre antarvāsobahir vāsasī cāśaktau dhārayet || 8.49 ||
atriḥ |
tyaktvā yajñopavītaṃ tu ṣaṭ kṛcchrāṇi samācaret |
gāyatrīsahitāny eva punaś cāpy upanāyayet || 8.50 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
hīno yajñopavītena yadi syād dhyānabhikṣukaḥ |
tasya kriyā niṣphalāḥ syuḥ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || 8.51 ||
gāyatrīsahitān eva prājāpatyān ṣaḍ ācaret |
punaḥ saṃskāram āhṛtya dhāryaṃ yajñopavītakam || 8.52 ||
kuryāt tridaṇḍagrahaṇaṃ kālakṣepaṃ na kārayet |
evaṃ na yāti narakaṃ sugatiṃ cāpi gacchati || 8.53 ||
idam eva yajñopavītatyāgātmakaṃ pārivrājyaṃ kṛtavatāṃ prāyaścittam | hārītaḥ |
vinā yajñopavītena yat kṛtaṃ bhojanādikam |
tatpāpapraśamāyālaṃ prāyaścittaṃ bravīmi vaḥ || 8.54 ||
teṣāṃ tu bhojanādīnāṃ pratyahaṃ susamāhitaḥ |
sahasrārdhair asuyamair vidhipūrvakṛtaiḥ śuciḥ || 8.55 ||
yogābhyāsaratasyeha naśyante pātakāni tu |
tasmād yogarato bhūtvā dhyāyen nityaṃ kriyāntare || 8.56 ||
sa evāha |
ajñānena tu viprendro vinā yajñopavītakam |
kṛtvā mūtrapurīṣāṇi prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 8.57 ||
nityaṃ yajñopavītī syād ṛte tv āvaśyakakriyām |
kurvann āvaśyakaṃ karma nivītaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṣipet || 8.58 ||
atha vastram ucyate | tatrāha gautamaḥ |
kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ vāso bibhṛyāt | prahīṇam eke nirṇijya || 8.59 || [GDh 3.18–19]
bṛhaspatiḥ |
kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ ca bibhṛyāj jīrṇam ambaram |
prahīṇam eke nirṇijya bahis tyājyaṃ tu yad bhavet || 8.60 ||
hārītaḥ |
kaupīnācchādanaṃ vāsaḥ kanthāṃ śītanivāriṇīm |
pāduke cāpi gṛhṇīyāt kuryān nānyasya saṃgraham || 8.61 ||
jābāliḥ |
kārpāsaṃ dhārayed vastraṃ paṭaṃ valkalavarjitam
nānyathācamane śudhyet saṃdhyopāsana eva ca |
kāṣāyam eva kārpāsaṃ vāsaḥ kanthāṃ ca dhārayet || 8.62 ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tridaṇḍapavitrapāṇir viparidhānaṃ kuśacīravalkalavāsāḥ śītatrāṇārthaṃ kambalam eke
|| 8.63 ||
yady api kāṣāyam ity aviśeṣeṇoktaṃ tathāpi gairikākhyadhātunā raktaṃ kartavyam | tathā hi pārivrājyaliṅgadhāraṇaṃ gṛhasthasya pratiṣedhayann etaj jñāpayati vṛddhajābāliḥ |
na dhārayed dhātuvastraṃ śikyaṃ cāpi na dhārayet |
na dhārayet triviṣṭabdham etat snātakalakṣaṇam || 8.64 ||
dattātreyaḥ |
mokṣāśrame smṛtaṃ vastraṃ raktaṃ gairikadhātunā |
dhāryaṃ yajñopavītaṃ tu sitaṃ navaguṇānvitam || 8.65 ||
etāsu vihitāsv api mātrāsu saṅgo na kartavyaḥ | tathā ca devalaḥ |
samamṛtkāñcanaḥ svamātrāsv apy asakto madhyasthaḥ || 8.66 ||
manuḥ |
mātrāsaṅgādvinirgataḥ iti || 8.67 ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
na kuṭyāṃ nodake saṅgo na cele na tripuṣkare |
nāgāre nāsane nānne yasya vai mokṣavit tu saḥ || 8.68 || [VaDh 10.23]
iti yatidharmasamuccaye liṅgadharmaprāyaścittavidhir nāma
aṣṭamaṃ parva || 8.69 ||
navamaṃ parva
gatisthitinirūpaṇam
atha yatīnāṃ gatisthitir nirūpyate | tatra gautamaḥ |
ūrdhvaretā dhruvaśīlo varṣāsu || 9.1 || [GDh 3.11–12]
medhātithiḥ |
udite ʼnyatra madhyāhne anyatrāstamite ravau |
dṛśyate tv aniketaḥ syāt sūryavat saṅgavarjitaḥ || 9.2 ||
devālayāgnyāgāreṣu tv ekadeśe niśāṃ nayet |
anyatra maraṇatrāsān mahataś cāpy upadravāt || 9.3 ||
vārṣikāṃś caturo māsān bahirgrāmāt samāpayet |
tatrāpy upakrame hetū pūrvoktāv anivāritau || 9.4 ||
atra varṣāsv ekatra vastavyam anyathā na vastavyam iti pratīyate | tatrālpānugrahaṃ manur āha |
graiṣmahaimantikān māsān aṣṭau prāyeṇa paryaṭet |
dayārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ varṣāsv ekatra saṃvaset || 9.5 ||
atra prāyeṇeti sūcitam anugrahaṃ kratur viśinaṣṭi |
grāme dinaṃ pure trīṇi nagare pañca sapta vā |
tiṣṭhed ekatra varṣāsu vrajet sūryodite pathi || 9.6 ||
likhitaḥ |
pare brahmaṇi saṃnyasya kurvan karmāṇi śaktitaḥ |
yogābhyāsapradhānas tu japaśīlaḥ kṣamāparaḥ || 9.7 ||
paryaṭet puṇyatīrthāni devatāyatanāni ca |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādīni kuryāc chuddhyartham ātmanaḥ || 9.8 ||
anandho ʼvyādhito ʼpaṅguḥ ṣaḍrātraṃ na vaset kvacit |
dharmadhīr anyadeśāc ced vased vā sārvakālikam || 9.9 ||
śrāvaṇaṃ māsam ārabhya yāvan māsacatuṣṭayam |
vaseyuḥ sarvayataya ekatraiveti niścayaḥ || 9.10 ||
devalaḥ |
na ciram ekatra vased anyatra vārṣikāt | śrāvaṇādicaturmāsiko vārṣikaḥ | tasmin na yāyī syāt || 9.11 ||
gālavaḥ |
śūnyāgāre vṛkṣamūle vā varṣāsv ekatra nivaset || 9.12 ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
varṣābhyo ʼnyatrāpy ekatra vased eva || 9.13 ||
vṛddhamanuḥ |
dharmavṛddho tv ā maraṇād ekatrāpi vaset | anyatra vrajanaśīlaḥ syād ity ācāryāḥ || 9.14 ||
prakāraṃ deśaṃ yama āha |
grāmaikarātravāsī syān nagare pañcarātrikaḥ |
ekatra nivasen māsāṃś caturo vārṣikān muniḥ || 9.15 ||
jale jīvāḥ sthale jīvā ākāśe jīvamālikā |
jīvamālikākule loke varṣāsv ekatra saṃvaset || 9.16 ||
adhvā sūryasya nirdiṣṭaḥ kīṭavac ca caren mahīm |
aśvastananidhānas tu sumatiḥ susamāhitaḥ || 9.17 ||
bhayaṃ prāṇāntikaṃ tyaktvā viṇmūtrotsargam eva ca |
nānyatra vicared rātrau na madhyāhne na saṃdhyayoḥ || 9.18 ||
ekākī vicaren nityaṃ muktātmā tv asahāyakaḥ |
ekasya hi samaḥ panthā jāyate ʼnyatra jīyate || 9.19 ||
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
sūnṛtām īrayed vācaṃ sarvasattvahitaṃ caret || 9.20 ||
ekaśāṭy ekabhaktaś ca ekapātrī ca bhikṣukaḥ |
ekaikaṃ vicared grāmaṃ tat purāṇam ṛṣivratam || 9.21 ||
nityaṃ sthaṇḍilaśāyī syād anityāṃ vasatiṃ vaset |
araṇyanityo muṇḍī syān manasā dhyānam abhyaset || 9.22 ||
bhaikṣadeśaṃ nṛpajñātiputramitrādiyoṣitaḥ |
upakārakathāś caiva manasāpi na cintayet || 9.23 ||
āgaccha gaccha tiṣṭheti svāgataṃ suhṛdaṃ prati |
saṃmānam api na brūyān munir mokṣaparāyaṇaḥ || 9.24 ||
yatīnāṃ vratavṛddhānāṃ svadharmam anuvartinām |
viṣṇurūpeṇa vai kuryān namaskāraṃ dine dine || 9.25 ||
yo bhavet pūrvasaṃnyāsī tulyo vā dharmato yatiḥ |
namaskārārcanādīni kuryān nānyasya kasyacit || 9.26 ||
svadharmasthān yatīn vṛddhān devāṃś ca praṇamed yatiḥ |
nānyam āśramiṇaṃ kaṃcit praśastam api taṃ namet || 9.27 ||
sāyaṃ prātar na tṛpyeta na prasajjeta bhojane |
nendriyārtheṣu sajjeta kadācid api kāmataḥ || 9.28 ||
panthānam api pṛcchec ca bhikṣāṃ dehīti ca dvayam |
tāvad eva vyāharaṇaṃ śeṣam anyan niṣidhyate || 9.29 ||
atriḥ |
gūḍhadharmāśrito vidvān ajñānacaraṇe sthitaḥ |
amūḍho mūḍharūpeṇa cared dharmam adūṣayan || 9.30 ||
kapilaḥ |
vṛddhāturavad yathākathaṃcit sthitiṃ saṃpādayet || 9.31 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
naivādadīta pātheyaṃ yatir vai yad anāpadi |
pakvam āpatsu gṛhṇīyād yāvad ahnopayujyate || 9.32 ||
jamadagniḥ |
araṇye nirjane deśe coravyāghrākule pathi |
kṛtvā mūtrapurīṣaṃ ca dravyahasto na duṣyati || 9.33 ||
bhūmau nidhāya tad dravyaṃ śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi |
utsaṅge gṛhya tad dravyam ācāntaḥ prayataḥ śuciḥ || 9.34 ||
gālavaḥ |
yojanādhikagamane dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet || 9.35 ||
atha cāturmāsyakāla ucyate | āṣāḍhyāṃ paurṇamāsyām ārabhya kārttikyām utthānam | atha vā śrāvaṇyām ārabhya mārgaśīrṣyām | anye tv evam āhuḥ | varṣāsu dhruvaśīla ity anena ṛtuvidhānāt māsadvayena vratasamāptir iti || 9.36 || jābāliḥ |
graiṣmahaimantikān māsān aṣṭau bhikṣur viparyaṭet |
dayārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ varṣāsv ekatra saṃvaset || 9.37 ||
cāturmāsyān vased grāme śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ |
sthitibhaṅgam akurvan hi śāśvataṃ padam āpnuyāt || 9.38 ||
yāvad varṣaty akāle ʼpi yāvat klinnā ca medinī |
grāme sīmādhikaṃ neyād vistīrṇe krośataḥ param || 9.39 ||
medhātithiḥ |
praśastāṃ mṛttikāṃ śuṣkāṃ gṛhṇīyāc chuciśukrayoḥ |
dvādaśyāṃ mṛdam ādāya śubhāṃ kiṃcid apīḍayan || 9.40 ||
yāvad varṣaty akāle ʼpi yāvat klinnā ca medinī |
tāvan na vicared bhikṣuḥ svadharmam anupālayan |
āṣāḍhādi vasen māsāṃś caturaḥ kārttikodayāt || 9.41 ||
atriḥ |
cāturmāsyavidhiṃ vakṣye yatīnāṃ niyatātmanām |
yam āśritya sadā mukto bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 9.42 ||
śuklamāsādikaḥ kālo yāvad āṣāḍhapūrṇimā |
tatra puṇye ʼhni kasmiṃścit saṅkalpadivase ʼpi vā || 9.43 ||
śarkarāśmalatāvallīloṣṭādirahitāṃ śubhām |
śuṣkāṃ sādhāraṇīṃ bhūmiṃ gatvā sasikatāṃ yatiḥ || 9.44 ||
smaran varāharūpeṇa dhārayantaṃ mahīṃ harim |
sarvabhūtādhivāsena vāsudevena pāvanīm || 9.45 ||
adhivāsitāsi devi tvaṃ praṇavena svayaṃbhuvā |
pavitratvāya yasmāt tvaṃ devadevena nirmitā || 9.46 ||
praṇavena tathā pṛthvī devi tvam adhivāsitā |
adhivāsya kṣamām evaṃ mantraiḥ sapraṇavais tataḥ || 9.47 ||
aśvakrānta ity ārabhya pade pade, uddhṛtāsi, bhūmir dhenuḥ, mṛttike hana, tvayā hatena, mṛttike dehi, gandhadvārām, īśvarīm || 9.48 ||
yenoddhṛtāsi devi tvaṃ krodharūpeṇa daṃṣṭriṇā |
tvayā saha sa māṃ pātu keśavo dharaṇīdharaḥ || 9.49 ||
ādhāraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tvaṃ devi vasudhe smṛtā |
pālanī pāvanī dhātrī sarvabhūtābhayaṃkarī || 9.50 ||
viṣṇukrāntāsi devi tvaṃ viśvakrāntāsi medinī |
pāhi māṃ sarvabhūtebhyo viṣṇunā saha mādhavī |
iti vijñāpya yuktātmā mādhavīṃ lokadhāriṇīm || 9.51 ||
mṛttike brahmadattāsi kāśyapenābhimantritā |
mṛttike jahi tat sarvaṃ yan mayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam || 9.52 ||
idaṃ viṣṇur iti dvābhyāṃ śubhāṃ gṛhṇīta mṛttikām |
atha darbhakaras teṣu cāsīnaḥ prāṅmukho yatiḥ |
vārṣikaṃ vratam etat tu cāturmāsikam āharet || 9.53 ||
mādhavaś caturo māsān sarvabhūtahitāya vai |
svāpaṃ yāsyati śeṣāṅke lakṣmyā saha jagatpatiḥ || 9.54 ||
suptaś caivotthito yāvan na bhavet sa sanātanaḥ |
ahaṃ tāvan nivatsyāmi sarvabhūtahitāya vai || 9.55 ||
dhruvā dyaur ity ṛcaṃ japtvā tato bhūmiṃ ca saṃspṛśet |
anenaiva vidhānena āṣāḍhe māsi bhikṣukaḥ || 9.56 ||
dvādaśyāṃ śuklapakṣasya paurṇamāsyām athāpi vā |
uttarāṣāḍhayuktāyāṃ yadi khaṇḍā tu pūrṇimā || 9.57 ||
mṛdaṃ gṛhītvedaṃ viṣṇur iti tām ānayen mṛdam |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa tīrthān saṃpūjayet tataḥ || 9.58 ||
namaskṛtya vidhānena vṛddhānukramato yatīn |
jyāyase yataye dadyād yavīyān mṛttikāṃ yatiḥ || 9.59 ||
piṇḍam ekam atha dvau vā mantreṇānena pūjayet |
gṛhāṇa mṛttikāṃ bhikṣo sarvapāpaharāṃ śivām || 9.60 ||
yuṣmān asmān iyaṃ devī viṣṇunā saha rakṣatu |
mṛdam itthaṃ gṛhītvā tāṃ tayā snānādi sādhayet || 9.61 ||
āhṛtya mṛttikām evaṃ tatkālahṛtayā mṛdā |
kuryān mṛcchaucakarmāṇi yāvan māsacatuṣṭayam || 9.62 ||
śvobhāvinīṃ pratipadam ārabhya śrāvaṇīṃ yatiḥ |
na prayāyāc caturmāsān vapanaṃ kārayen na ca || 9.63 ||
ārambhotthānadinayor vāpayed vapane ʼpi tu |
adhaḥkaṇṭhaśikhākṣibhrūromāṇi na hi jātucit || 9.64 ||
dvādaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vānārambho vighnato yadi |
sthānābhāvād vrajet tāvad yāvat syāt kṛṣṇapañcamī || 9.65 ||
prāyaścittena yujyeta tadūrdhvaṃ hi vrajan yatiḥ |
kṛcchram ekaṃ cared bhikṣur yadi vyājena gacchati || 9.66 ||
vistīrṇe grāmasīmānte krośād ūrdhvaṃ vrajed yadi |
triṃśat prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā japet trikaśatatrayam || 9.67 ||
idaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tasminn evāhani punaḥ pratyāgatasya | tatraiva rātrim adhyuṣitasya tu prāyaścittaṃ vṛddhajābālir āha |
grāme sīmādhikaṃ neyād vistīrṇe krośataḥ param |
yadi kaścit pramādena krośamātrādhikaṃ vrajet |
prājāpatyena śudhyeta ṣoḍaśāsuyamais tathā || 9.68||
vāyuḥ |
varṣābhedaṃ tu yaḥ kuryād brāhmaṇo yogadīkṣitaḥ |
taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhātmā virajā jāyate punaḥ || 9.69 ||
śārīraṃ dṛśyate yatra bhayaṃ kasyāṃścid āpadi |
durbhikṣe rāṣṭrabhaṅge vā varṣāsv api tam utsṛjet || 9.70 ||
corair upadrutaṃ deśaṃ durbhikṣavyādhipīḍitam |
cakreṇānyena vākrāntaṃ varṣāsv apy āśu taṃ tyajet || 9.71 ||
atriḥ |
varṣartau tu vinā vighnaṃ śaraṇaṃ saṃtyajed yadi |
japet trikasahasraṃ vai prāṇāyāmaśataṃ tathā || 9.72 ||
varṣākāle vinā vighnaṃ viśet sthānāntaraṃ yadi |
japet trikasahasraṃ vai prāṇāyāmaśatatrayam || 9.73 ||
sthitiyogyān bahūn grāmān yadi varṣāsu laṅghayet |
pratyekaṃ tu caret kṛcchraṃ tataḥ pāpāt pramucyate || 9.74 ||
samudrajyeṣṭhādibhir mantraiś caturbhiḥ śirasodakam |
caturdiśaṃ jalair vāpaḥ saṃ naḥ śiśīhi mantrataḥ || 9.75 ||
kṣurābhimantraṇaṃ kuryād vyādhitasyāturasya ca |
āpañcamyāḥ praśastāḥ syur yateḥ kṣaurādikāḥ kriyāḥ || 9.76 ||
dvādaśyāṃ kārttike māsi paurṇamāsyām athāpi vā |
kṛtavāpo mṛdaṃ śiṣṭām urv iti prakṣipej jale || 9.77 ||
śvobhūte ʼtha trayodaśyāṃ pratipady api vā kvacit |
utthāyeṣṭaṃ vrajed deśaṃ tadā trīṇi padety ṛcā || 9.78 ||
mahi trīṇām ṛcas tisro ye te panthāna ity api |
japitvaitā ṛco gacched ārabhya prāg udak ca vā || 9.79 ||
gautamaḥ |
na dvitīyām apartu rātriṃ grāme vaset || 9.80 || [GDh 3.21]
apagate ṛtau dvitīyāṃ rātriṃ grāme na vaset | ekasmin grāme dinadvayaṃ na vased ity arthaḥ | grāmagrahaṇād araṇye na doṣa iti maskarī | na dvitīyām apartu rātrim iti yady api ṛtur aviśeṣitaḥ tathāpi vārṣiko grāhyaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ vārṣikābhyāṃ naikasthānaṃ vidhīyata iti śaṅkhaḥ | yau ca śrāvaṇabhādrapadau aśaktasya tau | catvāro māsāḥ devalaḥ |
na ciram ekatra vased anyatra vārṣikāt | śrāvaṇādayaś catvāro māsā vārṣikāḥ [see 6.11] |
iti viśvarūpaḥ || 9.82 ||
satyakāma-jābālau |
grāme nagare pure vā prāpte varṣākāle sthitim eva karoti bhikṣus tasminn eva sthāne ūrdhvakālaṃ kṣaṇam api sthitiṃ na kuryāt | ajñānāt sthitiṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret
|| 9.83 ||
punar atriḥ |
japitvaitā ṛco gacched ārabhya prāg udak ca vā |
grāmaikarātraṃ vihared dīrgham āsīta vā kvacit |
andhavaj jaḍavac cāpi badhironmattamūkavat || 9.84 ||
nāmagotrādicaraṇaṃ deśaṃ vaṃśaṃ śrutaṃ kulam |
vayo vṛttaṃ vrataṃ śīlaṃ khyāpayan naiva siddhyati |
prakhyāpanād yater hāniḥ puṇyasya syān na saṃśayaḥ || 9.85 ||
gūḍhadharmāśrito vidvān ajñātacarite sthitaḥ |
amūḍho mūḍharūpeṇa cared dharmam adūṣayan || 9.86 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye gatisthitinirūpaṇaṃ nāma
navamaṃ parva || 9.87 ||
daśamaṃ parva
prāyaścittavidhiḥ
atha prāyaścittāny ucyante | tatra vāyuḥ |
prāyaścittāni kāryāṇi yāny akāmakṛtāni ca |
kṛtāni kāmataś cāpi tatra cety apare viduḥ || 10.1 ||
pāpaṃ tu trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyasaṃbhavam |
saṃtataṃ syād ahorātraṃ yenedaṃ dahyate janaḥ || 10.2 ||
na karmāṇi na cāpy āyus tiṣṭhatīti parā śrutiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ kṣaṇenātha prayuñjyād āyuṣi sthite || 10.3 ||
abhyāsād ramate pāpe vardhate dinasaṃkhyayā |
abhyāse buddhipūrve ca prāyaścittena śodhayet |
śodhayet tāvad ātmānaṃ yāvac cittaṃ prasīdati || 10.4 ||
yadi syāt pātakaṃ kiṃcid yogī kuryāt pramādataḥ |
yogam eva niṣeveta nānyāṃ kurvīta niṣkṛtim || 10.5 ||
prāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhyānayogasamanvitaḥ |
naśyet pramādajo doṣo vratair nānyair anuṣṭhitaiḥ || 10.6 ||
abhyāse buddhipūrve ca vratānuṣṭhānaṃ pramādakṛte yoga ity uktam | tad āha hārītaḥ |
yogābhyāsaratasyeha naśyante pātakāni tu || 10.7 || [see 8.56]
atriḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ parākaṃ vā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanādi vā |
na śaknoti yatiḥ kartuṃ sa kuryāt prāṇasaṃyamam || 10.8 ||
yat pāpaṃ naśyate puṃsām ekāhno ʼbhojanena tu |
japāt trikasahasrasya tat pāpaṃ naśyati dhruvam || 10.9 ||
japāt trikasahasrasya yat pāpaṃ nāśayed dvijaḥ |
prāṇāyāmaśatenaiva tat pāpaṃ naśyate ʼkhilam || 10.10 ||
prāṇāyāmasahasreṇa yat pāpaṃ naśyate dvijaiḥ |
kṣaṇamātreṇa tat samyag gharer dhyānāt praṇaśyati || 10.11 ||
prāṇāyāmāj japād dhyānān nānyad bhikṣor viśodhanam |
tasmāt tāny eva kurvīta yatiḥ śuddhyartham ātmanaḥ || 10.12 ||
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ bhikṣur yaḥ karoti dine dine |
pitṛmātṛgurughno ʼpi tribhir ebhir vimucyate || 10.13 ||
jamadagniḥ |
prāṇāyāmaiś ca dhyānaiś ca japaiś ca satataṃ kṛtaiḥ |
dahyante sarvapāpāni pāvakenendhanaṃ yathā || 10.14 ||
vyāsaḥ |
upapātakeṣu sarveṣu pātakeṣu mahatsu ca |
rajanyāḥ pādam ekaṃ tu brahmadhyānena śudhyati || 10.15 ||
gālavaḥ |
īśvaradarśanaṃ sarvaprāyaścittam iti || 10.16 ||
vāyuḥ |
bhaved yogo ʼpramattasya yogo hi paramo mataḥ |
na hi yogāt paraṃ kiṃcin narāṇāṃ vidyate hitam || 10.17 ||
pavitrāṇāṃ pavitraṃ tat pāvanānāṃ ca pāvanam |
tasmād yogaṃ praśaṃsanti tat kṣamasya manīṣiṇaḥ || 10.18 ||
jamadagniḥ |
atha vā yad gurur brūyāt tat kāryam aviśaṅkayā |
nigrahe ʼnugrahe vāpi guruḥ sarvatra kāraṇam || 10.19 ||
vāyuḥ |
vyatikramet tu yaḥ kaścid vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhiḥ |
sabhyāḥ sarve viniścitya yad brūyus tat samācaret || 10.20 ||
atriḥ |
upapātakino bhikṣos tathā laghvaśanaṃ japaḥ |
maunaṃ kālatrayasnānaṃ vihitāni viśeṣataḥ || 10.21 ||
sahasrāsuyamān kṛtvā praṇavasyāyutaṃ japet |
tatas trikasahasrāṇāṃ vidhinā pañcaviṃśatiḥ |
araṇye vratam etat tu kṛtvā bhikṣur viśudhyati || 10.22 ||
atha saṃdhyātikramaprāyaścittam ucyate | tatra devalaḥ |
saṃdhyālopo ʼnāturasya yadi jātaḥ pramādataḥ |
japāt trikasahasrasya śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10.23 ||
gargaḥ |
bhikṣuḥ saṃdhyām atikramya prāyaścittena yujyate |
japet trikasahasraṃ tu prāṇāyāmaśataṃ tathā || 10.24 ||
gālavaḥ |
ardhodita upasthānaṃ kuryāt | kālātikramaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmān dvādaśaiva tu dhārayet
|| 10.25 ||
atha bhikṣāṭanaprāyaścittam | tatra devalaḥ |
upasthānaṃ kṛtvā cirakālaṃ na tiṣṭhet | cirakālaṃ sthitvā prāṇāyāmān ṣoḍaśācaret | upasthānaṃ kṛtvā devatāvṛddhādīnāṃ namaskāraṃ na kuryāt | ajñānāt kṛtvā ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | upasthānaṃ kṛtvā bhikṣāgrahaṃ na vyatikramet | vyatikramaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmāṃs triṃśac caret | upasthānaṃ kṛtvā ekabhikṣāṃ na samācaret | yadi samācaret prājāpatyaṃ samācaret || 10.26 ||
bharadvājaḥ |
saṃbhāṣaṇādīn pariharet | ajñānāt kṛtvā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt | savyenādāya pātraṃ tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe na kvacid api parivartayet | ajñānāt parivartanaṃ kṛtvā trīn prāṇāyāmāṃś caret | savyenādāya pātraṃ dakṣiṇasthatridaṇḍe na saṃśleṣayet | saṃśleṣaṇaṃ yadā karoti tadā prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret | bhikṣārthaṃ copasthānaṃ kṛtvā praviśya nopaviśet | kvacid upaviśan prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet | yadi bhikṣuḥ pramādena śūdrodakyācoditaṃ gṛhṇāti bhaikṣaṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣoḍaśa dhārayet | tridaṇḍaṃ bahiḥ sthāpya bhikṣāṃ na yācayet na ca gṛhṇīyāt | yadi gṛhṇīyāt prāṇāyāmān ṣoḍaśa dhārayet | tridaṇḍaṃ karād vihāya bhikṣāṃ na yācayet na gṛhṇīyāt | ajñānāt kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 10.27 ||
devala-kāśyapau |
śvakharoṣṭrākhumārjārabhāsavānarasūkarān |
nīlīkāṣṭhāgnicayane narāsthi grāmakukkuṭam || 10.28 ||
sṛgālaṃ ca kṛtaghnaṃ ca śūdraṃ madyapam eva ca |
tyaktopavītaṃ nagnaṃ ca pāṣaṇḍānugataṃ dvijam || 10.29 ||
viśrambhaghātinaṃ caiva devabrāhmaṇanindakam |
agāradāhinaṃ caiva dharmavikrayiṇaṃ tathā || 10.30 ||
amedhyanicayaṃ ṣaṇḍhaṃ spṛṣṭvā devalakaṃ yatiḥ |
sthāpya pātraṃ śucau deśe śubhaṃ gacchej jalāśayam || 10.31 ||
śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyaṃ kṣālayec ca mṛdambhasā |
yat syād upahataṃ cāṅgaṃ mṛdbhir dvādaśabhir yatiḥ || 10.32 ||
adbhiḥ saṃśodhya tat sarvaṃ tathā snānaṃ samācaret |
ācamya prayato bhuktvā prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 10.33 ||
āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhir mārjayitvā punaḥ punaḥ |
jale ʼghamarṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 10.34 ||
bhikṣādhāraṃ samādadyāt trikeṇābhyukṣya vāriṇā |
nimajjayet punas toye śuddhyarthaṃ sakare tathā || 10.35 ||
evam eṣāṃ tu saṃsparśe bhikṣā bhikṣor viśuddhyati |
yadi pātre ʼnyasaṃsargas tadā śudhyati nānyathā || 10.36 ||
yadi pātre ʼnyasaṃspṛṣṭiḥ śvakākādibhir antyajaiḥ |
yadā ca carate tatra tadā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 10.37 ||
jamadagniḥ |
śvakākakroḍaveśyāviṭkharoṣṭrasparśane yatiḥ |
sabhaikṣas tu jale magnaḥ śudhyej japtvāghamarṣaṇam || 10.38 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
bhikṣām aṭan yaḥ saṃnyāsī śuno ʼmedhyaṃ ca vā spṛśet |
bhikṣāṃ gṛhītvā snātvā ca prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 10.39 ||
bhikṣāṃ pavitrāṃ kurvīta gāyatryā praṇavena ca |
vāruṇena tu mantreṇa prokṣitaṃ tu tataḥ śuciḥ || 10.40 ||
gargaḥ |
patite pātre bhikṣāṃ gṛhītvā prāṇāyāmāṃs triṃśac caret || 10.41 ||
gālavaḥ |
matsyamāṃsaṃ yad gṛhe pacyate tad gṛhaṃ varjayet | tatra gṛhītvā prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet || 10.42 ||
devalaḥ |
grāmād bahis tathā grāme yadi bhikṣāṃ samācaret |
ubhayatra ca gṛhṇānaś caret sāntapanavratam || 10.43 ||
jamadagniḥ |
grāme praviṣṭe bhikṣārthaṃ yadi bhikṣā na labhyate |
grāme dvitīye bhikṣitvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || 10.44 ||
pātre tu patite bhaikṣe ekabhikṣāṃ samācaret |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāc chudhyarthaṃ yatir ātmanaḥ || 10.45 ||
bhikṣāṃ mādhūkarīm aśnan naivedyam api varjayet |
prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10.46 ||
bhikṣāṃ mādhūkarīm aśnan naivedye tu visarjite |
parato ʼnnam abhuñjānas trirātreṇaiva śudhyati || 10.47 ||
gālavaḥ |
parānnaṃ bhikṣānnaṃ caikasminn ahni bhuktvā gāyatrīm ayutaṃ japet || 10.48 ||
devalaḥ |
pātre kṛtvā nivedyaṃ tu yadi mantraṃ na vinyaset |
japāt trikasahasrasya virajā jāyate yatiḥ || 10.49 ||
udakyāśavacaṇḍālacoranagnakapālinaḥ |
patitān piśunān spṛṣṭvā bhikṣāṃ bhikṣuḥ parityajet || 10.50 ||
patitānnaṃ sakṛd bhuktvā prāyaścittaṃ cared yatiḥ |
vajrakṛcchratrayaṃ kṛtvā śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10.51 ||
yavaprasṛtimātraṃ tu gavāṃ mūtreṇa saṃpacet |
amāvāsyāyām aśnīyād vajrakṛcchram idaṃ mahat || 10.52 ||
prājāpatyaṃ tu vā kṛcchraṃ caret patitabhojane |
ajñānāt tu sakṛd bhuktvā prājāpatyena śudhyati || 10.53 ||
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt pādakṛcchraṃ samācaret |
trirātraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpaṃ vā paktvāḍhyaṃ payasā pibet || 10.54 ||
hārītaḥ |
śaṅkāsthāne samutpanne bhakṣyabhojyasya saṃkare |
āhāraśuddhiṃ vakṣyāmi tan me nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 10.55 ||
akṣāralavaṇāṃ kṛtvā pibed brahmasuvarcalām |
trirātraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpaṃ vā payasā saha pāyayet || 10.56 ||
pālāśabilvapatrāṇi kuśān padmān udumbarān |
kvāthayitvā pibed āpas trirātreṇa viśudhyati || 10.57 ||
kapilāpañcagavyena vidhiyuktena vā punaḥ |
pītvā śuddhim avāpnoti abhakṣyānnasya bhakṣaṇāt || 10.58 ||
atriḥ |
ekānnaṃ madhu māṃsaṃ cāpy annaṃ viṣṭhādidūṣitam |
hantakāram anaivedyaṃ pratyakṣalavaṇāni ca || 10.59 ||
eṣām ekaṃ yatir bhuktvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
madhumāṃsāśanair bhuktvā parākaṃ tu samācaret |
trikasya ca japel lakṣaṃ japaś ced vatsaraṃ japaḥ || 10.60 ||
apaḥprāṇyaṅgabhoktā yo madhumāṃsasya cāpadi |
japet trikasahasraṃ vai prāṇāyāmaśataṃ tathā || 10.61 ||
vṛddhajābāliḥ |
dayāpūrvam adattaṃ ca dattvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
dattvā saumyaṃ mahat kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyam athāpi ca || 10.62 ||
bhikṣur bhuṃkte ya ekānnaṃ dākṣiṇyāl laulyato ʼpi vā |
prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa prāṇāyāmaśatena ca || 10.63 ||
devalaḥ |
vraṇāt krimisamudbhūte bhakṣaṇe madhumāṃsayoḥ |
upavāsatrayaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 10.64 ||
gālavaḥ |
māṃsaṃ madhu vā bhakṣayitvā parākaṃ kuryāt | tāmbūlaṃ bhakṣayitvāhorātram upavaset | buddhipūrve prājāpatyaṃ caret || 10.65 ||
jamadagniḥ |
ekānnaṃ madhu māṃsaṃ ca navaśrāddhaṃ ca sautikam |
abhojyāni yatīnāṃ tu pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ tathā |
ekaikātikrame teṣāṃ prājāpatyena śudhyati || 10.66 ||
kratuḥ |
śvākhumārjārakākānāṃ keśakīṭāsthivāsasām |
dūṣitānnaṃ yatir bhuktvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || 10.67 ||
bharadvājaḥ |
bhojanamadhye yat kiṃcit sūkṣmakeśakīṭamakṣikābhihataṃ cetyādi || 10.68 ||
devalaḥ |
kravyādāśuddhisaṃpāte bhuñjānasya kathaṃcana |
pūrvaṃ tyaktvā bhojanaṃ tu paścāt snānaṃ samācaret |
japet trikasahasraṃ tu prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 10.69 ||
jamadagniḥ |
bhuñjānasya tu viprasya kadācit prasraved gudam |
ucchiṣṭatāśucitvaṃ ca tasya śaucaṃ kathaṃ bhavet || 10.70 ||
pūrvaṃ kṛtvātha śaucaṃ tu tatra paścād upaspṛśet |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || 10.71 ||
bhaikṣaśeṣaṃ tu yat kiṃcid yadi bhikṣuḥ samutsṛjet |
grāse grāse tu kartavyāḥ prāṇāyāmās trayas trayaḥ || 10.72 ||
sakardamaṃ tu varṣāsu praviśya grāmasaṅkaṭam |
jaṅghayor mṛttikās tisraḥ pradadyāt sapta pādayoḥ || 10.73 ||
devalaḥ |
keśaśmaśrugataṃ toyaṃ pātre tu patitaṃ yadi |
tyajed bhikṣāṃ tathā pātraṃ tyaktvā copavased yatiḥ || 10.74 ||
viriktavāntaviṇmūtraskandanaikānnajagdhiṣu |
snātvā bhikṣāṭanād etya prāṇāyāmās trayas trayaḥ || 10.75 ||
jamadagniḥ |
ghrātvā gandhaṃ surāyāś ca tathā mūtrapurīṣayoḥ |
kravyādapūtigandhaṃ vā prāṇāyāmās trayas trayaḥ || 10.76 ||
chardimūtrapurīṣaṃ vā dṛṣṭvā tu sahasā yatiḥ |
prāṇāyāmena śudhyeta kṛtvā taddviguṇaṃ caret || 10.77 ||
gālavaḥ |
pūtiviṇmūtrakravyādasurāgandhāghrāṇaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmāṃs trīṃs trīn kuryāt || 10.78 ||
niyamāṃs tu sadā kuryād yamāṃs tebhyo ʼbhipālayet |
āpatkāle ʼpy aśaktau vā pane ʼpāṃ bheṣajādane || 10.79 ||
haviṣi brāhmaṇecchāyāṃ puṣpamūlaphalādane |
dadhni dugdhe guror vākye tvakpatre dantadhāvane |
niyamātikramo nāsti brāhmaṇānām anujñayā || 10.80 ||
teṣāṃ puṇyāhaghoṣeṇa kāryaṃ pāpāpanodanam |
akāmakṛtapāpebhyas tat kṛtvā vipramucyate || 10.81 ||
bodhāyanaḥ |
atha yatropaniṣadam ācāryā bruvate tatrodāharanti | sthānamaunāsanaṃ savanopasparśanaṃ caturthaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamakālavratayuktatvaṃ kaṇapiṇyākayāvakadadhipayovratatvaṃ ca | tatra maunayuktas traividyavṛddhair ācāryair munibhir anyair vāśramibhir bahuśrutair dantair dantān saṃdhāyāntarmukha eva yāvadarthaṃ saṃbhāṣeta evaṃ na yatra lopo bhavatīti vijñāyate | sthānamaunavīrāsanānām anyatamena saṃprayogo na trayaṃ saṃnipātayet | yatra gataś ced yāvan mātram anuvrajed āpatsu na yatra lopo bhavatīti vijñāyate | sthānamaunavīrāsanopasparśanacaturthaṣaṣṭhāṣṭamakālavratayuktasya || 10.82 ||
aṣṭau tāny avrataghnāni āpo mūlaṃ ghṛtaṃ payaḥ |
havir brāhmaṇakāmāya guror vacanam auṣadham || 10.83 || [BDh 2.18.15–19]
devalaḥ |
yadi kaścit pramādena svāpācāraṃ tu vismaret |
japet trikasahasraṃ tu śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10.84 ||
atha rātrijānām aparādhānāṃ niṣkṛtir ucyate | tatra vṛddhajābāliḥ |
saṃdhyākāle tu saṃprāpte niśi bhuñjīta yo yatiḥ |
upasthānaṃ ca japyaṃ ca prāṇāyāmo balis tathā || 10.85 ||
sarve te niṣphalās tasya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
ekarātropavāsaś ca prāṇāyāmāś ca ṣoḍaśa || 10.86 ||
jamadagniḥ |
āhāragrahaṇe rātrau prāṇāyāmā daśa smṛtāḥ |
jalamṛdgrahaṇe cāpi niśāyāṃ tadvad ācaret || 10.87 ||
gṛhītanāśe dharmeṇa sravantīṣu saritsu ca |
sakṛd gṛhṇan na lipyeta devatāyataneṣu ca || 10.88 ||
rātrau tu kṣaraṇe snātvā dvādaśāsuyamāṃś caret |
prāṇāyāmair viśuddhātmā virajā jāyate yatiḥ || 10.89 ||
śaunakaḥ |
pūrvasaṃbhogān na smaret | pramādāt smaraṇe ācamya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet | pramādāt strīdarśane prāṇāyāmān ācaret | svapnasaṃbhoge trikasahasraṃ japtvā prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet | saṃbhoge svapnakṣaraṇe dvādaśa tu dhārayet | mehamāno dvayaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāmet || 10.90 ||
kapilaḥ |
svapnasaṃbhoge snātvā trikasahasraṃ japet | kaṇḍūyanarudhirakṣaraṇe upavāsaḥ
|| 10.91 ||
gālavaḥ |
kāmād retautsarge trīn parākān dhārayet | akāmataḥ svapne strīsaṃbhoge dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | nidrāyāṃ pramādāt kṣaraṇe dvādaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | prāṇāyāmāṃs trīṃs trīn guhyapramādadarśane dhārayet | svapne etaddarśane aghamarṣaṇam | sadā cāprayatnotsargaṃ kṛtvā retasa ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | prayatnena retaḥsekaṃ kṛtvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 10.92 ||
jābāliḥ |
śuklotsargaṃ yadā kuryāt kāmād abhyāsato ʼpi vā |
parākatrayasaṃyuktaṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 10.93 ||
divā tu skandayed retaḥ prāyaścittī pramādataḥ |
sa trirātropavāsena prāṇāyāmaśataṃ caret || 10.94 ||
skanna indriyadaurbalyāt striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā pramādataḥ |
tena dhārayitavyāḥ syuḥ prāṇāyamās tu ṣoḍaśa || 10.95 ||
vāyuḥ |
śuklotsargaṃ yadā kuryāt kvacid bhikṣus tu kāmataḥ |
prājāpatyatrayaṃ kuryāt prāṇāyāmaśatāni ca || 10.96 ||
vṛddhaparāśaraḥ |
vāsudeva prasupto ʼsi yāvan nodayate raviḥ |
ahaṃ tāvan nivatsyāmi sarvabhūtahitāya vai || 10.97 ||
sapta ṛṣayaḥ pratihitāḥ śarīre
sapta rakṣanti Sadam apramādam |
saptāpaḥ svapato lokam īyus tatra
jāgṛto asvapnajau satradau ca devau || 10.98 || [VS 34.55]
ābhyāṃ mantrābhyāṃ śayīta | yadi na kuryāt prāṇāyāmaṣaṭkaṃ kṛtvā punar api mantrābhyāṃ śayīta | uttiṣṭhety utthānaṃ prātaḥkāle kuryāt | yadi na kuryāt prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā punar uttiṣṭhed iti || 10.99 ||
atha niyamātikrame prāyaścittāni | tatra vāyuḥ |
vratāni yāni bhikṣūṇāṃ tathaivopavratāni ca |
ekaikātikrame teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || 10.100 ||
upetya tu striyaṃ kāmāt prāyaścittī bhaved yatiḥ |
prāṇāyāmasamāyuktaṃ kṛcchram abdaṃ samācaret || 10.101 ||
tataś caritanirvedaḥ kṛcchradhyāne samāhitaḥ |
bhūyo nirvedam āpannaś cared bhikṣām atandritaḥ || 10.102 ||
kapilaḥ |
sakṛtsaṃbhoge dvādaśarātram upavāsaḥ trikalakṣaṃ japaś ca | abhyāse trirātraṃ saṃvatsaraṃ japet | sakṛd antyajāṃ gatvāraṇye bilvādīni svayaṃpatitāni prakṣālyopayuñjīta brahmāvartayan yāvat sa mṛtyutaḥ śuddho bhavati || 10.103 ||
jābāliḥ |
upetya tu striyaṃ kāmāt prāyaścittī bhaved yatiḥ |
prāṇāyāmasamāyuktam abdaṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || 10.104 ||
gālavaḥ |
sakṛt sādhāraṇīṃ gatvā brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ caret || 10.105 ||
atriḥ |
mahāpātakajād doṣāc chuddhyarthaṃ vartayed yatiḥ |
trikalakṣaṃ japed yogī prāṇāyāmāyutaṃ tathā |
tataḥ śatasahasrāṇi praṇavānāṃ japed yatiḥ || 10.106 ||
strīsaṃbhoge sakṛd mohād vidhinānena śudhyati |
kāmato vā tathābhyāse prāyaścittena śudhyati || 10.107 ||
vāyuḥ |
yad etad draviṇaṃ nāma prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ |
sa tasya harati prāṇān yo ʼsya saṃharate dhanam || 10.108 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā sa duṣṭātmā bhinnavṛttāśramo yatiḥ |
tato nirvedam āpannaś carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
vidhinā śāstradṛṣṭena saṃvatsaram iti śrutiḥ || 10.109 ||
tataḥ saṃvatsarasyānte bhūyaḥ prakṣīṇakalmaṣaḥ |
bhūyo nirvedam āpanno bhikṣāṃ bhikṣuḥ samācaret || 10.110 ||
gālavaḥ |
dhanagobhūmitilādīnāṃ buddhipūrvaṃ parigṛhya patitavat prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt || 10.111 ||
jamadagniḥ |
labdhadravyaṃ parityajya upavāsatrayaṃ caret |
gāyatrīm ayutaṃ japtvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 10.112 ||
sa evāha |
bhūmir gāvo hiraṇyaṃ vā yater yasya parigrahaḥ |
tādṛśaṃ kaśmalaṃ dṛṣṭvā savāsā jalam āviśet || 10.113 ||
vāyuḥ |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryād upavāse vadan mṛṣā |
asadvādo na kartavyo yatinā dharmalipsunā || 10.114 ||
gālavaḥ |
anṛtam uktvā trirātram upavaset | dharmayuktam anṛtam anūcyāhorātram upoṣya prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt || 10.115 ||
jamadagniḥ |
vāṅmanaḥkāyahiṃsāyāṃ careṣu sthāvareṣu ca |
yena yenoparudhyeta tena tena viśodhayet || 10.116 ||
vāksaṃprayoge vāgdaṇḍaḥ prāṇadaṇḍas tu mānase |
karmadaṇḍaḥ śarīrasya buddhyā nirvedam ādiśet || 10.117 ||
vāgdaṇḍo hanti vai jñānaṃ manodaṇḍaḥ parāṃ gatim |
karmadaṇḍas tu lokāṃs trīn hanyād aparirakṣitaḥ || 10.118 ||
vāgdaṇḍaṃ maunam ātiṣṭhet karmadaṇḍas tv abhojanam |
manasaḥ sa tu daṇḍaḥ syāt prāṇāyāmasya dhāraṇam || 10.119 ||
samutpanneṣu doṣeṣu syād anena tu niṣkṛtiḥ |
atīva cirakāle tu dviguṇaṃ vratam ācaret || 10.120 ||
gālavaḥ |
catuṣpadān mṛgān hatvā vrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
karkaṭacitraromādīn daśarātrāṇy upavaset || 10.121 ||
kāmād eva tu hiṃsīta yadi bhikṣuḥ paśūn mṛgān |
kṛcchrātikṛcchre kurvīta cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || 10.122 ||
jamadagniḥ |
kṛkalāse kṣīragale maṇḍūke gṛhagolike |
kurarādiṣu bhūteṣu daśāhaṃ cārdhabhojanam || 10.123 ||
mārjāre mūṣake sarpe sthūlamatsyeṣu pakṣiṣu |
nakulādiṣu bhūteṣu carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
pipīlikāyāṃ sūkṣmāyāṃ prāṇāyāmās trayas trayaḥ || 10.124 ||
sūkṣmāsthimadvadhe bhikṣoḥ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
triṃśat prāṇāyāmānkṛtvā ekāhaṃ tv ardhabhug bhavet || 10.125 ||
gālavaḥ |
parṇamūlāṅkurapuṣpāṇāṃ chedanaṃ kṛtvā triṃśat prāṇāyāmān dhārayet || 10.126 ||
dattātreyaḥ |
nagnaṃ vānupanītaṃ vā vipraṃ dṛṣṭvā pramādataḥ |
mṛdbhir dvādaśabhiḥ snātvā vāsobhiḥ saha śudhyati || 10.127 ||
hārītaḥ |
nagnaṃ vānupanītaṃ vā yaḥ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇi sa pitṛbhyaḥ prayacchati || 10.128 ||
preraṇād atha vā lobhād ekadaṇḍaṃ samāśritaḥ |
bhūyo nirvedam āpannas tridaṇḍaṃ dhārayed budhaḥ || 10.129 ||
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ niyato māsasaṃkhyayā |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇaṃ gāyatrīṃ ca sadā japet || 10.130 ||
atha cet tvarate kartuṃ divasaṃ mārutāśanaḥ |
rātrau sthitvā jale vyuṣṭaḥ prājāpatyaphalaṃ labhet || 10.131 ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ tu japaṃ kṛtvotthite ravau |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo yadi na bhrūṇahā bhavet || 10.132 ||
sarveṣām eva pāpānāṃ saṅkare samupasthite |
daśasāhasram abhyasya gāyatrī śodhanaṃ param || 10.133 ||
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha |
triḥ paṭhed āyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate || 10.134 ||
prāṇāyāmān dhārayet trīn yathāvidhi samāhitaḥ |
ahorātrakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati || 10.135 ||
karmaṇā manasā vācā yad ahnā kurute dvijaḥ |
āsīnaḥ paścimāṃ saṃdhyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || 10.136 ||
sahasraparamāṃ devīṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām |
gāyatrīṃ yo japen nityaṃ na sa pāpena lipyate || 10.137 ||
savyāhṛtikāḥ sapraṇavāḥ prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa |
api bhrūṇahataṃ māsāt punanty ahar ahaḥ kṛtāḥ || 10.138 ||
duritānāṃ duriṣṭhānāṃ pāpānāṃ mahatāṃ tathā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 10.139 ||
saṃvartaḥ |
saṃnyāsāt pracyutānāṃ tu niṣkṛtiṃ śrotum arhatha |
saṃnyasya durmatiḥ kaścit pratyāpattiṃ bhajet tu yaḥ || 10.140 ||
sa kuryāt kṛcchram aśrāntaṃ ṣaṇmāsāt pratyanantaram |
yater vipattau saṃdṛṣṭaḥ prāyaścittavidhikramaḥ || 10.141 ||
sa evāha |
caturvidyopapannasya brahmaghno gurughātinaḥ |
setubandhapathe yuktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || 10.142 ||
setubandhapathe bhikṣāṃ cāturvarṇyaṃ samācaret |
khyāpayaṃś ca svakarmāṇi chatropānahavarjitaḥ || 10.143 ||
ahaṃ duṣkṛtātmā vai mahāpātakakārakaḥ |
veśmadvāreṣu tiṣṭhāmi bhikṣārthī brahmaghātakaḥ || 10.144 ||
gokuleṣu ca goṣṭheṣu grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
paryaṭaṃś caiva tīrthāni puṇyaṃ gatvā tu sāgaram || 10.145 ||
setubandhaṃ tataḥ paśyel laṅkāmārgaṃ mahodadheḥ |
daśayojanavistīrṇaṃ śatayojanam āyatam || 10.146 ||
rāghavasya samādeśān nalasaṃcayasaṃcitam |
setubandhaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā rājānaṃ pṛthivīpatim |
yajantam aśvamedhena dṛṣṭvā sadyaḥ śucir bhavet || 10.147 ||
devalaḥ |
rajasvalāṃ tu nārīṃ vā veśyāṃ vā madyapāṃ tu vā |
śavopaskarakāṣṭhaṃ vā spṛṣṭvā snātvā dvayaṃ japet || 10.148 ||
sa evāha |
yūkotpattiḥ śarīre cet prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
kṛtvopavāsam ekaṃ tu prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 10.149 ||
gargaḥ |
bhikṣavaḥ parasparaṃ na gṛhṇīyuḥ | pramādād grahaṇe mitrasya carṣaṇī dhṛta iti japeyuḥ prāṇāyāmās trayaś ca || 10.150 ||
gālavaḥ |
kaṇḍūyanarudhirakṣaraṇe ʼhorātram upavāsaḥ || 10.151 ||
jamadagniḥ |
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ putraṃ manasāpi na cintayet |
yadi sneho bhavet teṣu prāṇāyāmās tu ṣoḍaśa || 10.152 ||
satyakāma-jābālau |
