ekavaṣaṭkārasvāhāsvadhāvaṣaṭkāraṃ karoti kārayed vā sarvaṃ tan niṣphalaṃ bhavati | tasya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate | svayam eva prayuñjānaḥ prājāpatyatrayaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet || 10.153 ||
anuktānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ satyakāma āha |
vihitātikramaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmān dvādaśa dhārayet || 10.154 ||
aśaktasya deśakālādyapekṣayā gurūṇi laghūni prāyaścittāni vyavasthāpanīyān | ṛṣīṇāṃ matabhedāt tu vikalpa iti kecit || 10.155 || tathānye ʼpi dharmās tatprāyaścittaṃ ca gṛhasthadharmād eva yojanīyam || 10.156 ||
iti yatidharmasamuccaye prāyaścittavidhir nāma
daśamaṃ parva || 10.157 ||
ekādaśaṃ parva
yatisaṃskāravidhiḥ
atha yatīnāṃ saṃskāravidhir ucyate | tatra bodhāyanaḥ |
yatīnāṃ pretasaṃskāravidhiṃ vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ |
snātvā gṛhasthaḥ śuddhātmā yatisaṃskāram ācaret || 11.1 ||
śikye śarīram āropya gandhamālyair alaṃkṛtam |
ghoṣitaṃ jayaśabdena dundubhīnāṃ raveṇa ca |
prācīm udīcīṃ vā gatvā śuddhadeśaṃ samāśrayet || 11.2 ||
nadītīre ʼśvatthavṛkṣe devagṛhe goṣṭhe brahmavṛkṣasyādhastād vā vyāhṛtibhir daṇḍāyāmapramāṇaṃ devayajanaṃ khātvā saptavyāhṛtibhiḥ prokṣya puruṣasūktena snāpayitvā gandhādibhir alaṃkṛtya viṣṇo rakṣasveti nidadhāti | idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrama iti dakṣiṇahaste daṇḍaṃ nidadhāti | haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ṛcā hṛdayadeśe japet | puruṣasūktaṃ bhruvor madhye japet | brahma jajñānam iti mūrdhni deśe japet | bhūmir bhūmim agād iti mūrdhānaṃ bhedayet | gāyatryābhimarṣaṇaṃ | praṇavena devayajanaṃ pūrayet | punaḥ punar gāyatryā deśasaṃskāraḥ | sṛgālakākādibādho yadi doṣam āpādayet kartus taddeśe ʼnāvṛṣṭir bhaviṣyati | tasmāt punaḥ punaḥ pūrayet | tasmin pracchādayet | nāśaucaṃ nodakakriyā | yater vahanakhananasparśanāc ca sadyo ʼvabhṛthasnānaṃ bhavati | pade pade ʼśvamedhaphalaṃ puruṣaḥ prāpnoti |
ity āha bhagavān bodhāyanaḥ || 11.3 ||
brahmaniṣṭhe mṛte putraḥ khanec chiṣyo ʼtha vā gṛhī |
tasya tenaiva kartavyo nārāyaṇabalis tathā || 11.4 ||
na vahnir nodakaṃ śrāddhaṃ nāpi brāhmaṇatarpaṇam |
sarvaṃ nārāyaṇoddiṣṭam ekoddiṣṭavad ācaret || 11.5 ||
yatimātre mṛte ʼpy etad vidhānaṃ kavayo viduḥ |
ahany ekādaśe prāpte pārvaṇaṃ tu vidhīyate || 11.6 ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ caiṣāṃ na kurvīta kadācana |
tridaṇḍagrahaṇād eva pretatvaṃ naiva gacchati || 11.7 ||
etad eva hi paryāptaṃ bhikṣor ekāntaśīlinaḥ |
na cāsya kriyate kaścit kriyate na sa kasyacit || 11.8 ||
athāparo vidhir dṛṣṭo vyāhṛtibhis tu pūrvavat |
tābhir eva nidhāyainaṃ tābhiḥ kṛtvā tathāvaṭam || 11.9 ||
saptavyāhṛtibhiś caiva prokṣya tac cāvaṭaṃ punaḥ |
piṣṭapraskandamantreṇa devo vaḥ savitādinā || 11.10 ||
datvā śikyaṃ tu nikṣipya pārśve daṇḍaṃ tu nikṣipet |
sakhā metyādinā śikyaṃ hṛdaye ʼsya vinikṣipet || 11.11 ||
yena devāḥ pavitreṇa mukhe jalapavitrakam |
tvaṃ no agna iti nyasya kuṇḍikāṃ dakṣiṇe kare || 11.12 ||
bhūmir bhūmim agān mantram uktvā pātraṃ tathodare |
hotṛbhiḥ samupasthāya prokṣya vyāhṛtisaptakaiḥ || 11.13 ||
pūrayed avaṭaṃ mantram agnināgniḥ samidhyate |
yathā sṛgālāḥ śvāno vā noddhareyuḥ punas tathā || 11.14 ||
prāksāgnikānāṃ bhikṣūṇāṃ nidadhyād vā bile kṣitau |
niragnīnāṃ dahed vipraḥ saṃsiddhānāṃ kalevaram || 11.15 ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ jalaṃ piṇḍam āśaucaṃ pretasaṃskriyāḥ |
eteṣāṃ tu vidhānena kuryād abde sapiṇḍatām |
na kuryāt pārvaṇād anyad brahmabhūtāya bhikṣave || 11.16 ||
atriḥ |
putro gṛhasthaḥ śuddhātmā yatisaṃskāram ācaret |
śikye śarīram āropya gandhamālyair alaṃkṛtam || 11.17 ||
tuṣāgnim atra cotpādya saṃskārārthaṃ yater haret |
ghoṣitaṃ jayaśabdena dundubhīnāṃ raveṇa ca |
prācīm udīcīṃ vā gatvā śuddhadeśaṃ samācaret || 11.18 ||
khātvā vyāhṛtibhir bhūmiṃ daṇḍāyāmapramāṇakam |
saptavyāhṛtibhiḥ prokṣya tatra dārucitir bhavet || 11.19 ||
yājñīyais tu yathāśakti kāṣṭhair anyair aninditaiḥ |
tataḥ śarīraṃ prakṣālya sāvitryā śuddhamānasaḥ || 11.20 ||
viṣṇo havyaṃ rakṣasveti dārucityāṃ nidhāya tat |
pavitram ity anenaiva pavitraṃ sthāpayen mukhe || 11.21 ||
tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe pāṇāv idaṃ viṣṇur iti nyaset |
savye śikyaṃ yad asyeti svāhāntena nidhāya tat || 11.22 ||
sāvitryā codare pātraṃ guhyasthāne kamaṇḍalum |
bhūmir bhūmim agād iti sthāpayed vidhikovidaḥ || 11.23 ||
śvabhre kāṣṭhe ʼtha nikṣiptaṃ sarvasādhanasaṃyutam |
śarīraṃ hotṛbhiḥ kartā abhimantryopatiṣṭhate |
tuṣāgninā dahed dehaṃ yāvad bhasmībhaviṣyati || 11.24 ||
athāpy udāharantīmaṃ vedārthanipuṇā budhāḥ |
niṣekādiśmaśānāntā vidhayo brāhmaṇe smṛtāḥ |
tasmād yateś ca saṃskāraṃ mantravat kurute gṛhī || 11.25 ||
ātmany agnīn samāropya yaḥ praiṣam avadad yatiḥ |
tasya putro vidhānena hy avaropyāgninā dahet || 11.26 ||
api hotravidhānena gāyatryā praṇavena vā |
karmaniṣṭhe tu saṃnyaste pitary uparate sutaiḥ |
dahanaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ piṇḍodakakriyāḥ || 11.27 ||
ādāv eva vikalpena brahmacārī yatir bhavet |
tasya sarvaṃ gṛhasthena kartavyaṃ saṃnidhau hareḥ || 11.28 ||
yatīn vahan dahan sparśan snānamātreṇa śudhyati |
pade pade ʼśvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 11.29 ||
śaunakaḥ |
sarvasaṅganivṛttasya dhyānayogaratasya ca |
na tasya dahanaṃ kāryaṃ naiva piṇḍodakakriyāḥ || 11.30 ||
nidadhyāt praṇavenaiva dhyānabhikṣoḥ kalevaram |
prokṣaṇaṃ khananaṃ caiva sarvaṃ tenaiva kārayet || 11.31 ||
yatīn vahan spṛśan vāpi snānamātreṇa śudhyati |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ sarve pṛthak pṛthag avāpnuyuḥ iti || 11.32 ||
etam evāha bhagavān vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ || 11.33 ||
iti yādavaprakāśīye yatidharmasamuccaye yatisaṃskāravidhir nāma
ekādaśaṃ parva || 11.34 ||
